Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下思路可间接参考：1）"信息不对称"概念可类比遥感多模态（如光学-SAR）融合中的对齐问题；2）方面引导的聚类-正则化思想或可用于遥感多标签场景下的特征解耦；3）但整体方向偏离课题核心，不建议深入。

Abstract: Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining learns a shared representation space through large-scale contrastive learning. However, existing methods that enforce global consistency regularization overlook a key challenge: the inherent information asymmetry between images and text: captions typically describe only one specific aspect of an image, thus images with similar visual content can be paired with completely divergent textual content and semantic information. Consequently, global regularizers inadvertently impose constraints between visually similar images whose captions describe divergent aspects, introducing semantic distortion into the representation space. We propose AspectCLIP, a framework that reformulates consistency regularization to respect this one-to-many structure. AspectCLIP first partitions training samples into attribute clusters based on textual similarity to identify aspect-coherent groups, then applies full cyclic consistency within each cluster while restricting cross-cluster regularization to prototype-level comparisons. This aspect-guided regularization enforces strict geometric alignment only when images and texts describe a consistent facet, while allowing flexibility across divergent aspects. Extensive experiments on downstream tasks demonstrate that AspectCLIP consistently outperforms traditional methods and achieves a more structured representation space.

</details>


### [68] [Bake It Till You Make It: Ultrafast Spatial Texture-Atlas Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13808)
*Neel Kelkar,Simon Niedermayr,Kaloian Petkov,Klaus Engel,Rüdiger Westermann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种解耦辐射场表示方法，通过2D surfel建模低频几何与视角相关外观，用哈希网格烘焙到纹理图集处理高频纹理，结合稀疏性优化剪枝，实现比3DGS快5倍的实时4K渲染。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于3D场景渲染（3D Gaussian Splatting的加速与稀疏化），属于计算机图形学/神经渲染领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及星上/边缘部署场景。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，属于计算机图形学/3D渲染领域，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：3D场景辐射场重建与实时渲染；应用场景：计算机图形学、新视角合成；传感器类型：摘要未说明（通常为多视角RGB图像）；目标类别：无目标检测相关。

Model architecture: 基于3D Gaussian Splatting的改进架构：使用2D surfel建模低频几何与视角相关外观特征，结合view-independent spatial hash grid处理高频纹理，并烘焙为compact texture atlas。

Lightweight method: 涉及剪枝（pruning）：通过sparsity-enhancing optimizations（惩罚半透明度和per-primitive falloff）来剪枝insignificant surfels，实现更稀疏的重建。但这是针对渲染图元的剪枝，与模型剪枝（结构化/非结构化剪枝）概念不同。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。仅提及在消费级硬件上实现实时4K 60FPS渲染，但未给出参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。渲染加速（5倍于3DGS）和实时性（4K@60FPS）是针对图形渲染管线，非目标检测推理。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明对比方法和消融实验细节，仅提及相比prior work（3DGS）实现5倍加速并保持SOTA视觉保真度。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴。方法针对的是3D场景渲染的加速与稀疏化，而非目标检测模型的轻量化。摘要未说明在遥感图像上的任何实验。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，可借鉴点有限：(1) 稀疏性优化（sparsity-enhancing optimization）的思想——在模型压缩中引入稀疏正则化来剪枝冗余参数，这一思路可迁移到检测模型的剪枝中；(2) 将高频信息烘焙到紧凑表示（texture atlas）的思路，类似于将复杂特征蒸馏到轻量网络中。但整体方向差异较大，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Recent extensions of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) capture fine color details using hash-grid-based appearance parameterization but incur high computational cost during fragment rendering. We introduce a decoupled radiance representation that models low-frequency geometry and view dependent appearance features with 2D surfels while representing high-frequency textures via a view-independent spatial hash grid that is baked into a compact texture atlas. By including sparsity-enhancing optimizations that penalize semi-transparency and per-primitive falloff, our method aggressively prunes insignificant surfels and achieves significantly faster and sparser reconstructions than prior work. Exploiting geometric sparsity and efficient GPU texture mapping, our approach achieves up to a fivefold speedup over 3DGS while preserving state-of-the-art visual fidelity, enabling real-time 4K rendering at 60 FPS on consumer hardware.

</details>


### [69] [Multimodal Assessment of Pancreatic Cancer Resectability Using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13826)
*Vincent Ochs,Christoph Kuemmerli,Florentin Bieder,Julia Wolleb,Joel L. Lavanchy,Julia Ruppel,Jan Liechti,Stephanie Taha-Mehlitz,Christian Andreas Nebiker,Beat Mueller,Giuseppe Kito Fusai,Joerg-Matthias Pollok,Anas Taha,Philippe C. Cattin,Sebastian Staubli*

TL;DR: 提出一个基于Swin-UNETR的多模态深度学习框架，利用3D增强CT和临床信息自动分类胰腺导管腺癌的可切除性（NCCN三类），通过辅助分割胰腺/肿瘤/血管结构获得解剖感知特征，并用轻量分类头融合临床嵌入，动态多任务训练平衡分割与分类目标。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文是医学影像分析（胰腺癌CT可切除性分类），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感场景；也不涉及目标检测任务（而是医学分类+分割）；虽然提到了"轻量分类头"，但轻量化不是核心贡献，且与遥感在轨部署场景无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：医学图像分类（NCCN可切除性三分类）+ 辅助分割（胰腺、肿瘤、血管）；应用场景：胰腺导管腺癌（PDAC）术前CT评估；传感器类型：3D对比增强CT（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：upfront resectable, borderline resectable, locally advanced三类。

Model architecture: Swin-UNETR backbone（3D Swin Transformer + UNETR结构），辅助分割头输出胰腺/肿瘤/血管分割图，临床信息编码为compact clinical embedding，轻量分类头融合图像特征与临床嵌入进行三分类。

Lightweight method: 仅提及"lightweight classification head"（轻量分类头），未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等主流轻量化方法，轻量化不是本文核心贡献。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然使用了轻量分类头，但Swin-UNETR backbone本身计算量较大，且面向的是临床CT工作站而非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称、样本量、评价指标（如准确率、AUC、Kappa等）。仅提到使用3D对比增强CT和17个临床变量。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果、对比方法、消融实验结果。仅提到动态多任务目标根据当前肿瘤Dice性能调整分割与分类的平衡。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供遥感场景下的借鉴。医学CT与遥感图像在传感器特性、目标尺度、背景复杂度、部署约束等方面差异巨大。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但以下两点可能有间接启发：(1) 多任务学习（分割+分类联合训练）的思路可迁移到遥感目标检测中，例如同时检测目标+分割道路/建筑等辅助任务提升特征表示；(2) 动态任务权重调整策略（根据当前性能自适应平衡多任务损失）可用于遥感检测+轻量化联合训练场景。但整体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Accurate determination of pancreatic ductal adenocarcinoma (PDAC) resectability relies on evaluating how the tumor interacts with major peripancreatic vessels on CT imaging, yet expert assessment often shows substantial variability. We introduce a fully automated multimodal deep learning framework that jointly analyzes 3D contrast enhanced CT and structured clinical information to classify patients into the three National Comprehensive Cancer Network (NCCN) resectability categories (upfront resectable, borderline resectable, locally advanced). The approach uses a Swin-UNETR backbone to obtain anatomy aware image representations through auxiliary segmentation of pancreas, tumor, and vascular structures. These features are fused with a compact clinical embedding derived from 17 routinely collected variables and processed by a lightweight classification head. Model training is guided by a dynamic multitask objective that adapts the balance between segmentation and classification based on current tumor Dice performance, promoting feature representations that remain both anatomically informed and discriminative.

</details>


### [70] [Towards Enhancing 3D Spatial Reasoning in Medical Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13860)
*Zhuoyuan Fu,Zeshang Li,Yiqiong Zhang,Hangui Lin,Yan Shu,Yan Li,Binyang Li,Yaru Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于切片级数据合成范式的结构化推理数据集构建方法，用于提升2D预训练多模态大模型在3D医学影像理解中的性能，无需3D专用预训练。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于3D医学影像（CT等）的多模态大模型推理能力增强，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：3D医学影像理解（volumetric medical image understanding），具体为CT影像的视觉问答与临床推理。应用场景：放射科诊断辅助。传感器类型：医学CT扫描仪。目标类别：医学病灶/解剖结构。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 基于2D预训练的MLLM（Multimodal Large Language Model）基线，通过指令微调（instruction-tuning）增强3D体素理解能力。未使用原生3D架构。与遥感目标检测模型无关。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计、实时推理或边缘部署。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文强调"无需计算昂贵的3D专用预训练"，但未给出具体计算开销数据。与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自建大规模结构化推理数据集（基于切片级数据合成范式），在多个3D医学基准上评估。评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标名称。与遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、NWPU VHR-10、SSDD、SARDet等）无关。

Experiments: 在多个3D医学基准上，指令微调后的2D MLLM相比2D基线有显著性能提升，具备鲁棒的空间推理能力，可媲美资源密集型的原生3D架构。消融实验和对比方法细节摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无可借鉴性。2. 仅针对医学CT影像，数据模态（Hounsfield单位）与遥感影像（光学/SAR/高光谱）差异巨大。3. 未涉及任何模型压缩或加速技术。4. 未提供计算效率指标。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。论文的数据合成范式（slice-wise synthesis + Chain-of-Thought）虽在医学领域有效，但遥感在轨场景对实时性、计算约束、轻量化有严格要求，与本文的"数据驱动提升理解能力"路线差异过大。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable success in 2D medical image understanding, their extension to 3D volumetric imaging remains hindered by prohibitive annotation costs and dataset opacity. Current data formats, predominantly consisting of rigid Visual Question Answering (VQA) pairs or unstructured final clinical reports, typically fail to capture explicit clinical reasoning. To address this limitation, we introduce a large-scale structured reasoning dataset constructed via a novel slice-wise data synthesis paradigm. Inspired by the genuine diagnostic workflow of radiologists, this paradigm models visual cognition by decomposing the complex 3D reading process, translating global clinical priors into fine-grained, per-slice observations that are subsequently synthesized into an interpretable Chain-of-Thought (CoT). Crucially, this synthesized reasoning framework enforces essential clinical principles: sequential spatial tracking, multi-slice spatial awareness for artifact mitigation, and differential exclusion. To validate this approach, we instruction-tune a standard 2D-pretrained MLLM baseline using the synthesized data to enhance its volumetric comprehension. Comprehensive evaluations across multiple 3D medical benchmarks demonstrate that our method yields significant performance improvements over the 2D baseline. Furthermore, the resulting model exhibits robust spatial reasoning capabilities and rivals resource-intensive native 3D architectures, effectively bridging the performance gap. Ultimately, this data-centric strategy unlocks deep volumetric understanding and highly interpretable clinical logic without requiring computationally expensive 3D-specific pre-training. The complete repository, including datasets and training workflows, is publicly available at https://github.com/2020420145009/hounsfield.

</details>


### [71] [Unleashing Multimodal Large Language Models for Training-free HOI Detection in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13881)
*Ting Lei,Jialin Liu,Zhu Xu,Yuxin Peng,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的AgentHOI框架，利用多模态大模型(MLLM)的推理能力实现开放世界人-物交互检测(HOI detection)，不依赖任何HOI检测训练数据。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是人-物交互检测(HOI detection)，属于视觉-语言多模态理解和开放世界检测范畴，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测的轻量化、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、边缘部署等方向。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文研究人-物交互检测(HOI detection)，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：人-物交互检测(Human-Object Interaction Detection, HOID)；应用场景：开放世界/野外场景(in the wild)；传感器类型：普通RGB图像；目标类别：人(human)与物体(object)之间的交互关系。与遥感目标检测(舰船/飞机/车辆/小目标/旋转框)完全无关。

Model architecture: AgentHOI框架，采用agentic范式，模块化编排多个视觉基础模型(vision foundation modules)，结合多模态大模型(MLLM)进行开放式语义推理和空间定位。包含两个关键机制：(1) Context-aware Multi-round Reasoning（上下文感知多轮推理）；(2) Multifaceted Interaction Localization（多层面交互定位）。整体是training-free的agent框架。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。该论文是training-free框架，依赖多个大型基础模型(MLLM + vision foundation models)，计算开销可能很大，与轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署等方向完全无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。由于依赖多个大型基础模型(如MLLM)，推理开销很可能很大，不适合星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。仅提到在real-world settings下与state-of-the-art supervised和weakly supervised方法对比，且无需HOID训练数据。

Experiments: 摘要未提供详细的实验对比结果和消融实验数据。仅声称在真实场景下超越现有监督和弱监督方法。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测方向完全无关；(2) 依赖多个大型基础模型，计算开销大，不满足轻量化需求；(3) 摘要未提供定量结果（mAP、速度、参数量等）；(4) 开放世界推理的稳定性和可靠性在遥感场景下未验证。

Ideas for my research: 对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的可借鉴点极少。唯一可能值得思考的是：该论文的"training-free"思路——如果未来遥感在轨场景需要适应新类别，是否可以用轻量级的基础模型做类似agentic推理？但当前论文依赖的MLLM和vision foundation models计算量过大，与轻量化目标背道而驰，不建议直接借鉴。

Abstract: Human-object interaction detection (HOID) has traditionally been formulated as a supervised detection problem over predefined interaction categories. While such paradigms achieve strong performance on closed-set benchmarks, they fundamentally entangle interaction understanding with dataset-specific supervision, limiting their ability to generalize to open-world and compositional scenarios. Recent HOI detectors attempt to leverage MLLMs through prompting strategies to transfer interaction-specific knowledge. However, such prompt-based approaches primarily focus on extracting discriminative representations from pretrained models, while underexploring their inherent multimodal reasoning capabilities. As a result, they struggle to provide informative contextual reasoning for ambiguous and open-world interaction scenarios. In this work, we present AgentHOI, a training-free, agentic framework that transfers the generalist multimodal reasoning capabilities of foundation models to HOI detection in the wild. Instead of learning interaction classifiers, AgentHOI modularly orchestrates complementary vision foundation modules to perform open-ended semantic reasoning and spatial grounding in a coordinated manner. To address the challenges of incomplete interaction discovery and ambiguous localization in complex scenes, we introduce two key mechanisms: (1) Context-aware Multi-round Reasoning, which progressively refines interaction hypotheses to ensure exhaustive and compositional HOI discovery, and (2) Multifaceted Interaction Localization, which enhances grounding precision by generating instance-specific descriptions that integrate semantic, spatial, and appearance cues. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AgentHOI achieves superior performance over state-of-the-art supervised and weakly supervised methods in real-world settings, despite requiring no HOID data for training.

</details>


### [72] [Fine-Grained Vision-Language Pretraining with Organ-Conditioned Pattern Tokens for CT Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13892)
*Guoliang You,Xiaomeng Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于器官条件化模式-令牌对齐的CT视觉-语言预训练框架OCP-CT，用于从CT扫描和放射学报告中进行零样本异常诊断。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于医学影像（CT）的视觉-语言预训练，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心要素；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。属于医学影像分析领域的多模态预训练工作。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：CT视觉-语言预训练 + 零样本异常诊断；应用场景：医学影像分析；传感器类型：CT（计算机断层扫描）；目标类别：放射学异常/病变。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: OCP-CT框架：包含全局CT-报告对比分支 + 器官模式接口（稀疏MoE路由 + 可学习slot查询 + 成对令牌对比）。使用稀疏Mixture-of-Experts进行图像和文本令牌的路由。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向医学影像分析，无在轨/边缘部署考虑。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CT-RATE、RAD-ChestCT（公开医学CT数据集）；评价指标：AUROC（零样本异常诊断）。与遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、NWPU VHR-10等）完全无关。

Experiments: 在CT-RATE上零样本AUROC达84.5%，在RAD-ChestCT上达69.9%，相比先前最强结果分别提升6.7和0.8个百分点。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 未提供任何模型效率指标（参数量、FLOPs、推理速度等）；3. 仅针对医学CT模态，方法迁移到遥感场景的可行性存疑。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能的技术启发是：稀疏MoE路由机制可用于遥感多模态（如SAR+光学）特征融合，但该论文面向的是文本-图像对齐而非目标检测，且未涉及轻量化设计，与本课题核心方向不匹配。

Abstract: Computed tomography (CT) vision-language pretraining from paired volumes and radiology reports is a scalable yet challenging task. Existing methods commonly adopt global scan-report contrast, which is scalable but obscures heterogeneous organ evidence. Meanwhile, direct organ-level alignment remains coarse, since the same anatomy can exhibit multiple distinct radiological appearances. Therefore, pretraining requires a finer alignment unit: the organ-conditioned radiological pattern. In this work, we propose OCP-CT, an organ-conditioned pattern-token alignment framework for CT vision-language pretraining. Specifically, OCP-CT preserves a stable global CT-report contrastive branch and introduces an organ pattern interface: sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routes image and text tokens according to latent radiological patterns, learnable slots query the routed tokens into continuous pattern tokens, and paired token contrast aligns image-text pattern tokens with structured soft targets built from report-derived clinical similarity. On the publicly available CT-RATE and RAD-ChestCT benchmarks, OCP-CT achieves average AUROCs of 84.5% and 69.9% for zero-shot abnormality diagnosis, respectively. Compared with the strongest prior reported results, these results yield absolute AUROC gains of 6.7 and 0.8 percentage points.

</details>


### [73] [The 2nd International StepUP Competition for Biometric Footstep Recognition: From Steps to Strides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13905)
*Robyn Larracy,Anant Gupta,Gourav Gupta,Ethan Eddy,Maxime Devanne,Cyril Meyer,Jin-Chern Chiou,Yueh-Shan Lee,Zong-Han Lu,Aaron Tabor,Erik Scheme*

TL;DR: 该论文是关于基于压力的脚步生物特征识别竞赛（StepUP Competition），与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。论文主题是压力式脚步生物特征识别（footstep biometrics），使用地面压力传感器数据，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱图像、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关领域。也不涉及目标检测、轻量化模型、边缘部署等方向。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文完全不属于遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化任何相关领域，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：压力式脚步生物特征识别（footstep biometrics）；场景：地面步态识别；传感器：压力传感器（pressure-based）；目标类别：行人个体身份。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 摘要提及：spatiotemporal CNN + ensemble-based scoring strategy。但这是用于步态识别的架构，非遥感目标检测模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。且该任务本身不涉及星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：StepUP-P150（200,000+高分辨率动态脚步数据，150人）；评价指标：Equal Error Rate (EER)，最佳EER为8.00%。与遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、NWPU VHR-10等）无关。

Experiments: 竞赛设置三个挑战：1) 未见用户泛化（少量注册数据）；2) 鞋类和行走速度导致的域偏移鲁棒性；3) 左右脚配对融合。最佳方案来自ArogyaPandit Research Team。与遥感目标检测实验无关。

Limitations: 摘要指出：在未见过的个人鞋类上识别用户仍具挑战性，尤其存在相似特征的干扰项时。该局限性针对步态识别，与遥感无关。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接可借鉴点。弱相关角度：竞赛中使用的inference-time normalization和calibration策略在域迁移场景下有一定通用性，但与本课题关联度极低。

Abstract: The International StepUP Competition Series was launched to advance research in pressure-based footstep biometrics through a standardized and challenging evaluation framework. Using the large-scale StepUP-P150 dataset (with more than 200,000 high-resolution dynamic footsteps from 150 individuals) and a previously unreleased test set, the 2nd edition of the competition addressed three key challenges: (1) generalization to unseen users with limited enrollment data, (2) robustness to domain shift caused by variations in footwear and walking speed and (3) effective fusion of paired left-right footsteps. While the first two challenges built on the inaugural competition, this edition introduced more extreme cross-domain conditions and moved beyond isolated footsteps to stride-level verification, enabling new opportunities for representation learning and inter-step information fusion. The competition attracted 26 registrants from academia and industry, with a best equal error rate of 8.00% achieved by the ArogyaPandit Research Team using a spatiotemporal CNN combined with an ensemble-based scoring strategy. The top solutions showcase the value of harnessing temporal patterns and of incorporating inference-time normalization and calibration strategies to improve scoring. However, the results also reveal that recognizing users in unseen personal footwear remains a challenge, especially in the presence of distractors with similar characteristics.

</details>


### [74] [Thresholded Cross-Attention for Reliable Intensity-Chromaticity Fusion in Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13925)
*Yanyi Wu,Xu Zhang,Junkai Chen,Laibin Chang,Jiaqi Ma,Shi Chen,Linwei Zhu,Jianglei Di,Huan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TCA-Net，通过阈值化交叉注意力机制改进HVI空间中的亮度-色度融合，用于低光照图像增强。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦低光照图像增强（LLIE），不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。虽然提到了"compact parameter size"，但这是LLIE领域的效率考量，与遥感在轨目标检测的轻量化无直接关系。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非对低光照增强作为遥感预处理有特殊兴趣，否则可忽略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：低光照图像增强（LLIE）；场景：通用摄影图像增强；传感器类型：RGB相机；目标类别：无目标检测相关任务。不涉及遥感、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等。

Model architecture: TCA-Net，核心是Thresholded Cross-Attention (TCA)，替代固定Top-K稀疏注意力，采用固定置信度阈值实现输入和层自适应的注意力选择。辅助模块：Phase-guided Fourier Interaction Module（相位引导傅里叶交互模块）、Decoupled Dual-Stream Guidance Module（解耦双流引导模块）、Scale-Aware Consistency Regularization（尺度感知一致性正则化）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未明确提及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。仅提到"compact parameter size"表明模型参数量较小，但未说明具体压缩技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及"compact parameter size"和"efficiency"，但无具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：LOL-v1, LOL-v2, Sony-Total-Dark, LSRW-Huawei。评价指标：摘要未明确列出具体指标名称（如PSNR、SSIM等），仅提到"restoration accuracy, improved color fidelity"。

Experiments: 在LOL-v1、LOL-v2、Sony-Total-Dark、LSRW-Huawei四个数据集上实验，TCA-Net在恢复精度、色彩保真度和紧凑参数量方面具有竞争力。摘要未提供与SOTA方法的定量对比表格或消融实验结果。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，不涉及遥感图像特性（如多光谱、SAR、大幅面、地物复杂背景等）；2. 未提供任何轻量化部署指标（参数量、FLOPs、推理速度等）；3. 未在遥感数据集上验证；4. 低光照增强可作为遥感图像预处理的潜在前处理步骤，但论文未朝此方向讨论。

Ideas for my research: 1. 阈值化稀疏注意力机制（Thresholded Cross-Attention）的设计思路可借鉴到遥感目标检测的轻量注意力设计中，用自适应阈值替代固定Top-K选择，在保持精度的同时减少冗余计算；2. 解耦双流设计（亮度-色度解耦）的思路可类比遥感中的光谱-空间特征解耦，但需重新设计以适应多光谱/高光谱数据；3. 尺度感知一致性正则化（Scale-Aware Consistency Regularization）对遥感多尺度目标检测有潜在借鉴价值。总体而言，借鉴价值有限且间接。

Abstract: Low-Light Image Enhancement (LLIE) requires a careful balance among noise suppression, color fidelity, and efficiency. Recent HVI-based methods alleviate color entanglement by decoupling intensity and chromaticity, yet how reliably the two streams are fused again is an overlooked factor that largely determines the final quality. We observe that the confidence of cross-stream attention is strongly layer-dependent, so the fixed-quota selection of Top-K sparse attention is mismatched to it, discarding informative dependencies in some layers while retaining noisy ones in others. Motivated by this observation, we propose TCA-Net, a network built around Thresholded Cross-Attention that targets reliable intensity-chromaticity fusion in the HVI space rather than introducing yet another color representation. At its core, TCA replaces the rigid Top-K quota with a fixed confidence threshold whose retained cardinality is input- and layer-adaptive, retaining only high-confidence cross-stream interactions while suppressing unreliable ones. Around this core, two complementary designs clean up the fusion before and after it: a Phase-guided Fourier Interaction Module provides a structure-aware brightness initialization for the intensity stream prior to fusion, and a Decoupled Dual-Stream Guidance Module constructs residual intensity features to suppress chromaticity leakage during reconstruction. A Scale-Aware Consistency Regularization further improves structural robustness under scale perturbations during training. Extensive experiments on LOL-v1, LOL-v2, Sony-Total-Dark, and LSRW-Huawei demonstrate that TCA-Net delivers competitive restoration accuracy, improved color fidelity, and a compact parameter size.

</details>


### [75] [Cyclone: Diffusion Model for Cycle-Consistent Weather Editing from Unpaired Driving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13927)
*Thang-Anh-Quan Nguyen,Moussab Bennehar,Luis Guillermo Roldao Jimenez,Nathan Piasco,Dzmitry Tsishkou,Laurent Caraffa,Jean-Philippe Tarel,Roland Brémond*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于潜扩散的天气编辑框架Cyclone，用于自动驾驶场景下生成多样化天气条件以提升感知鲁棒性。

Abstract: Reliable perception under diverse weather conditions remains a major challenge for autonomous driving systems. A common strategy to improve robustness is either to synthesize adverse weather conditions for training perception models or to apply weather-removal techniques to recover clean inputs. However, existing approaches typically rely on synthetic data augmentation or physics-based, task-specific models that require paired training data and often struggle to generate realistic weather effects or generalize robustly to out-of-domain scenarios. Toward this problem, we present Cyclone, a unified framework for weather editing based on latent diffusion, equipped with cycle-consistent constraints and knowledge from image-text models. Cyclone enables the generation of multiple weather conditions across diverse scenes while eliminating the need for paired data. Experimental results show that our approach produces more realistic, structure-preserving outputs than existing baselines and leads to consistent improvements across several downstream driving perception tasks. Furthermore, we demonstrate that Cyclone can be distilled to a video diffusion model for temporally consistent weather editing.

</details>


### [76] [SIVA-RL: Sensitivity-Invariance Visual Alignment for Multimodal Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13931)
*Cheng Tang,Junzhi Ning,Min Cen,Wei Li,Xinyi Zeng,Pinxian Zeng,Rongbin Li,Qiming Zhu,Yuqiang Li,Junjun He,Yirong Chen,Ming Hu*

Model architecture: 基于视觉-语言模型(VLM)，使用GRPO或DAPO作为强化学习骨干。核心组件：Sensitivity-Invariance Visual Alignment框架，包含token对齐的距离约束PatchSwap干预构造、冻结审计策略模型(frozen audit policy)、软路由权重分配机制。

Experiments: 在3B和7B模型上，SIVA-RL在所有设置下均优于匹配的RL基线。视觉依赖推理任务上提升8.79个百分点，GRPO和DAPO四种配置下整体相对提升最高达14.9%。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 模型规模(3B/7B)过大，不适合星上部署；3. 仅针对自然图像多模态推理，未涉及遥感场景理解或目标检测任务。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) drives multimodal reasoning, but answer-level correctness does not guarantee that a vision-language model grounds its predictions in visual evidence. Existing visual-intervention methods contrast policy behavior on original and modified images, yet assign supervision by the type of intervention rather than its observed effect. This assumption fails: identical operators produce heterogeneous outcomes across samples. We propose SIVA-RL, a Sensitivity-Invariance Visual Alignment framework that replaces operator-conditioned regularization with sample-wise, outcome-conditioned supervision. SIVA-RL constructs localized interventions through token-aligned, distance-constrained within-image PatchSwap. A frozen audit policy then scores each clean-intervention pair, and the observed reward drop becomes soft routing weights. Large-drop pairs drive sensitivity alignment, low-drop pairs drive clean-anchored invariance alignment, and ambiguous pairs are down-weighted. This design decouples intervention construction from supervision assignment and is compatible with both GRPO and DAPO backbones. Across nine multimodal reasoning benchmarks spanning mathematical, logical, and vision-dependent tasks, SIVA-RL improves 3B and 7B models over matched RL baselines in every setting. It yields an 8.79 percentage-point gain on vision-dependent reasoning and up to 14.9% relative overall improvement across all four GRPO- and DAPO-based configurations.

</details>


### [77] [A novel unsupervised machine learning strategy to handle multimodal cardiac PET/MRI data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13936)

Model architecture: 两步聚类方法：第一步对每个患者的T1/T2 maps、LGE、18F-FDG-PET图像进行z-score归一化并求和，然后进行超体素聚类；第二步对跨患者的超体素进行谱聚类，得到32个跨患者超体素组。非深度学习模型，无神经网络结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。该方法面向临床诊断，非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：99例基因确诊的致心律失常性左室心肌病患者PET/MRI数据，以及167个数值体模（phantom）；评价指标：平衡准确率（Balanced Accuracy, BA）；实验设置：重复嵌套交叉验证。

Experiments: 患者数据上BA=0.76±0.04（重复嵌套交叉验证），体模数据上BA≥0.8；异常聚类结果与心脏影像医师的视觉观察高度吻合。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 方法基于传统聚类而非深度学习，缺乏通用性和可迁移性；3. 仅针对特定心肌病，无遥感场景适用性。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于医学影像分析领域，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向无交叉。

Abstract: Arrhythmogenic left ventricular cardiomyopathy is a genetic myocardial disease difficult to diagnose due to the lack of gold standard criteria. Simultaneous PET/MR imaging, combined with multiparametric quantitative analysis, could facilitate the identification of different profiles related to the phenotype and progression of cardiomyopathy. This preliminary study focuses on a methodological strategy for dealing with PET/MRI data, including inter-patient data linkage and regional analysis. Two-step clustering was applied to T1 and T2 maps, LGE, and 18F-FDG-PET images of 99 patients genetically diagnosed with arrhythmogenic left ventricular cardiomyopathy. Each patient's images were independently z-scored and summed into a single volume, which was clustered into supervoxels. Thirty-two inter-patient groups of supervoxels were obtained by spectral clustering. An "abnormality" score was assigned to each cluster and modality, and used to visualise abnormal regions likely associated with disease. They enabled the generation of automated textual and bullseye health reports for each patient, which were compared with cardiac imager assessments using balanced accuracy in repeated nested cross-validation. This approach was further validated on a larger cohort of 167 numerical phantoms. The reports generated by clustering accurately identified most of the cardiac physicians' observations (BA = 0.76 $\pm$ 0.04 in repeated nested cross-validation on patients, and BA $\ge$ 0.8 on phantoms). Furthermore, the identified abnormal clusters closely matched their visual observations, facilitating the identification of varying degrees of fibrosis or inflammation on the images. This approach enables a more systematic handling of multimodal PET/MRI data to characterise myocardial heterogeneity in arrhythmogenic left ventricular cardiomyopathy patients.

</details>


### [78] [Peak-End-Net: A Peak-End Rule Inspired Framework for Generalizable Video Aesthetic Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13941)
*Geng Li,Haiwen Li,Rui Chen,Jing Tang,Lei Sun,Xiangxiang Chu*

TL;DR: 提出一种基于"峰终定律"心理学的轻量级视频美学评估框架Peak-End-Net，利用冻结ViT和少量可训练参数实现SOTA性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文属于视频美学评估(VAA)，既非遥感/卫星场景，也非目标检测任务，轻量化方法虽有一定通用性但并非针对遥感在轨部署设计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：视频美学评估(VAA)；场景：通用视频审美；传感器类型：通用RGB视频；目标类别：无目标检测相关。

Model architecture: 基于冻结Vision Transformer (ViT)作为骨干网络，引入预训练图像美学评估(IAA)头生成帧级美学先验，设计美学节奏编码器(Aesthetic Rhythm Encoder)建模时序演变，动态门控融合机制(Dynamic Gated Fusion)提升鲁棒性。

Lightweight method: 使用冻结ViT（不训练骨干网络），仅少量可训练参数（parameter-efficient），属于轻量级框架设计。但未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等典型轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到"轻量级"(lightweight)和"参数高效"(parameter-efficient)，但缺乏量化指标。

Datasets and metrics: VADB（域内评估）、DIVIDE-3K（跨域测试）；评价指标为美学评估相关指标，摘要未具体说明指标名称。

Experiments: 在VADB和DIVIDE-3K上达到SOTA性能，验证了心理学建模对VAA的有效性。代码和模型已开源。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 缺乏参数量、FLOPs、推理速度等轻量化关键指标；3. 未涉及任何遥感场景或目标检测任务；4. 轻量化仅体现在"冻结骨干+少量可训练参数"，未使用剪枝/量化/蒸馏等压缩技术。

Ideas for my research: 1. "冻结骨干+少量可训练参数"的设计思路可借鉴，在遥感在轨场景中可考虑冻结预训练遥感基础模型，仅微调检测头；2. 门控融合机制可迁移到多模态遥感特征融合中；3. 但整体与遥感目标检测轻量化课题相关性极低，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Video aesthetic assessment (VAA) aims to predict how aesthetically pleasing a video is, yet remains far less explored than other visual assessment tasks. Its progress is hindered not only by the scarcity of large-scale benchmarks, but also by the intrinsic subjectivity of aesthetic judgment, which is shaped by human perception. In this paper, we revisit VAA from a psychological perspective and propose \textit{Peak-End-Net}, a lightweight and interpretable framework inspired by the \textit{peak-end rule}, which suggests that people tend to judge a temporal experience mainly according to its salient moments and the ending. Building on this intuition, we first transfer knowledge from image aesthetic assessment (IAA) to VAA by introducing a pretrained IAA head to produce frame-wise aesthetic priors, which serve as surrogate signals for identifying aesthetically salient moments and guiding \textit{peak-end rule}-based temporal aggregation. To further capture how a video evolves aesthetically over time, we design an aesthetic rhythm encoder that models temporal progression beyond isolated moments. Additionally, we refine the overall assessment through a dynamic gated fusion mechanism to improve robustness under distribution shift. Our method is built on a frozen vision transformer (ViT) and requires only a small number of trainable parameters, making it scalable and parameter-efficient. Extensive experiments on two existing VAA benchmarks, including in-domain evaluation on VADB and cross-domain testing on DIVIDE-3K, demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance, affirming the value of psychologically grounded modeling for VAA. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/AMAP-ML/Peak-End-Net.

</details>


### [79] [PlumeQuant: Uncertainty-aware consistency assessment of methane plume masks and emission-rate estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13945)
*Parisa Masnadi Khiabani,Wolfgang Jentner,Alireza Rangrazjeddi,Michael C. Wimberly,Binbin Weng,David Ebert,Charles Nicholson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PlumeQuant工具，用遗传算法集成和透明掩膜生成方法分析EMIT甲烷羽流产品中掩膜与标量参数之间的非唯一性（equifinality）问题，并给出产品一致性诊断指标。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。虽然涉及遥感（EMIT成像光谱仪、Carbon Mapper甲烷羽流产品），但核心问题是地球物理参数反演的产品一致性诊断（plume mask与IME/排放率之间的equifinality），不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等任务，也不涉及任何模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、实时推理或星上部署技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。仅当对甲烷羽流遥感或产品不确定性量化有交叉兴趣时可作背景参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：甲烷羽流产品一致性诊断与掩膜不确定性量化；应用场景：温室气体遥感监测（EMIT/Carbon Mapper）；传感器类型：成像光谱仪（imaging spectrometer）；目标类别：甲烷羽流（methane plume），非传统目标检测中的舰船/飞机/车辆等。

Model architecture: PlumeQuant工具包含：遗传算法（GA）集成用于生成目标一致掩膜集合；透明Carbon Mapper模拟掩膜（CM-like）生成器；四种掩膜表示（参考掩膜、CM-like、GA集成、专家编辑）。摘要未说明具体网络结构或深度学习模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计或任何模型压缩/加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性信息。该工具为产品后处理诊断工具，非星上推理模型。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：63个EMIT-derived Carbon Mapper plume records（来自27个场景），另有一个场景不重叠的44个羽流开发集（development split）。评价指标：IME中位差异百分比、排放率中位差异百分比、平均绝对百分比、掩膜IoU（中位0.843）、不确定性尺度比值（中位1.01）、holdout MAE（IME 7.6%、长度9.5%、排放率6.1%）。

Experiments: CM-like掩膜与参考掩膜对比：IME中位差异+0.72%，排放率中位差异+0.16%（平均绝对6.98%），IoU中位0.843，不确定性尺度比值中位1.01。Holdout集MAE：IME 7.6%、长度9.5%、排放率6.1%。GA集成显示高置信核心仅覆盖可行足迹包络的中位13%，弱羽流歧义最大。

Limitations: 1. 与在轨目标检测轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 摘要明确说明结果为产品级一致性诊断，非独立验证；3. 仅针对甲烷羽流这一特定地球物理参数，不涉及通用目标检测；4. 未讨论任何模型效率或部署约束。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。可借鉴点有限：1. 遗传算法（GA）用于不确定性量化的思路，可类比于目标检测模型输出不确定性估计；2. 产品一致性诊断框架（PlumeQuant）的设计思想，可迁移到遥感目标检测结果的后处理质量评估；3. 掩膜IoU作为一致性指标的使用。但这些与轻量化、在轨部署无直接关系。

Abstract: Imaging spectrometers increasingly distribute source-resolved methane plume products in which the plume mask, integrated mass enhancement (IME), plume length, emission rate, and uncertainty are physically and algorithmically linked. Using 63 EMIT-derived Carbon Mapper plume records from 27 scenes, we show that these published scalar quantities do not uniquely constrain the plume boundary: substantially different yet plausible masks reproduce the same IME, plume length, and emission rate. Genetic-algorithm (GA) ensembles conditioned on the published IME and plume length make this equifinality explicit: the high-confidence core selected by nearly all target-consistent masks covers a median of 13% of the plausible footprint envelope, and ambiguity is largest for weak, low-overlap plumes. The diagnostics come from PlumeQuant, which recomputes IME, plume length, emission rate, and five-term uncertainty from distributed product components under stated conventions and evaluates four mask representations: the distributed reference mask, a transparent Carbon Mapper-informed analogue (CM-like), the GA ensemble, and optional expert edits. The CM-like mask is generated per plume without access to the reference mask or published quantities, with settings fixed once on a scene-disjoint 44-plume development split. It reproduced published IME with +0.72% median difference and emission rate with +0.16% (6.98% mean absolute), reached 0.843 median intersection-over-union against the reference masks, and matched the published uncertainty scale (median ratio 1.01). Holdout mean absolute errors were 7.6% (IME), 9.5% (length), and 6.1% (rate). These are product-level consistency diagnostics, not independent validation. They flag weak, offset, or ambiguous plumes for expert review.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：矛盾与犹豫情绪检测（Ambivalence and Hesitancy Detection）；场景：非约束视频情感识别；传感器/数据：视频（视觉）、音频、文本转录；目标类别：矛盾/犹豫情绪（非目标检测中的物体类别）。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: CF-Net：多模态网络，包含三个冻结backbone（SigLIP2用于视觉、HuBERT用于音频、DistilBERT用于文本），逐说话人特征归一化，ConflictFusion模块显式计算成对跨模态不一致性，辅助确定性回归头。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。使用了冻结backbone（SigLIP2、HuBERT、DistilBERT），但这属于迁移学习/参数冻结，并非剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化技术。不涉及实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。三个大模型backbone（SigLIP2、HuBERT、DistilBERT）均为冻结使用，计算开销较大，不适合星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：BAH数据集（ABAW挑战赛第3版，ECCV 2026）；评价指标：Macro F1（验证集0.7155，测试集0.7364），AP（测试集0.7492）。

Experiments: 在BAH验证集上Macro F1=0.7155，在私有挑战测试集上Macro F1=0.7364, AP=0.7492。使用了确定性加权focal loss、manifold mixup、模态dropout等训练策略。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 三个大模型backbone计算开销大，未做任何轻量化处理；3. 仅针对情感识别任务，无法迁移到遥感目标检测场景。

Ideas for my research: 可借鉴点极少。唯一可能相关的是ConflictFusion模块中"显式建模不一致性"的思想，如果类比到遥感多模态（如光学+SAR）目标检测中，可考虑设计跨模态冲突/互补特征融合模块。但整体借鉴价值很低。

Abstract: Detecting ambivalence and hesitancy (AH) in unconstrained video is challenging because the target signal is inherently ambiguous and expressed through subtle cross-modal incongruence rather than prototypical affect. We present CF-Net, a deep multimodal network submitted to the 3rd Edition of the AH Video Recognition Challenge (ABAW 11th, ECCV 2026), targeting the BAH dataset. CF-Net encodes visual, audio, and transcript streams with frozen SigLIP2, HuBERT, and DistilBERT backbones, normalises backbone features per speaker to reduce identity leakage, and fuses them via a ConflictFusion module that explicitly computes pairwise cross-modal incongruence. Training combines certainty-weighted focal loss, manifold mixup, and modality dropout; an auxiliary certainty-regression head leverages ambiguity annotations to stabilise learning on genuinely borderline samples. CF-Net achieves a Macro F1 of 0.7155 on the BAH validation set and 0.7364 (AP = 0.7492) on the private challenge test set.

</details>

Experiments: 与现有基线方法对比，在结构连贯性、节奏对齐和感知自然度上有显著提升，同时提供更好的可解释性和可控编辑能力。具体数值结果摘要未说明。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。唯一可能间接参考的点：将大语言模型（LLM）用于语义标注和聚类精炼的思路，在遥感领域或许可用于遥感场景语义理解或目标类别语义关系建模，但与本课题核心方向（在轨目标检测轻量化）关联度极低。

Abstract: Music-driven dance generation aims to produce human motion that is both rhythmically synchronized and semantically consistent with music. While recent neural approaches have achieved impressive visual realism, they typically model motion as a continuous signal and neglect its compositional nature, making generated dances structurally incoherent and difficult to control. In this work, we introduce a structure-aware framework that models choreography as a sequence of atomic movements-semantically interpretable motion events that serve as the building blocks of dance. To construct this atomic movement vocabulary, we first segment large-scale dance data and cluster them into atomic movement groups. We then employ a large language model to semantically relabel and refine the clusters, yielding a set of interpretable and reusable atomic movements. Based on these atomic movement annotations, we design a two-stage generation framework that mirrors the human choreography process. In the atomic movement planning stage, the model predicts the type, duration, and timing of atomic movements conditioned on the input music, forming a symbolic dance allocation. In the completion stage, a transition-aware generator synthesizes smooth and stylistically coherent motion conditioned on the planned structure. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method produces dances with significantly improved structural coherence, rhythmic alignment, and perceptual naturalness compared to existing baselines, while providing enhanced interpretability and controllable editing through explicit structural representation.

</details>


### [82] [Screening Is Effective for Visual Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13983)
*Shunya Shimomura,Kazuhiro Hotta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VisionScreen模型，将语言模型中的Screening机制（基于query-key相似度独立评估token相关性并阈值过滤低相关token）扩展到视觉识别领域，替代ViT的softmax自注意力机制。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。该论文是纯视觉识别方法研究，不涉及遥感/卫星场景，不涉及目标检测任务，不涉及任何轻量化/部署技术。虽然ViT在遥感领域有应用，但本文仅讨论图像分类且方法本身与轻量化无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：图像分类（image classification）；应用场景：通用视觉识别；传感器类型：未说明（通用自然图像）；目标类别：未说明（通用ImageNet类）。不涉及遥感场景、不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: VisionScreen模型：将Screening机制从一维语言序列扩展到二维空间域，每个patch基于query-key相似度独立评估与其他patch的相关性，通过阈值显式排除低相关patch，替代ViT中的softmax自注意力。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。无法判断星上/边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要仅提及"image classification benchmarks"，未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。

Experiments: 在图像分类基准上，VisionScreen优于传统ViT。未提供与其他SOTA方法的详细对比，未提供消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 仅在图像分类任务上验证，未涉及目标检测、分割等更复杂的视觉任务；2. 未讨论计算开销——Screening机制引入的阈值判断和独立相关性计算可能增加额外计算量；3. 未在遥感数据上验证；4. 未讨论轻量化或部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：Screening机制通过显式排除低相关patch来减少冗余信息聚合，这一思路在遥感图像（背景占比大、目标稀疏）中可能有价值，但需要验证其在目标检测任务上的有效性以及计算开销是否可控。

Abstract: Vision Transformer (ViT) has been widely used as a powerful framework for modeling global dependencies among image patches. However, its core component, self-attention assigns softmax-normalized relative weights to all patches, making it difficult to evaluate the relevance between patches independently. In visual recognition, images often contain many background or redundant patches, yet self-attention cannot explicitly reject such irrelevant patches, which may introduce unnecessary information into feature aggregation. To address this limitation, Screening has been proposed in the field of language modeling, where the relevance of each token is independently evaluated based on query-key similarity and low-relevance tokens are explicitly excluded through thresholding. In this work, we propose VisionScreen, a new vision model that extends Screening mechanism to visual recognition. VisionScreen treats image patches as tokens arranged on a two-dimensional grid and extends absolute relevance estimation based on query-key similarity to the two-dimensional spatial domain. This allows each patch to selectively aggregate only content-wise and spatially relevant patches without relying on competition among patches. Experiments on image classification benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms conventional ViT. These results suggest that Screening can be effective for visual recognition, offering an alternative to relative feature aggregation based on softmax attention.

</details>


### [83] [Task-Specific Feature Fusion Method for Multi-Task Affective Behavior Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13986)
*Jiajun Sun,Zhe Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出任务自适应特征融合策略，用于ABAW11多任务情感行为分析（valence-arousal、表情分类、面部动作单元），基于冻结的DINOv2/DINOv3视觉骨干提取特征，为不同任务选择不同的融合与预测策略。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于**面部情感行为分析（Affective Behavior Analysis）**，属于人脸/情感计算领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化/在轨部署。唯一可能间接相关的点是使用了预训练视觉骨干（ViT/ConvNeXt）和特征融合策略，但属于通用视觉方法，对遥感轻量化目标检测的借鉴价值极低。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。除非对多任务自适应融合策略在遥感场景下的迁移感兴趣，否则优先级极低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多任务情感行为分析（valence-arousal回归、categorical expressions分类、facial action units分类）。应用场景：in-the-wild面部情感识别。传感器类型：RGB摄像头（人脸图像）。目标类别：情感维度（valence-arousal）、表情类别、面部动作单元。与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: 使用两个预训练视觉骨干：DINOv2 ViT-L和DINOv3 ConvNeXt-base，在外部表情数据集上微调后冻结。在冻结特征之上，比较了多种预测头：frame-level heads、temporal convolutional heads、post-hoc temporal smoothing、LightGBM models；多种融合策略：feature concatenation、gated fusion、residual fusion、late logit fusion；以及threshold calibration和shared MTL structures。最终为每个任务选择不同的融合与预测策略。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。虽然使用了冻结特征（可减少部分计算量），但骨干网络本身是ViT-L和ConvNeXt-base这样的大模型，并非为轻量化设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。使用的DINOv2 ViT-L和DINOv3 ConvNeXt-base均为大规模视觉模型，不适合星上/边缘部署。未提及FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等硬件部署方案。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：s-Aff-Wild2（ABAW11官方数据集），以及外部表情数据集（external expression-oriented facial image set）。评价指标：EXPR macro-F1（0.4222）、AU macro-F1（0.5402）、mean VA CCC（0.6717），总体验证分数1.6341。与遥感目标检测常用指标（mAP、F1、Recall、IoU等）完全不同。

Experiments: 在ABAW11验证集上进行了系统的消融实验，比较了多种预测头、融合策略、校准方法和共享MTL结构。最终选择任务特定的融合与预测策略，而非所有任务共享单一架构。验证集结果：EXPR macro-F1=0.4222，AU macro-F1=0.5402，mean VA CCC=0.6717，总体=1.6341。未与遥感目标检测方法进行对比。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 未涉及任何轻量化技术；3. 未提供模型效率指标（参数量、FLOPs、推理速度等）；4. 仅在情感分析数据集上验证，未在遥感场景下测试；5. 骨干网络为大规模预训练模型，计算开销大。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴价值极低。唯一可能间接参考的点是：1）"任务自适应融合"的思想——在遥感多任务场景（如同时检测舰船、飞机、车辆）中，不同目标类别可能受益于不同的特征层或融合策略；2）使用冻结预训练骨干+轻量预测头的范式，可类比于遥感领域使用预训练遥感基础模型（如Swin Transformer、ViT）作为特征提取器，再设计轻量检测头。但整体而言，该论文对"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题的参考意义非常有限。

Abstract: The 11th Affective Behavior Analysis in-the-wild (ABAW11) Multi-Task Learning Challenge requires a unified system to predict valence-arousal, categorical expressions, and facial action units from the official s-Aff-Wild2 images. Although these tasks are naturally related through facial behavior, our validation experiments show that they benefit from different visual features, temporal processing strategies, fusion mechanisms, and calibration procedures. In this paper, we study task-adaptive feature fusion for ABAW11 multi-task affective behavior analysis. We first adapt two pretrained visual backbones, DINOv2 ViT-L and DINOv3 ConvNeXt-base, on an external expression-oriented facial image set and then freeze them to extract complementary frame-level features from the official ABAW11 data. On top of these frozen features, we systematically compare frame-level prediction heads, temporal convolutional heads, post-hoc temporal smoothing, LightGBM models, feature concatenation, gated fusion, residual fusion, late logit fusion, threshold calibration, and shared MTL structures. The final system selects task-specific fusion and prediction strategies rather than forcing all tasks to share a single architecture. On the ABAW11 validation set, the selected system achieves an EXPR macro-F1 of 0.4222, an AU macro-F1 of 0.5402, and a mean VA CCC of 0.6717, resulting in an overall validation score of 1.6341. The results suggest that task-adaptive fusion of frozen visual features is a simple and effective strategy for ABAW-style multi-task affective behavior analysis.

</details>

Reading priority: 1分，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多专家路由OCR识别；应用场景：历史满文文档识别（regular script, running script, semi-cursive chancery hand）；传感器类型：文档扫描图像；目标类别：满文字符。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 多专家系统：迭代微调检查点作为领域专家 + 轻量级页面级图像分类器（router）进行风格分派。摘要未说明具体网络结构。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：三个冻结测试集（regular script, memorials, running script）；评价指标：CER（字符错误率）、页面级领域分类准确率（99.3%）。与遥感数据集无关。

Experiments: 路由系统在三个测试集上匹配所选专家的精度：regular script CER 0.30%，memorials CER 1.57%，running script CER 4.83%；路由器页面级领域准确率99.3%，与领域标签oracle精度一致。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的任何借鉴。局限性包括：仅适用于文档OCR场景，路由策略依赖预定义的书写风格类别，未讨论跨领域泛化问题。

Ideas for my research: 对本课题无可借鉴点。虽然"多专家路由"的思想在理论上可迁移到遥感多场景（如不同传感器/不同分辨率）的模型选择，但本文未涉及任何遥感目标检测或模型轻量化的核心技术，不建议作为参考。

Abstract: Historical Manchu OCR must accommodate various visually distinct writing styles, including regular script, running script, and the semi-cursive chancery hand used in palace memorials, despite limited labeled data. We study a multi-expert system that reuses checkpoints from an iterative fine-tuning process as domain specialists and uses a lightweight page-level image classifier to dispatch pages by visual style. When the checkpoint pool lacks a suitable specialist, we train an additional expert for that domain. On three frozen test sets, the routed system matches the selected specialist for each style at two-decimal precision: 0.30 percent CER on regular script, 1.57 percent on memorials, and 4.83 percent on running script. The router achieves 99.3 percent page-level domain accuracy and matches the domain-label oracle at the same precision. Two of the three selected specialists were not trained specifically for their final domain; only the running-script expert was trained with that domain as its target. We report the evaluation protocol, router design, and per-page predictions to make the comparison reproducible.

</details>

Lightweight method: 不涉及。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时推理等星上/边缘部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Black Myth: Wukong 游戏数据集，超过90小时游戏视频，包含帧对齐的玩家动作、真实游戏状态、视觉观测及结构化/语义标注。评价指标：摘要未说明。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果。论文为综述性质，按四个维度对现有方法进行分类讨论。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可参考的局限性信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题在问题定义、技术路线、评价体系上均无交集。

Abstract: Building interactive worlds that respond coherently to player actions has long been a shared goal of computer graphics, games, and artificial intelligence. Recent video generative models provide a data-driven route toward this goal by predicting future observations conditioned on user actions, and are increasingly regarded as potential next-generation game engines. Realizing a genuinely interactive game world, however, requires interaction outcomes that follow rules over evolving game conditions, consequences that persist over long horizons, and a generation loop that operates in real time. Conventional game engines realize these properties through a recurrent action-state-observation loop, in which player actions update an explicit game state according to predefined rules and observations are rendered from the resulting state. Taking this loop as an organizing lens, this paper examines interactive game world modeling along four dimensions: player action control, game state dynamics, state-observation persistence, and real-time interactive generation. For each dimension, we start from the capabilities required by an interactive game world, group existing approaches into representative families, and discuss the strengths and trade-offs of each family. Complementing this analysis, we present a scalable data engine for Black Myth: Wukong that collects over 90 hours of gameplay with frame-aligned player actions, ground-truth game states, and visual observations, together with structured and semantic annotations, as a resource for state-aware game world modeling. We hope this paper offers a clear picture of where the field stands and fosters progress toward interactive game worlds.

</details>


### [86] [VideoRAE: Taming Video Foundation Models for Generative Modeling via Representation Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14088)
*Zhihao Xie,Junfeng Wu,Xinting Hu,Junchao Huang,Li Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VideoRAE，利用冻结的视频基础模型（VFM）的多尺度层次特征，通过轻量1D自注意力投影器和多码本高维量化，构建兼具重建能力和生成友好性的视频潜空间表示，替代传统3D-VAE。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于视频生成模型（video generative models）的潜空间表示学习，涉及UCF-101等视频数据集，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及在轨部署或星上推理。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不推荐优先阅读。仅对量化压缩和表示对齐方法有极有限的间接参考价值。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：视频潜空间表示学习（video latent representation learning）、视频重建与生成；应用场景：视频生成（class-to-video, text-to-video）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: VideoRAE：使用冻结的VFM（V-JEPA 2 / VideoMAEv2）作为编码器提取多尺度层次特征，通过轻量1D self-attention projector进行压缩，支持连续潜变量（用于Diffusion Transformers）和离散token（用于自回归模型，通过multi-codebook high-dimensional quantization实现）。解码时采用local-and-global representation alignment objective与冻结的VFM教师进行对齐。

Lightweight method: 轻量1D self-attention projector（轻量投影器）；multi-codebook high-dimensional quantization（多码本高维量化）。但轻量化并非本文核心贡献方向，而是作为潜空间压缩的手段。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到训练收敛速度比竞争方法快约5倍，但这是训练效率而非推理部署效率。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：UCF-101（class-to-video生成任务）；另有2B-scale text-to-video研究（具体数据集未说明）。评价指标：gFVD（generation Frechet Video Distance），连续和离散模式下分别取得40和93的SOTA结果。

Experiments: 在UCF-101上，使用AR生成器获得gFVD=40，使用DiT生成器获得gFVD=93，均为SOTA；训练收敛速度比竞争autoencoder基线快约5倍；在2B-scale text-to-video实验中，替换LTX-VAE为VideoRAE后收敛更快。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星场景；2. 不涉及目标检测任务；3. 不涉及在轨部署或边缘部署；4. 未报告推理时的参数量、FLOPs、延迟等部署关键指标；5. 依赖大规模预训练的VFM（如V-JEPA 2），计算开销可能较大。

Ideas for my research: 1. 借鉴"冻结预训练编码器+轻量投影器"的思路：在遥感在轨场景中，可考虑冻结一个预训练的遥感基础模型（如SatMAE、SpectralGPT等），仅训练轻量投影头进行特征压缩，降低在轨计算负担；2. multi-codebook quantization的思路可用于遥感目标检测模型的权重量化或特征量化，减少模型存储和传输开销；3. representation alignment（表示对齐）的思想可用于遥感检测模型的知识蒸馏，将大教师模型的知识对齐到轻量学生模型。

Abstract: Video generative models commonly rely on latent spaces learned by 3D Variational Autoencoders (3D-VAEs). However, conventional 3D-VAEs are mainly optimized for pixel-level reconstruction, which can limit the semantic and spatio-temporal structure captured by their latents. Meanwhile, Video Foundation Models (VFMs) such as V-JEPA 2 and VideoMAEv2 show strong video understanding capabilities, yet whether their frozen representations can be transformed into compact, reconstruction-capable, and generation-friendly video latents remains largely unexplored. We answer this question with VideoRAE, a representation autoencoder that leverages multi-scale hierarchical features from a frozen video foundation encoder and compresses them with a lightweight 1D self-attention projector. VideoRAE supports both continuous latents for Diffusion Transformers and discrete tokens for autoregressive models via multi-codebook high-dimensional quantization. During decoding, a local-and-global representation alignment objective with the frozen VFM teacher improves semantic preservation and enables training without KL regularization. Experiments show that VideoRAE achieves strong reconstruction in both continuous and discrete regimes. On UCF-101, it obtains state-of-the-art class-to-video gFVDs of 40 and 93 with AR and DiT generators, respectively, while converging approximately 5x faster than competing autoencoder baselines. In a controlled 2B-scale text-to-video study, replacing LTX-VAE with VideoRAE leads to faster convergence under comparable settings. These results validate frozen VFM representations as versatile and generation-friendly video latents. The model and code will be released on https://zhxie0117.github.io/VideoRAE.

</details>

### [87] [OriginBlame: Record- and Token-Level Data Provenance for AI Training Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13037)
*Haolin Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种记录级和token级的数据溯源系统ob，用于精确定位需要遗忘的训练数据记录，解决模型遗忘中因缺乏细粒度溯源导致的过度删除问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于数据溯源和模型遗忘（machine unlearning）问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：数据溯源与模型遗忘（machine unlearning）；应用场景：大语言模型训练数据管理；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，仅在评估中提及1.7B参数模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化方法。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等内容。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：219,555 Wikipedia pages；评价指标：over-deletion倍数（从101x降至1.3x）、吞吐开销（1.3-4.0% on HuggingFace, 2.1-19.0% on Datatrove）、遗忘效果提升（42% over random baselines）。

Experiments: 在Wikipedia数据上验证了记录级溯源消除了数据集级过度删除（从101倍降至1.3倍）；集成开销在HuggingFace上为1.3-4.0%，在Datatrove上为2.1-19.0%；在1.7B模型上，基于溯源的遗忘集比随机基线提升42%。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可参考的局限性信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文研究的是LLM数据溯源与遗忘，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: When a data contributor requests removal, model trainers face a practical gap: unlearning algorithms require a forget set, yet no tool can locate which training records belong to a given author. Existing provenance systems operate at file or dataset level, forcing catastrophic over-deletion. We present ob, a record- and token-level data provenance system that propagates author identity through data processing pipelines and resolves revocation requests into precise forget sets via deterministic queries. Evaluation on 219,555 Wikipedia pages demonstrates that record-level provenance eliminates dataset-level over-deletion (from 101x to 1.3x), while integration adds 1.3-4.0% throughput overhead (HuggingFace) and 2.1-19.0% (Datatrove) on wiki data. On a 1.7B model, provenance-based forget sets improve unlearning by 42% over random baselines.

</details>


### [88] [SPINE: Bridging the Cyber-Physical Gap with Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13049)
*Minkyu Ham,Dongho Kim,Chan Lee,Jiayi Wang,Min Jun Kim,Yixi Zhang,Guo Ye,Jihai Zhao,Soyeon Park,Han Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SPINE智能体框架，用于自动化调试和部署双臂机器人，降低对机器人专家的依赖。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。论文聚焦于机器人（bimanual robot）的自动化部署与调试框架，属于Embodied AI和机器人操作领域，不涉及任何遥感图像、卫星在轨处理或目标检测任务。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人部署与调试（robot deployment & debugging）；应用场景：双臂机器人（bimanual robot）的遥操作（teleoperation）校准；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：无。

Model architecture: SPINE框架包含两个多智能体工作流：profile builder（构建机器人特定上下文）和debugger（诊断-修复-验证循环）。属于agentic framework，非深度学习模型架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文讨论的是机器人平台的部署调试，而非星上/边缘部署。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等信息。

Datasets and metrics: 实验平台：DOBOT X-Trainer（7个调试场景）、AgileX PiPER（10个植入bug）；评价指标：operationalization success（成功率）、mean time-to-teleoperation（平均遥操作时间）。

Experiments: 在DOBOT X-Trainer上，使用SPINE的机器人新手将成功率从75%提升至100%，平均遥操作时间从16分45秒降至13分47秒。在AgileX PiPER上，SPINE解决了全部10个植入bug（专家基线解决9/10）。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无直接可参考的局限性信息。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。虽然论文的"自动化部署与调试"思路在概念层面可能启发在轨模型的自动化部署流程设计，但技术栈（多智能体框架、机器人遥操作）与遥感目标检测轻量化（模型压缩、硬件部署）差异极大，借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Foundation models have given robots a sophisticated brain for complex decision-making, yet deploying that intelligence into a physical platform still demands tedious, expert-driven calibration. This deployment gap, the robot's spinal cord, remains a primary bottleneck to scalable Embodied AI. Hence, we propose SPINE (Scalable Physical Integration with ageNtic Expertise): an agentic framework for systematically debugging and deploying bimanual robots with minimal robotics expertise. SPINE's harness comprises two orchestrated multi-agent workflows: a profile builder that creates robot-specific context, and a debugger that cycles through diagnosis, repair, and validation until teleoperation works. Across seven DOBOT X-Trainer debugging scenarios, a robotics novice using SPINE outperformed human operators using Claude Code with the same reference materials, but without SPINE's structured workflow, improving operationalization success from 75% to 100% and reducing mean time-to-teleoperation from 16 min 45 s to 13 min 47 s. On AgileX PiPER, a distinct ROS/CAN bimanual arm, SPINE resolved all 10 implanted bugs, versus 9 out of 10 for the expert baseline, in nearly the same amount of time. Together, these results show that SPINE can transfer across bimanual platforms, reduce dependence on expert calibration, and move embodied AI closer to scalable real-world deployment.

</details>


### [89] [Interventional Grounding Audits: Black-Box Premise-Dependency Tests for LLM Chain-of-Thought via Predicate Substitution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13069)
*Hironao Nakamura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种黑盒、步骤级的干预式审计方法（interventional grounding audits），用于检测大语言模型在链式推理中是否真正依赖其声称的前提，在ProntoQA合成数据集上验证。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。研究的是大语言模型的推理忠实度审计方法，属于自然语言处理/可解释AI方向。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：大语言模型链式推理的忠实度审计（premise dependency detection）；场景：合成推理基准ProntoQA；传感器/目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 被审计模型为GPT-4o；审计方法为interventional grounding audits（黑盒、步骤级、前提替换干预）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：ProntoQA（合成多跳演绎推理基准，含金标准证明树）；指标：F1、Recall；基线：self-consistency baseline。

Experiments: 在50个ProntoQA问题上用GPT-4o测试，检测证明树依赖的F1=0.806（谓词决定依赖F1=0.885，Recall=100%），显著优于自一致性基线（F1=0.343，95% bootstrap CI不重叠）。

Limitations: 1. 仅适用于形式化、可解析的基准（ProntoQA），超出此范围的方法局限性作者已讨论；2. 与遥感、目标检测、轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于NLP/可解释AI方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Large language models produce chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning that appears logically sound yet may not genuinely depend on its stated premises. We introduce interventional grounding audits, a black-box, step-level test of premise dependency: we intervene on a single premise by substituting its target predicate with a fresh symbol, re-run the model, and check whether each reasoning step's normalized conclusion (canonical predicate form) changes. We evaluate on ProntoQA, a synthetic multi-hop deductive reasoning benchmark with gold proof trees, where step-level premise dependencies are known. Applied to 50 ProntoQA problems with GPT-4o, our method achieves F1 = 0.806 on detecting proof-tree dependencies (F1 = 0.885 on predicate-determining dependencies; Recall = 100%), significantly outperforming a self-consistency baseline (F1 = 0.343; 95% bootstrap CIs non-overlapping). We further identify that 66% of correctly-solved problems contain at least one aligned step insensitive to a direct proof-tree dependency under consistent substitution -- all involving entity-introduction premises, a documented blind spot of the consistent-substitution evaluator -- a "right answer, wrong reasoning" signal invisible to passive methods. All audit certificates, raw outputs, and reproduction scripts are available in a public GitHub repository, and we discuss scope limits beyond formal, parsable benchmarks.

</details>


### [90] [Probabilistic Extension of Neuro-Symbolic AGI Robots based on Belnap's Typed Intensional FOL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13073)
*Zoran Majkic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于IFOL_B的概率神经符号AGI框架，利用神经网络计算香农最大信息熵下的概率密度函数，实现全局和局部对称性变换下的知识推理与实时决策。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文讨论的是神经符号AI与通用人工智能（AGI）的形式逻辑框架，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文属于通用人工智能/神经符号推理领域，未涉及任何遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 基于IFOL_B的神经符号AI架构，包含全局对称性变换（保持知识库和逻辑演绎）和局部对称性变换（用于实时子问题决策），利用神经网络计算概率密度函数KI。

Abstract: Neuro-symbolic AI based on $IFOL_B$ is a way to combine neural learning and symbolic reasoning to overcome limitations of purely neural systems (like lack of interpretability and logical structure) with formal logical machinery for self-reference. In this paper we expand the cognitive power of $IFOL_B$ by using the probability computation for the currently unknown sentences, based on Nilsson's probability structure for the $IFOL_B$. We introduce the global symmetry transformation that preserves the current knowledge database and logical deduction, and the local one used for real-time decisions about concrete (sub)problems that involve only a very strict subset of $IFOL_B$ predicates. The computation of probability density function $KI$ in both cases, based on the Shannon's maximum information entropy, is provided by neural networks of this probabilistic neuro-symbolic AGI.

</details>

*Zhe Ren,Yimeng Chen,Dandan Guo,Guowei Rong,Tonghui Li,R. B. Xiong,Qingfeng Lan,Wenyi Wang,Li Nanbo,Yibo Yang,Mingchen Zhuge,Jürgen Schmidhuber*

Abstract: Self-improving autonomous agents are moving from research prototypes to deployed systems. The primary goal is controllable evolution, or adaptation, from experience with minimal or even no human input. This survey frames modern self-improving agents as adaptive systems that convert experience into accumulated capability gains. We offer a system-level framework that represents a modern agent as a configuration coupling a foundation model with an operational scaffold of prompts, memory, tools, and control logic. Within this framework, self-improvement is formalized as a self-induced update operator that obtains and commits updates to model parameters or scaffold components. We organize prior work by update target and by the signals that drive change, then review applications and discuss evaluation, before closing with open problems and future directions. For convenience, we track technical updates on https://github.com/selfimproving-agent/awesome-Self-Improving-Agents.

</details>


### [92] [Improving Molecular Property Prediction in Small Language Models Using Graph-based Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13115)
*Konstantinos Bougiatiotis,Dimitrios Kelesis,Georgios Paliouras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种上下文增强提示框架，利用GNN为小语言模型提供分子图结构信息，提升SMILES字符串的分子性质预测能力。

Experiments: 图上下文增强提示相比仅SMILES提示，准确率提升超过25%，在Tox21上最高达74%相对提升。但仍有差距于专用GNN模型。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感、目标检测或轻量化内容。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) have shown promise for zero-shot molecular property prediction from SMILES strings, yet they often suffer from structural blindness because sequence representations under-specify key graph-topological cues. We propose a modular Context-Augmented Prompting framework that enables agentic tool use at inference time: a trained GNN expert model provides a predictive hint with confidence, and a GNN extracts an instance-specific explanatory subgraph (e.g., a subgraph SMILES and an accompanying explanatory paragraph). We evaluate three commonly used SLMs on MUTAG and Tox21 under five prompting configurations ranging from SMILES-only to using all available tools at hand. Across two datasets, enriching prompts with graph-derived context yields substantial accuracy gains, often exceeding 25% relative improvement and up to 74% on Tox21. We further validate the functional relevance of the extracted motifs via a necessity-based edge-drop intervention. Despite the observed gains, a persistent gap remains to specialized GNN models, highlighting both the value and limits of text-conditioned reasoning for molecular structure.

</details>

*Richmond Alake,Cesare Bernardis,Paul Cayet,Luca Engel,Damien Hilloulin,Sungpack Hong,Allen Hosler,Nickolas Kavantzas,Ingo Kossyk,Son Le,Rhicheek Patra,Kartik Talamadupula,Valentin Venzin*

<details>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感相关任务。论文任务为长周期AI Agent的记忆管理，场景为对话系统/智能体系统，不涉及任何遥感传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明目标检测模型结构。论文提出的是Oracle Agent Memory架构，包含active memory core和passive memory-store interface，以及记忆生命周期管理（ingestion, extraction, consolidation, retrieval, summarization, revision/removal）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。论文关注的是记忆系统的token效率（比flat-history基线少用约10.7x tokens），但这属于系统级优化而非模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署信息。论文提及latency和estimated token use，但这是针对记忆检索系统的延迟和token消耗，而非模型推理的部署指标。完全不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 使用LongMemEval数据集，指标包括下游任务准确率（93.8%）、evidence retrieval、recall、latency、estimated token use。这些是Agent记忆系统的评估指标，与遥感目标检测的mAP、FPS、FLOPs等无关。

Experiments: 在LongMemEval上达到93.8%准确率，与flat-history基线对比，使用约10.7x更少的token。与外部基线进行了对比。但所有实验均围绕记忆系统性能，不涉及目标检测任务。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关，无法提供遥感目标检测或轻量化方面的局限性分析。论文本身作为Agent记忆系统研究，其局限性可能涉及记忆检索的准确性、跨会话一致性等，但与遥感在轨检测课题无关。

Ideas for my research: 基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能间接相关的是：论文中提到的"memory lifecycle"概念（ingestion→extraction→consolidation→retrieval→summarization→revision/removal）如果类比到星上模型持续学习/增量学习场景，或许有启发，但关联非常弱。不建议在本课题中参考此论文。

Abstract: Agent memory is a systems problem for long-horizon agents. Practical deployments require retention of task state across extended conversations, recovery of user-specific facts and preferences across sessions, and accumulation of procedural knowledge from prior outcomes. These requirements extend beyond document retrieval: a memory layer must determine which interactions become durable state, how that state is scoped, how it is retrieved under latency constraints, and how it is revised or removed over time. This report studies Oracle Agent Memory as a database-native memory substrate built on Oracle Database. Three themes organize the discussion: memory as a lifecycle spanning ingestion, extraction, consolidation, retrieval, summarization, and revision or removal; a layered architecture that separates an active memory core from a passive memory-store interface with explicit scope control across users, agents, and threads; and evaluation methodology in which downstream task accuracy is complemented by memory-centric measures such as evidence retrieval, recall, latency, and estimated token use. The report summarizes LongMemEval results, reaching 93.8% accuracy, compares Oracle Agent Memory against flat-history baselines, using about 10.7x fewer tokens, and published or reported external baselines where available, and closes with implementation-oriented appendix material covering setup, thread lifecycle, and search semantics.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Limitations: 1）完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化任何相关主题；2）方法依赖人类在线反馈，不适合星上自主推理场景；3）未讨论模型大小、推理速度等部署效率指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的间接启发是：在缺乏标注数据时，人类偏好反馈（preference feedback）作为一种弱监督信号的思想，但与本课题的遥感目标检测轻量化方向差异过大。

Abstract: We address the problem of safely training an agent policy and deploying a good and safe policy, in settings where the environment dynamics are unknown and no suitable reward function is available. In the context of safety-critical environments, we consider traditional reinforcement learning impractical and resort to the resource of human input. We introduce DROPJ, a human-centred method for both safe training and deployment. We first learn a world model (a learned simulator) from a dataset of prior real-world trajectories. A human then plays the game in this learned simulator to extract several informative simulated trajectories. From these, we sample pairs of simulated trajectory segments and elicit from a human their preference over these segments, as well as a reason (justification) for their choice. We then train a reward model from these justified preferences and use it, together with the world model, to directly deploy the agent using model predictive control. Running real-user experiments, we find that generating informative simulated trajectories from a user significantly reduces the computational cost during training compared to other strategies, and can also improve the performance during deployment. In the context of training within a learned simulator, we show that the use of preferences rather than other types of feedback substantially improves the performance during deployment. We further demonstrate that safety justifications accompanying preferences can significantly enhance safety or prioritise user-prescribed aspects of safety associated with them during deployment.

</details>


### [95] [CayleyR: Solving the TopSpin puzzle via cycle intersection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13219)
*Yuri Baramykov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个用于求解排列谜题的R包cayleyR，核心算法基于Cayley图中循环交集的迭代双向搜索，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Model architecture: 核心算法为迭代双向搜索（iterative bidirectional search），在Cayley图中从初始和目标排列状态分别生成随机操作序列，检测循环交集以构建连接路径。实现采用C++哈希索引状态存储，可选Vulkan GPU加速。不涉及任何神经网络或深度学习模型架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然提及Vulkan GPU加速，但这是针对谜题求解的计算加速，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。论文针对TopSpin(n,k)谜题进行求解，不涉及标准遥感或目标检测数据集。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于组合数学/算法求解领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: We present cayleyR, an R package for solving permutation puzzles by detecting cycle intersections in Cayley graphs. The core algorithm performs an iterative bidirectional search: from both the initial and target permutation states, random operation sequences generate cycles in the Cayley graph of the symmetric group Sn; their intersection yields a connecting path. When no direct intersection is found, a distance-guided bridge selection narrows the gap, and the process repeats. The package targets the TopSpin(n,k) puzzle, whose state space is a Cayley graph of Sn generated by a cyclic shift and a prefix reversal. We describe the mathematical framework, the algorithm, and its implementation, which combines a C++ hash-indexed state store with optional Vulkan GPU acceleration. The software is publicly available on CRAN.

</details>


### [96] [Networked Intelligence: Active Shared Context Graphs for Human-AI Team Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13220)
*Sutanay Choudhury,Jeffrey J. Czajka,Lummy M. O. Monteiro,Erin Bredeweg,Jason McDermott,Katherine Wolf,Alex Beliaev,Josh Elmore,Paul Piehowski,Kylee Tate,Yuqian Gao,Aivett Bilbao,Kelly Stratton,Scott Baker,Jaydeep P. Bardhan,Kristin Burnum Johnson,Chris Oehmen,Robert Rallo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个名为Mycelium的多用户AI协同科学工作空间，用于连接研究人员和AI代理以解决复杂科学问题，核心是网络化智能而非单一模型扩展。

Model architecture: Mycelium系统架构：主动共享工作空间（active shared workspace），包含观察/假设捕获、关系追踪、知识路由等模块。将网络化智能建模为分布式科学上下文上的稀疏条件计算（sparse conditional computation）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Experiments: 在生物多组学（multi-omics）任务上进行了首次实证测试，展示了路由共享上下文如何将局部分析发现转化为跨专家机制约束并最终形成实验设计。未提供与其他方法的定量对比或消融实验。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 仅在一个生物领域场景进行了初步测试，缺乏广泛验证；3. 未提供量化指标和对比基线；4. 系统复杂度、可扩展性、实际部署可行性未讨论。

Ideas for my research: 对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的可借鉴点极少。唯一可能间接相关的概念是"sparse conditional computation over distributed scientific contexts"——稀疏条件计算的思想在模型轻量化中（如MoE、条件计算）有一定理论关联，但论文并未将其应用于模型压缩或加速，且场景完全不同。总体而言，不建议从本文寻找直接借鉴。

Abstract: Most AI-for-science systems focus on scaling a single reasoning process through better models, larger context windows, long-horizon agentic execution, or digital co-scientists working with one principal user. However, challenging scientific problems are rarely solved by one reasoner alone. They are solved by teams whose members bring different priors, experimental backgrounds, tacit knowledge, and domain-trained intuitions. The open problem is therefore not only how to scale models, but how to cultivate networked intelligence: scaling the connections between humans and AI systems so that a result or hypothesis produced in one context reaches another person, agent, instrument, or robot that can act on it. We introduce Mycelium, an active shared workspace that automatically connects researchers and AI agents as a multi-user co-scientist. As human users and agents work, the system captures important observations and hypotheses, tracks how they relate to the team's evolving model, and routes them to the person or agent whose next decision they can inform. We evaluate Mycelium in its first empirical test, a biological multi-omics campaign in which routed shared context turned a local analytical finding into a cross-expert mechanistic constraint and ultimately into an experimental design. We also give networked intelligence a computational account as sparse conditional computation over distributed scientific contexts. This account distinguishes when a scaled standalone agent can match the network from when independent expertise and non-mergeable contexts make the network irreducible.

</details>


### [97] [AI-Native Insurance for Agentic AI: Pricing, Underwriting, and End-to-End Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13230)

Abstract: Agentic AI introduces new insurance challenges because autonomous AI systems can make decisions, invoke tools, modify external environments, and interact with third-party services. This paper develops an AI-native mathematical framework for underwriting, pricing, and contract design for agentic AI deployments. A deployment is represented by a risk state that captures autonomy level, operational authority, permission exposure, governance maturity, and dependency concentration. The framework maps the risk state to event probabilities, loss severities, governance costs, premiums, deductibles, coverage allocation, and policy covenants, and formulates an optimization problem for insurance contract design under participation, profitability, and incentive compatibility constraints. The paper establishes structural properties of insurability, including characterization of an insurability region, monotone deterioration of feasibility with increasing exposure, and governance certification thresholds. Insurance is further interpreted as both an operational cost and a regulatory mechanism for AI deployment. A healthcare case study illustrates contract optimization, sensitivity analysis, and automated claims processing for agentic AI systems.

</details>


### [98] [Cost-Optimal Foundation Model Deployment Portfolio for Transportation Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13239)
*Xi Cheng,Ke Liu,Siyuan Feng,Jane Lin,H. Oliver Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Foundation Model Deployment Portfolio (FMDP)问题，用混合整数规划在共享GPU预算下为交通管理中心的多功能选择最优模型部署组合（闭源API/开源API/本地GPU），以最小化总拥有成本。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦交通管理中心（TMC）的通用基础模型部署组合优化问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务或模型轻量化方法（剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS等）。虽然涉及部署成本优化和GPU资源分配，但场景和任务与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"完全不对齐。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：基础模型部署组合优化（FMDP）；场景：交通管理中心（TMC）；传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器；目标类别：异常检测、事件报告、旅行者信息等TMC功能，非遥感目标检测。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。涉及LLMs和VLMs作为候选模型，但未给出具体模型名称或结构细节。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。虽然涉及GPU硬件预算和延迟约束，但场景是TMC数据中心，非卫星在轨环境。摘要提到本地GPU投资在约309次视觉查询/小时以上才合理，但这是TMC场景的break-even分析，与星上部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标：总拥有成本(TCO)、每功能质量、延迟、安全性约束。案例研究涉及5个TMC功能和19个候选(模型, 模式)对。

Experiments: 案例研究显示FMDP方法找到的混合部署组合成本为$34/月，比最便宜的纯闭源API方案低97%。将4个功能路由到开源API，1个功能路由到闭源API。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 不涉及任何模型轻量化技术；3. 不涉及目标检测任务；4. 场景假设（TMC数据中心）与星上边缘计算环境差异巨大；5. 未考虑星上特有的功耗、散热、辐射加固等约束。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义非常有限。唯一可能相关的点是：论文提出的"在共享硬件预算下为不同功能选择最优模型部署模式"的思路，在概念层面可类比为"在星上有限计算资源下为不同遥感任务选择不同轻量化模型"，但具体方法（混合整数规划、TCO优化）与在轨轻量化目标检测的研究范式差异很大，不建议直接借鉴。

Abstract: Foundation models, including large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs), are increasingly used for transportation management center (TMC) tasks such as anomaly detection, incident reporting, and traveler information. Deploying multiple such models across TMC functions raises a portfolio question: which model should serve each function, in which deployment mode, and under what shared hardware budget? We formulate this as the Foundation Model Deployment Portfolio (FMDP) problem, a mixed-integer program minimizing total cost of ownership (TCO) subject to per-function quality, latency, and safety constraints over shared GPU capacity. We prove the problem NP-hard by reduction from the 0-1 knapsack problem and propose a polynomial-time greedy heuristic. In an illustrative case study with five TMC functions and 19 candidate (model, mode) pairs, FMDP identifies a mixed portfolio costing $34/mo (97% below the cheapest feasible all-closed-API baseline) by routing four functions to open-source APIs and the one function whose quality floor no open-source model meets to a closed API. Break-even analysis shows that on-premise GPU investment becomes reasonable only above approximately 309 vision queries/hour or if API prices double.

</details>


### [99] [Harness Handbook: Making Evolving Agent Harnesses Readable,Navigable, and Editable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13285)
*Ruhan Wang,Yucheng Shi,Zongxia Li,Zhongzhi Li,Yue Yu,Junyao Yang,Kishan Panaganti,Haitao Mi,Dongruo Zhou,Leoweiliang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种面向AI agent系统（harness）的行为定位方法Harness Handbook，通过静态分析和LLM辅助结构化，帮助开发者在大型代码库中定位需要修改的代码位置。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关，属于软件工程/代码搜索领域的研究，讨论的是AI agent系统的harness代码演化问题。

Experiments: 在两个开源harness代码库上的修改请求上进行实验，验证了Handbook-Assisted planning在behavior localization和edit-plan质量上的提升，同时使用更少的planner tokens。
