### [186] [LiTCom: A Lightweight Transmitter and Inference-Capable Receiver Framework for 6G Uplink](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13114)
*Chunmei Xu,Siqi Zhang,Zhi Ding,Yi Ma,Rahim Tafazolli*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出LiTCom轻量级发射机与推理型接收机框架，面向6G上行链路通信，通过简化发射端编码（低通滤波+最小信道编码）和接收端大模型推理实现低SNR下的高保真通信，发射端计算量降低95%以上。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦6G无线通信上行链路，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及在轨部署或星上智能。虽然提到了"lightweight transmitter"和计算量降低，但这是通信编码层面的轻量化，与模型剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS等深度学习模型轻量化方法无关。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：6G上行链路通信（非目标检测）；应用场景：边缘设备到网络基础设施的无线通信；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 发射端：低通滤波（source coding）+ 最小信道编码；接收端：大规模生成式AI模型（GenAI）用于从高度失真信号中推断高语义保真内容。摘要未说明具体GenAI模型架构。

Lightweight method: 通信编码层面的轻量化：发射端采用低通滤波替代JPEG源编码，采用最小信道编码替代LDPC信道编码，降低发射端处理复杂度。这与深度学习模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提到发射端计算量降低95%以上（对比5G NR-like baseline），但接收端使用大规模GenAI模型，计算开销可能很大，不适合星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 评价指标：QoE（quality of experience）、SNR增益（dB）。摘要未说明使用的数据集。

Experiments: 对比基线：5G NR-like baseline（JPEG源编码+LDPC信道编码）和Deep-JSCC baseline。结果：LiTCom分别获得高达8 dB和2.5 dB的SNR增益，发射端计算量降低95%以上。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 接收端依赖大规模GenAI模型，推理开销大，不适合资源受限的边缘/星上场景；3. 仅关注通信链路，不涉及任何视觉感知任务；4. 未讨论模型轻量化技术。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：1. 通信编码层面的轻量化思路（低通滤波替代复杂编码）可类比于特征压缩/降维方法；2. 资源不对称（发射端轻量、接收端重）的设计思想，在星地协同场景中可参考——卫星端轻量推理、地面站端复杂后处理。但整体与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接关联度很低。

Abstract: This paper introduces LiTCom, a lightweight transmitter and inference-capable receiver framework, designed to enable robust 6G uplink communication under low signal-to-noise (SNR) conditions. It embraces the resource asymmetry between edge devices and the network infrastructure. LiTCom simplifies transmitter design by applying basic low-pass filtering for source coding and minimal channel coding, significantly reducing the processing complexity. The receiver employs large-scale generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) models to infer high semantic-fidelity content from highly distorted and degraded signals beyond traditional decoding capabilities. Furthermore, efficient power allocation strategies are developed by exploiting data importance to improve system performance, which is measured by the introduced quality of experience (QoE) metric. Simulation results validate the effectiveness of the proposed LiTCom framework and the lightweight coding design. Compared with the 5G NR-like baseline (using JPEG source coding and LDPC channel coding) and the Deep-JSCC baseline, LiTCom achieves SNR gains up to 8 dB and 2.5 dB, respectively, while reducing over 95% transmitter-side computations.

</details>


### [187] [Harmonic Analysis on Graphs via Isometric Group Embedding: A Canonical Fourier Transform, Shift, and Convolution for Network Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13338)
*Rigobert Fokam Souop,Laurent Bitjoka*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于群嵌入的图傅里叶变换，解决传统图信号处理中特征基非唯一、平移非等距、滤波非真卷积三个结构性问题。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文是纯图信号处理/图傅里叶分析的理论方法论文，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文为纯数学/信号处理理论工作，未涉及任何具体任务、场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何深度学习模型或神经网络架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文未使用任何遥感或目标检测数据集，评价指标为理论上的数值精度（machine precision），非检测指标。

Experiments: 仅在数值实验中验证了结构恒等式达到机器精度，以及在相同滤波协议下群特征基与拉普拉斯特征基的去噪效果等价。无目标检测实验。

Limitations: 论文本身是理论贡献，不涉及实际应用场景。与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的工程或部署信息。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于纯图信号处理理论，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交集。

Abstract: Graph signal processing built on the eigenvectors of a Laplacian or adjacency shift inherits three structural compromises: the eigenbasis is fixed only up to rotation within degenerate eigenspaces, the shift is not an isometry, and there is no genuine translation under which filtering is a true convolution. We develop an alternative harmonic analysis that removes all three at once. Given an isometric embedding of a connected graph into a Cayley graph of a finite abelian group, a host on which classical Fourier analysis applies exactly, we define a group-embedding graph Fourier transform from the host characters, lift graph signals to the host, and process them there. The characters supply a canonical orthonormal Fourier basis; the group translations form a family of unitary permutation operators obeying an exact group law; and filtering is genuine group convolution, for which the convolution theorem holds as a theorem rather than a definition and which possesses an identity element. We prove the Plancherel, convolution, translation-covariance, and sampling identities in the embedded setting, and compare the shift and convolution operators of the two frameworks side by side. Numerically, the structural identities hold to machine precision; under a same-filter protocol the groupcharacter basis denoises equivalently to the Laplacian eigenbasis once the host complement is filled by a smoothness-respecting extension. The contribution is exact, canonical structure, not a denoising advantage.

</details>


### [188] [Fine-Grained Open-Set Fault Diagnosis via Metric-Guided Time-Frequency Configuration Selection and Class-Specific Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13368)
*Youngjae Jeon,Dongjin Lee*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种结合度量引导的STFT配置选择与类别自编码器的开集故障诊断方法，用于旋转机械的细粒度故障严重程度识别。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是旋转机械故障诊断（rotating machinery fault diagnosis），属于工业机械领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等任何遥感相关场景，也不涉及目标检测任务。虽然使用了轻量化的配置选择策略（减少STFT候选配置的评估数量），但这不是模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等），而是数据预处理阶段的配置搜索加速，与在轨部署无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：开集故障诊断（open-set fault diagnosis, OSFD），细粒度严重程度诊断（fine-grained severity diagnosis）。应用场景：旋转机械工业系统。传感器类型：振动信号（非遥感图像）。目标类别：轴承故障严重程度等级（CWRU和PU数据集）。

Model architecture: 使用类别自编码器（class-specific autoencoder, CSAE）集合，每个已知故障类别训练一个独立的autoencoder来学习类别专属流形。推理时基于重构误差的类别亲和度（class affinity）进行已知类别识别，结合潜在空间维度级边界和类别专属重构误差的双重准则拒绝未知样本。

Lightweight method: 摘要中提到的"轻量化"体现在MGDC（metric-guided data-centric）方法通过Silhouette score筛选STFT配置，仅评估9/38（CWRU）和2/39（PU）的候选配置，降低了配置选择成本（factor of 5.69和29.87）。但这属于数据预处理阶段的配置搜索加速，不是模型层面的轻量化（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。该方法面向工业机械故障诊断，未涉及星上或边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Case Western Reserve University (CWRU) 轴承数据集、Paderborn University (PU) 轴承数据集。评价指标：H-score（开集识别综合指标）。

Experiments: 在CWRU上H-score达到0.9924，在PU上达到0.9509。MGDC在CWRU上仅评估9/38候选配置（降低5.69倍成本），在PU上仅评估2/39候选配置（降低29.87倍成本），即可找到与穷举搜索性能相当的配置。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴。2. 方法基于一维振动信号，不涉及图像/遥感图像处理。3. 未讨论模型参数量、计算量、推理速度等部署关键指标。4. 开集故障诊断与开集目标检测虽有概念相似性，但任务形式差异大。

Ideas for my research: 虽然论文与遥感在轨目标检测无关，但以下思路可能有间接启发：1. 开集识别（open-set recognition）思想可迁移到遥感目标检测中处理未知类别目标（如未见过的人造物或自然物体），但需要从一维信号扩展到二维图像检测。2. 类别专属自编码器（CSAE）的异常拒绝机制可类比于遥感检测中的异常目标过滤。3. 度量引导的配置搜索策略（MGDC）可类比于轻量化模型中的超参数搜索加速，但与本课题核心方向关联较弱。

Abstract: Reliable fault diagnosis of rotating machinery is essential for the safe and stable operation of industrial systems. Although deep learning methods perform well under closed-set conditions, real machinery may encounter previously unseen fault states. Existing open-set fault diagnosis (OSFD) methods remain limited in fine-grained severity diagnosis because they often rely on coarse type levels, heuristically selected Short-Time Fourier Transform (STFT) settings, and global class boundaries. We propose a fine-grained OSFD method that combines metric-guided data-centric (MGDC) STFT configuration selection with class-specific autoencoder (CSAE)-based anomaly rejection. MGDC screens candidate STFT configurations using the Silhouette score computed from spectrogram representations, identifying promising time-frequency representations before network training. The diagnostic model then uses a bank of CSAEs to learn compact class-specific manifolds for known degradation states. During inference, reconstruction-error-based class affinity identifies known classes, while a dual-criteria mechanism based on latent dimension-wise boundaries and class-specific reconstruction error rejects unknown samples. Experiments on the Case Western Reserve University (CWRU) and Paderborn University (PU) bearing datasets show that the proposed method achieves H-scores of 0.9924 and 0.9509 for fine-grained fault severity diagnosis. MGDC also identifies the best-performing configuration found by exhaustive search while evaluating only 9 of 38 candidates on CWRU and 2 of 39 candidates on PU, reducing the selection cost by factors of 5.69 and 29.87, respectively. These results indicate that the proposed method supports accurate open-set severity diagnosis with substantially lower configuration-selection cost.

</details>


### [189] [Parametric Diffraction-Based Object Sensing: Modeling, Estimation, and Fundamental Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13417)
*Jiaqi Xu,Bjorn Ottersten,A. Lee Swindlehurst*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于无线通信频率衍射机制的物理一致性参数化信道模型，用于环境障碍物感知，包括形状、距离和到达方向估计，并推导了Cramér-Rao下界作为性能极限。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于无线通信频段的衍射传感（diffraction-based sensing），属于通信感知一体化或电磁传感领域，完全不涉及遥感卫星、光学/SAR/高光谱图像、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：基于衍射的障碍物形状/距离/DoA估计；应用场景：无线通信环境感知；传感器类型：无线射频信号（非遥感卫星传感器）；目标类别：环境障碍物（blockage），非遥感典型目标（舰船/飞机/车辆等）。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明深度学习模型架构。提出的是物理参数化衍射信道模型（physics-consistent parameterized diffraction channel model），非神经网络模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩或加速方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、星上/边缘部署等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：未使用标准遥感/计算机视觉数据集，基于仿真信号进行数值实验；评价指标：最大似然估计性能、Cramér-Rao下界（CRB）、信噪比（SNR）。

Experiments: 数值实验表明ML估计器在中高SNR下接近CRB，验证了衍射建模在高保真障碍物表征中的有效性。无与目标检测方法的对比实验。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何深度学习模型；3. 未讨论轻量化或部署问题；4. 仅基于仿真验证，无真实遥感数据实验。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能的间接启发是：物理模型（如衍射模型）与数据驱动方法结合可能提升遥感目标检测的鲁棒性，但本文未朝此方向展开。

Abstract: This paper proposes a rigorous framework for sensing of environmental objects using diffraction mechanisms prevalent at wireless communication frequencies. Specifically, we develop a physics-consistent parameterized diffraction channel model, derive maximum likelihood (ML) approaches for estimating the blockage shape, range, and source directions of arrival (DoAs), and quantify fundamental performance limits via the Cramér--Rao bound (CRB). In our physics-based modeling, we integrate various approximations for the wave propagation (far-field, paraxial Fresnel, and exact near-field regimes), enabling a wide range of applicability. The underlying model is frequency-agnostic, and we derive Fresnel-number scaling laws that map the diffraction pattern, and hence the estimation problem, across carrier frequency, object size, and range. We quantify the maximum likelihood estimation performance and its relationship to the CRB, and we study the impact of the modeling approximations developed in this work. Numerical results demonstrate that ML estimators closely approach the CRB at moderate to high signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), and highlight the utility of diffraction-based modeling for high-fidelity blockage characterization.

</details>


### [190] [Compositional Zero-Shot Recognition based on Tangent Space Disentanglement for Composite Modulation Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13463)
*Yurui Zhao,Xiang Wang,Zhitao Huang,Baoguo Li*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于解耦语义空间和零样本学习的切线空间解耦网络（TSDN），用于复合调制识别（ACMR），解决内层/外层调制语义耦合和未知调制方案识别问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦通信领域的复合调制识别（ACMR），属于集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统，不涉及遥感图像、卫星对地观测、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无关联，不建议作为本课题的参考文献。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：自动复合调制识别（ACMR）；场景：集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统；传感器类型：通信信号接收机；目标类别：内层/外层复合调制类型。不涉及遥感、卫星、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等。

Model architecture: Tangent Space Disentanglement Network (TSDN)，包含对数映射（logarithmic mapping）、空间变换网络（Spatial Transformer Network）用于学习几何变换、多目标损失函数。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：摘要未说明具体数据集名称；评价指标：零样本识别准确率（zero-shot recognition accuracy），在4 dB SNR下保持鲁棒性能。

Experiments: TSDN达到93%以上零样本识别准确率，显著优于统一语义和多任务基线方法，在联合信道衰落和硬件非理想条件下（低至4 dB SNR）保持鲁棒。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 未提供模型复杂度、推理速度等部署指标；3. 仅针对通信调制识别，场景高度专一。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。解耦语义空间（disentangled semantic space）和零样本学习（zero-shot learning）的思想在遥感目标检测中处理未知类别或域适应问题时可能有理论启发，但与在轨轻量化部署的核心需求（小模型、低FLOPs、实时推理）无直接关联。

Abstract: Automatic composite modulation recognition (ACMR) is critical for integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems, while conventional approaches face significant challenges due to the semantic coupling between inner-layer and outer-layer modulations in composite modulation (CM), degraded performance under joint hardware and channel imperfections, and limited capability to handle unknown modulation schemes. To this end, we design a disentangled semantic space and propose zero-shot learning framework. Within this framework, a logarithmic projection first linearizes the multiplicative coupling between modulation layers and a learnable geometric transformation is used for layer-wise semantic features. We instantiate the framework as the Tangent Space Disentanglement Network (TSDN). TSDN integrates logarithmic mapping, a spatial transformer network for learning the geometric transformation, and a multi-objective loss function that balances discrimination with cross-domain generalization. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that TSDN achieves over 93\% zero-shot recognition accuracy, outperforms unified-semantic and multi-task baselines by significant margins, and maintains robust performance under combined channel fading and hardware imperfections down to 4 dB SNR.

</details>


### [191] [Posterior-Confidence Driven Beamforming for Energy-Efficient Integrated Sensing and Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13470)
*Nusaibah A. Alshorman,Huseyin Arslan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种面向6G ISAC系统的能效优化MIMO DFRC波束赋形框架，通过EKF驱动的选择性感知策略（skip-enabled sensing）降低发射功率，同时保证通信SINR和多目标跟踪可靠性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦6G通信感知一体化（ISAC）系统的能效波束赋形，属于通信/雷达信号处理领域，不涉及遥感图像、卫星在轨目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然涉及"目标跟踪"，但这是雷达多目标跟踪（multi-target tracking），而非遥感图像中的目标检测（object detection in remote sensing imagery）。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：MIMO DFRC波束赋形、通信感知一体化能效优化。应用场景：6G ISAC系统。传感器类型：MIMO雷达/通信双功能系统。目标类别：多目标跟踪中的雷达目标。与遥感无关。

Model architecture: 基于扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）的感知触发策略，结合后验置信度指标和归一化创新平方（NIS）两个统计量，决定何时激活感知。波束赋形采用sector-based beampattern约束加非零安全照明下限。

Lightweight method: 不涉及模型轻量化。论文的"能效优化"是通过感知调度策略（选择性跳过感知）降低发射功率，而非通过剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署指标。论文讨论的是通信发射功率优化，而非模型推理效率。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标：发射功率（transmit power）、用户SINR、多目标跟踪可靠性。

Experiments: 数值结果表明所提框架相比其他基线显著降低了发射功率，且不损害通信性能或对感知过程产生过大影响。具体对比方法和消融实验摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向所需的任何信息。摘要未讨论模型推理效率、边缘部署、遥感图像处理等内容。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能的启发是"选择性感知"（skip-enabled sensing）的思想——在遥感在轨场景中，是否也可以设计一种基于置信度的自适应推理策略，只在必要时触发高精度检测模型，从而节省星上计算资源？但这与本论文的雷达感知调度场景差异较大。

Abstract: Energy efficiency will pose an essential limitation for sixth-generation (6G) integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems, given the high sensing power consumption associated with persistent sensing, despite stable communication requirements. This paper proposes an energy-efficient multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) dual-functional radar-communication (DFRC) beamforming framework that minimizes transmit power while guaranteeing per-user signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) and reliable multi-target tracking. The key innovation is a tracking-aware, skip-enabled sensing policy that departs from the conventional always-on probing paradigm. Instead of enforcing sensing at every epoch, sensing is selectively triggered according to two complementary statistics derived from an extended Kalman filter (EKF): a posterior confidence metric and the normalized innovation squared (NIS). While the former ensures accurate estimation, the latter guarantees reliable measurements, and thus sensing can only be activated when additional information is required. To ensure robustness under intermittent sensing, sector-based beampattern constraints are combined with a nonzero safety illumination floor imposed to guarantee reliable target tracking when skipping occurs. Numerical results show that the proposed framework achieves a significant reduction in transmit power compared to other baselines, without any deterioration in the communication system's performance or excessive impact on the sensing process.

</details>


### [192] [Pinching-Antenna Systems (PASS)-Based User-Side Navigation: An Anchor-Line-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13485)
*Zongyi Li,Jun Wang,Tianwei Hou,Anna Li*

Abstract: Pinching-antenna systems (PASS) are capable of dynamically reconfiguring wireless channels by flexibly repositioning pinching antennas (PAs) along the waveguides to establish short-range line-of-sight links. In this paper, a user-side navigation framework for PASS is proposed, where mobile users determine their own positions using only downlink broadcast signals without any prior knowledge of the PA positions. First, a Lambert W function-based PA positioning and pseudorange estimation (LWF-PAP) algorithm is developed, in which the closed-form expressions for both the PA positions along the waveguide and the PA-user pseudoranges are derived. Second, a weighted least squares-based PASS navigation (WLS-PAN) algorithm is formulated, where the nonlinear PASS-based navigation equations are transformed into a closed-form linear system, and the optimal weight matrix is derived, achieving minimum-variance unbiased estimation. Third, the PA-derived position dilution of precision (PA-PDOP) metric is further defined to characterize the theoretical accuracy bound. Simulation results demonstrate that centimeter-level positioning accuracy is achieved for both PAs and users within the breakpoint distance. It is also shown that uniform PA deployment and a moderate increase in the number of PAs effectively improve navigation accuracy, thereby validating the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed framework for distributed real-time user-side self-navigation.

</details>

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。摘要未说明算法计算复杂度、实时性等对部署有意义的信息。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于无线通信信道估计领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: The terahertz (THz) frequency band offers the potential for ultra-high data rate transmission in future wireless communication systems. To extend the transmission distance and enhance spectral efficiency, the deployment of large-scale antenna arrays emerges as a promising solution in the THz band. This paper targets the critical challenge of cross-field (hybrid near-field/far-field) channel parameter estimation and channel characterization in such configurations. We first establish a 260-380 GHz virtual uniform linear array (ULA) measurement framework in an indoor scenario, capturing high-resolution channel transfer functions (CTFs) that reveal spatial non-stationarity and cross-field wavefront characteristics. Building upon these empirical observations, we propose a cross-field space-alternating generalized expectation-maximization (SAGE) algorithm that discriminatively estimates near-field and far-field multipath components (MPCs) via Bayesian phase-curvature classification, while explicitly tracking spatial birth-death phenomena through visibility region estimation. Analysis of the measurement data validates the algorithm's effectiveness in resolving cross-field MPCs and quantifies that near-field MPCs account for over 90% of total MPCs at 2 m transmission distance (380 GHz). We observe that spatial non-stationarity intensifies as the carrier frequency increases and the transmission distance decreases. These findings offer quantitative guidelines for channel modeling and system design in wireless THz communication systems.

</details>


### [194] [On the Performance of Pinching-Antenna Systems (PASS) Under Dynamic Channels with Blockages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13581)
*Jinhua Wang,Jun Wang,Tianwei Hou,Anna Li,Yuanwei Liu,Arumugam Nallanathan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究pinching-antenna系统在障碍物遮挡下的信道建模与性能分析，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。论文研究的是pinching-antenna通信系统的信道建模与遮挡性能分析，属于无线通信物理层领域。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化、在轨部署等任何相关主题。

Abstract: The performance of pinching-antenna systems (PASS) is fundamentally affected by line-of-sight (LoS) blockage in practical environments. In this paper, PASS is investigated under realistic, obstacle-induced blockage by jointly considering the LoS and non-LoS (NLoS) components, rather than relying on a LoS channel or a probabilistic blockage model. A geometry-aware blockage model is adopted, where a blockage region on the waveguide is defined according to the actual locations and geometric features of obstacles, such that a pinching-antenna (PA) located within the blockage region is unable to establish a LoS link to the user equipment (UE). The channel models of PASS are developed by jointly accounting for in-waveguide attenuation and spatial propagation loss. To quantify the impact of channel factors on PASS performance, a single-PA single-UE scenario is studied under Rayleigh and Rician fading channels. Closed-form expressions for the outage probability are derived for both cases. For the ergodic rate, a closed-form expression is obtained in the Rayleigh case, while a complete analytical expression and an approximate closed-form expression are derived in the Rician case. Analytical expressions are derived for the endpoints of the blockage region, and the deployment criteria of optimal PA are provided. Simulation results validate the analysis and reveal that: i) NLoS scattering has a twofold effect on PASS performance, potentially degrading the outage performance while improving the rate performance under Rician fading; ii) Sufficiently strong NLoS scattering can still sustain communication in the presence of LoS blockage; iii) The optimal PA position is jointly determined by the environment geometry and the interplay between spatial propagation loss and in-waveguide attenuation.

</details>


### [195] [On the Blockage Effect in Pinching-Antenna Systems (PASS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13582)
*Jinhua Wang,Jun Wang,Tianwei Hou,Xin Sun,Arumugam Nallanathan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究可重构天线系统(PASS)在随机视距遮挡下的通信性能，基于随机几何建模推导中断概率和遍历速率的闭式表达式。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是无线通信中的天线系统性能分析，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文聚焦于通信系统性能建模，非深度学习模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何部署相关指标（参数量、FLOPs、延迟、能耗等）。

Datasets and metrics: 使用随机几何建模（PPP过程）进行仿真，未使用遥感或目标检测数据集。评价指标为中断概率(outage probability)和遍历速率(ergodic rate)。

Experiments: 仿真结果表明：障碍物密度越大或障碍物统计尺寸越大，LoS遮挡概率越高，系统性能下降；PASS系统在LoS遮挡下始终优于传统天线系统。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于无线通信领域，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: Pinching-antenna systems (PASS) offer considerable potential for wireless communications due to their unique ability to dynamically reconfigure radiation positions along a waveguide. However, the performance of PASS remains a critical challenge in the presence of random line-of-sight (LoS) blockage, leading to significant attenuation and even communication outages. In this paper, the performance of PASS in the presence of LoS blockage is investigated from the perspective of stochastic geometry. Obstacles are modeled through a homogeneous Poisson point process (PPP), where the geometric dimensions, numbers, and positions are treated as random variables. To conduct a concrete characterization of LoS blockage, the random-height-and-random-radius (RHRR) blockage model and the deterministic-height-and-deterministic-radius (DHDR) blockage model are proposed. In particular, closed-form analytical and asymptotic expressions for the outage probability are obtained, along with analytical and approximate expressions for the ergodic rate. Our simulation results reveal that denser obstacle environments or statistically larger obstacles substantially increase the probability of LoS blockage and degrade the system performance. Moreover, owing to its ability to dynamically reposition PAs, PASS can consistently outperform conventional antenna systems in the presence of LoS blockage.

</details>


### [196] [RIS Beamforming under Element-Level Variations: Statistical Characterization and Robust Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13583)
*Dimitris Kompostiotis,Dimitris Vordonis,Vassilis Paliouras,George C. Alexandropoulos*

TL;DR: 提出一种分析可重构智能表面(RIS)单元级变化对辐射特性影响的统计模型与低复杂度优化方法。

Abstract: In this paper, a novel analytical framework to characterize the impact of element-level variations on the radiation characteristics of reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RISs) is introduced. Specifically, a statistical model is proposed to capture the effects of varactor capacitance fluctuations on the RIS reflection coefficients, and, subsequently, on the resulting power radiation pattern; both low- and large-variance independent perturbation scenarios, are investigated. Leveraging the proposed statistical model, a low complexity greedy optimization methodology is presented, having the goal to optimize the expected RIS radiation power, thereby, generating inherently robust configurations. Furthermore, the analytical proposed model serves as an efficient alternative to computationally expensive Monte Carlo simulations, enabling the quantification of element sensitivity to manufacturing and operational tolerances. As demonstrated, optimizing the mean power pattern significantly enhances system performance under element-level variations. For typical RIS sizes (e.g., 32x32 or 64x64), a main lobe gain exceeding 2 dB and a sidelobe suppression of approximately 10 dB are achieved.

</details>

*Pengxuan Gao,Kai Ying,Botao Wu,Jianhua Mo,Qingsong Wen*

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：LAMBDA dataset；评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标名称。

Experiments: 在LAMBDA数据集上，M3F-UAV优于单模态基线方法，且在模态缺失场景下保持鲁棒性能。未提供与其他多模态方法的详细对比。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未提供模型复杂度（参数量、FLOPs）信息；3. 未说明具体评价指标；4. 仅在一个数据集上验证；5. 轻量化仅体现在"轻量任务头"，整体模型可能较重。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。可参考的点：1）跨模态融合和缺失模态处理的思想，若未来研究多模态遥感（光学+SAR+高光谱）融合检测时可能有启发；2）特征级掩码重建预训练策略可用于遥感多模态自监督学习。但整体方向差异大，不建议深入。

Abstract: Low-altitude unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) are emerging as key platforms for wireless intelligence tasks. However, practical low-altitude wireless systems usually operate in complex urban environments, where visual occlusion, sparse geometric observations, multipath propagation, and sensor failures may degrade the reliability of single-modality models. To address these challenges, this paper proposes M3F-UAV, a missing-modality multimodal foundation model for low-altitude wireless sensing. The proposed framework learns a unified multimodal representation from visual, geometric, and wireless observations. Specifically, modality-specific pretrained feature extractors are adopted for RGB/depth images, LiDAR point clouds, and CSI matrices, respectively. Through cross-modal fusion and missing-modality-aware pretraining with feature-level masked reconstruction and UAV localization objectives, M3F-UAV can extract fixed-size features from different modality combinations and adapt them to downstream low-altitude wireless tasks with lightweight task heads. Experiments on the LAMBDA dataset show that M3F-UAV outperforms single-modality baselines and maintains robust performance under missing-modality settings.

</details>


### [198] [From Metric to Mechanism: Designing Wireless Resilience through Finite Blocklength Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13710)
*Kevin Weinberger,Aydin Sezgin,Mehdi Bennis*

TL;DR: 提出面向URLLC场景的跨层网络弹性框架，通过有限块长通信和时域资源优化实现网络恢复，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是无线通信网络的弹性与可靠性（URLLC场景），涉及有限块长通信、波束赋形、RIS相位优化等通信物理层技术。与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化均无任何关联。既不是遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测，更不涉及轻量化或星上部署。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Lightweight method: 不涉及。摘要未提及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络、实时推理、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何轻量化或模型压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 提出可解释的弹性度量（吸收损失、适应效率、恢复行为），数值实验基于信道中断和AI驱动的流量激增场景。不涉及遥感或目标检测数据集。

Experiments: 数值结果表明在重复信道中断和AI驱动流量激增下具有强弹性性能。无目标检测相关实验。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。摘要未说明与遥感或目标检测相关的局限性。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无任何可借鉴点。建议忽略此论文，寻找遥感目标检测或模型轻量化相关文献。

Abstract: Next-generation wireless networks must maintain reliable operation under abrupt and severe disruptions, particularly in ultra-reliable low-latency communication (URLLC) scenarios where strict time constraints dominate system design. This work addresses network resilience from a time-centric perspective by explicitly integrating finite blocklength (FBL) communication, thereby exposing transmission duration as a controllable resource for system recovery. To this end, we propose a unified cross-layer framework that jointly couples queue dynamics, rate adaptation, and blocklength optimization, enabling the system to actively absorb, adapt to, and recover from diverse resilience events.
  To systematically evaluate these mechanisms, we introduce an interpretable resilience metric that decomposes disruption impact into absorption loss, adaptation efficiency, and recovery behavior, enabling a direct and intuitive assessment of system resilience. Building on this framework, we develop a three-stage alternating optimization approach that jointly optimizes PHY-layer parameters, including beamforming, reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS) phase shifts, and blocklength, revealing the importance of time-aware resource allocation in the FBL regime. Numerical results demonstrate strong resilience performance under repeated channel disruptions and AI-driven traffic surges, highlighting the effectiveness of cross-layer resource adaptation. Finally, the proposed resilience metric enables an intuitive and consistent comparison of resilience performance across different approaches and disruption types, while revealing their respective strengths and limitations.

</details>


### [199] [Design and Analysis of a Higher-Order Enhanced Phase-Locked Loop via the Ahmadi-Chaudhry-Zhang Newton Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13752)
*Shafayat Abrar*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于ACZ高阶牛顿框架的增强型锁相环(EPLL)，用于电力系统中正弦电压的幅值、相位和频率估计。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是电力系统中的锁相环(EPLL)算法，属于电力电子/控制领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Model architecture: 基于ACZ高阶牛顿框架的连续时间优化流，应用于EPLL的相位更新，幅值更新保持经典形式。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文未提及任何遥感或视觉数据集，评价指标为相位图分析（phase-portrait analysis），非目标检测指标。

Experiments: 通过相位图分析，与标准EPLL、Newton EPLL、modified EPLL进行对比，证明所提方法消除了虚假平衡点和鞍点，扩大了吸引域。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的视觉或轻量化方法。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于电力系统控制领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: The enhanced phase-locked loop (EPLL) is widely used in power systems to estimate the amplitude, phase, and frequency of sinusoidal voltages. Existing EPLL formulations are primarily derived from first- or second-order optimization methods, which may exhibit slow convergence, saddle-point attraction, or undesired oscillatory behavior. This paper presents a new higher-order EPLL based on the recently proposed Ahmadi-Chaudhry-Zhang (ACZ) higher-order Newton framework. Since the ACZ method was originally developed for scalar discrete-time optimization, a continuous-time higher-order Newton flow is formulated for adaptive systems. The resulting framework is then applied to the EPLL through a coordinate optimization strategy, whereby the higher-order Newton flow is applied only to the phase update, while the amplitude update retains its classical form because its cost function is exactly quadratic.
  The proposed autonomous system is analyzed through phase portraits and compared with the standard, Newton, and modified EPLL formulations. Phase-portrait analysis of the autonomous model shows that the proposed flow eliminates the spurious equilibria and saddle points present in the autonomous Newton EPLL, substantially enlarges the basin of attraction associated with the desired equilibrium, and shares several desirable convergence characteristics with the modified EPLL.

</details>


### [200] [Hybrid Time-Frequency Domain Frequency Offset Compensation Under GHz Doppler Shift for LEO Satellite-to-Ground Coherent Free-Space Optical Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13904)
*Tiankuo Jiao,Hossein Kazemi,Harald Haas*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种面向LEO卫星相干自由空间光通信链路的Doppler感知混合时频频率偏移补偿(HTF-FOC)接收机架构，用于解决多GHz级动态Doppler频移的捕获与跟踪问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文涉及LEO卫星下行链路中的相干光通信(FSO)频率偏移补偿问题，属于卫星通信物理层信号处理领域，完全不涉及遥感成像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上智能处理。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：LEO卫星对地相干光通信链路的载波频率偏移(CFO)补偿；场景：卫星通信物理层；传感器类型：相干光通信接收机；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: HTF-FOC接收机架构，包含：4次幂FFT粗CFO捕获、残差CFO交接验证、低复杂度决策导向(DD)锁频环(FLL)跟踪。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。论文中提到的"低复杂度决策导向锁频环"属于接收机信号处理复杂度优化，与模型剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等深度学习模型轻量化方法无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署指标。论文关注的是通信接收机架构的Doppler跟踪能力（±5 GHz范围，残差<80 MHz），而非深度学习模型的星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 使用Monte Carlo仿真验证；评价指标：成对错误概率(PEP)、联合界符号错误率(SER)、残差CFO、捕获成功率(100%)。

Experiments: 通过Monte Carlo仿真验证了所提方法在LEO典型轨道高度400-800 km、轨道速度7.3-7.9 km/s条件下，可跟踪±5 GHz以上Doppler频移，残差CFO保持在80 MHz以下，成功率为100%。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，不存在该方向上的局限性可讨论。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于卫星通信物理层信号处理，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: Coherent free-space optical (FSO) communication is a promising solution for low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite downlink transmission. However, high orbital velocity introduces multi-GHz Doppler shifts that appear as a rapidly time-varying carrier frequency offset (CFO), which is a major challenge for conventional coherent optical receivers. Narrow-range digital loops cannot acquire the initial offset, while wide-range feedforward or optical domain solutions either leave large residual errors or impose substantial implementation cost. In this paper, a Doppler-aware hybrid time-frequency frequency offset compensation (HTF-FOC) receiver architecture is proposed for coherent LEO satellite-to-ground FSO links using a cumulative time-varying random process model for the dynamic Doppler-induced phase shift. The proposed receiver implements a hybrid acquisition and tracking procedure to acquire and compensate for multi-GHz Doppler variations, including 4th-power FFT-based coarse CFO acquisition, residual CFO handover verification, and low-complexity decision-directed (DD) frequency-locked loop (FLL) tracking. The phase-averaged pairwise error probability (PEP) and union-bound symbol error rate (SER) expressions are derived and verified using Monte Carlo simulations. The results demonstrate that the proposed HTF-FOC method tracks Doppler shifts beyond $\pm5$ GHz while keeping the residual CFO below $80$ MHz with a success rate of $100\%$ for typical LEO altitudes of $400{\!-\!}800$ km and orbital speeds of $7.3{\!-\!}7.9$ km/s.

</details>


### [201] [Fast Cascaded Recursive Filtering via a Block-Matrix Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14054)
*Haotian Zhai,Bernd-Peter Paris*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种将递归IIR滤波器（双二阶节级联）转化为块三对角线性系统，并通过部分LU分解和循环归约实现并行加速的算法，在Intel CPU上获得最高10倍加速。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。它研究的是数字信号处理中的递归IIR滤波器并行化算法，属于底层信号处理/计算架构方向，不涉及任何视觉任务或模型压缩技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何遥感/视觉任务或场景。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及神经网络模型结构，而是针对IIR数字滤波器（双二阶节级联）的递归计算结构进行并行化改造。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等模型轻量化方法。虽然提到了"coefficient quantization"（系数量化），但这是指滤波器系数的定点量化鲁棒性，而非神经网络量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文在Intel CPU（支持AVX2 SIMD）上验证，未涉及星上/边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等。但循环归约将顺序依赖深度从O(N)降到O(log₂N)的思路，对在轨处理中的某些信号预处理环节可能有间接参考价值。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。无视觉数据集。评价指标为时钟周期/样本（clock cycles per sample）和吞吐率（MS/s），在Intel Meteor Lake等架构上测量。

Experiments: 在三种Intel微架构（支持AVX2）上进行了cycle-accurate测量。16阶系统上，多块算法相比标量滤波最高加速10倍；单Meteor Lake核心上循环归约达约618 MS/s，比scipy.signal.sosfilt吞吐率提升8倍。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。论文聚焦于信号处理领域的IIR滤波器并行化，不涉及任何视觉模型或深度学习内容。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。唯一可能的间接启发是：循环归约（cyclic reduction）将递归依赖的串行计算转化为并行计算的思想，如果未来在轨处理中涉及递归神经网络（如RNN/LSTM）或时序信号处理，可能有参考价值。但对当前"遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接借鉴意义非常有限。

Abstract: Recursive (IIR) filters realized as cascaded second-order sections (biquads) offer both design generality and robustness against coefficient quantization. However, their inherent sample-to-sample feedback dependency poses a fundamental obstacle to parallel computation. This paper reformulates the biquad difference equation as a banded block-Toeplitz linear system and introduces a stride-$N$ permutation that maps a group of $NL$ samples into a block-tridiagonal structure whose entries are scalar multiples of identity and shift matrices. Within this framework, two parallel algorithms are developed for the recursive solution: a partial LU (PH) factorization that preserves the sparse block structure and a cyclic reduction that is applied to recursive filtering, to the best of our knowledge, for the first time. It reduces the sequential dependency depth from $\mathcal{O}(N)$ to $\mathcal{O}(\log_2 N)$. For a cascade of $K$ biquads, the intermediate permutations between successive sections cancel exactly, so that only a single permutation/de-permutation pair is required for the entire cascade, eliminating $2(K{-}1)$ redundant stages. Exact block-level operation counts are derived for every algorithmic stage and validated against cycle-accurate measurements on three Intel micro-architectures supporting AVX2 SIMD instructions. Experimental results for a 16th-order system show that the proposed multi-block algorithms reduce clock cycles per sample by up to $10\times$ compared to scalar filtering, with both algorithms scaling favorably on newer architectures. On a single Meteor Lake core, cyclic reduction achieves approximately 618 MS/s -- an $8\times$ throughput improvement over scipy.signal.sosfilt.

</details>

### [202] [Automatic Differentiation from Scratch: How PyTorch Computes Gradients in Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13042)
*Abdeladhim Tahimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过数值追踪的方式，详细解释了PyTorch自动微分引擎如何计算物理信息神经网络（PINN）训练中的梯度，包括物理导数计算和参数梯度反向传播的完整流程。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于PyTorch自动微分引擎在PINN训练中的梯度计算机制，属于科学计算/微分方程求解领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文任务为PINN训练中的自动微分梯度追踪，应用场景为科学计算/物理信息神经网络，不涉及遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 1-3-3-1多层感知机（MLP），用于求解常微分方程初值问题。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。使用解析解已知的常微分方程初值问题作为验证案例，不涉及遥感数据集或标准目标检测评价指标。

Experiments: 通过数值追踪验证了PyTorch autograd引擎在PINN训练中计算22个参数梯度的完整流程，并将每个伴随值（adjoint value）与Tahimi (2026)的手工推导结果进行了对比验证。

Limitations: 论文仅针对一个极简的1-3-3-1 MLP和一个简单的线性ODE案例，未涉及高维问题、复杂网络结构或实际遥感应用场景。与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向无任何交集。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能的间接联系是：如果未来在轨部署中需要将物理约束（如卫星轨道动力学）嵌入神经网络训练，PINN的自动微分机制可能有参考价值，但这与本课题的核心目标（检测模型轻量化）相距甚远。

Abstract: This paper traces, with explicit numerical values, how PyTorch's automatic differentiation (AD) engine computes gradients for Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) training -- a setting that requires two levels of differentiation: computing the physics derivative $\hat{y}'(t)=d\hat{y}/dt$ through the network, and computing parameter gradients $\nabla_θL$ of a loss that itself depends on $\hat{y}'(t)$. Using a 1-3-3-1 multilayer perceptron and the initial value problem $y'(t)+y(t)=0$, $y(0)=1$, we trace the complete pipeline at every node: the computational graph built during the forward pass, the reverse-mode backward traversal that computes all 22 parameter gradients in a single pass, and the graph-on-graph mechanism by which \texttt{create\_graph=True} enables correct differentiation through the physics-informed residual. Every adjoint value is verified against the hand derivations of Tahimi (2026), connecting the $P/Q$ sensitivity framework to the vector--Jacobian products used by PyTorch's autograd engine.

</details>


### [203] [Beyond Backbone Backpropagation: A Decoupled Strategy for Efficient Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13043)
*Daniel Vila-Cruz,Laura Morán-Fernández,Verónica Bolón-Canedo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量训练策略，通过解耦特征提取与分类器优化、适配归一化层并重新设计带margin加权损失的分类头，在多个CNN和Transformer架构上大幅减少训练时间和碳排放，但面向医学图像分类任务。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文面向医学图像分类（Brain Cancer MRI, BreakHis, PatchCamelyon），不涉及遥感/卫星/地球观测场景，不涉及目标检测任务，也不涉及模型在轨部署或边缘推理。虽然涉及轻量训练策略（减少训练开销和碳排放），但这是训练阶段的效率优化，而非推理阶段的模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏等），且应用场景与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非对训练阶段碳排放优化有特殊兴趣，否则对课题帮助极小。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：图像分类；应用场景：医学诊断（临床或原型环境）；传感器类型：MRI、组织病理学图像；目标类别：摘要未说明具体类别（Brain Cancer MRI为脑癌分类，BreakHis为乳腺癌组织病理，PatchCamelyon为淋巴结转移检测）。

Model architecture: CNN架构：ResNet18, ResNet50, MobileNet, DenseNet121；Transformer架构：ViT, Swin, DeiT。核心设计：解耦特征提取与分类器优化（precomputing features only once），重新设计的分类器头（margin-based weighted loss），适配归一化层到新域。

Lightweight method: 本文的"轻量"指训练阶段的轻量化策略（减少训练计算开销和碳排放），而非推理阶段的模型轻量化。具体方法：1) 解耦特征提取与分类器优化，特征仅预计算一次；2) 适配归一化层到目标域；3) 重新设计分类器头（margin-based weighted loss），避免端到端反向传播。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等推理轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明推理阶段的参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅报告了训练时间减少和CO2排放降低（orders of magnitude），但这些是训练效率指标，与星上/边缘推理部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Brain Cancer MRI（脑癌MRI）、BreakHis（乳腺癌组织病理）、PatchCamelyon（淋巴结转移）；评价指标：分类准确率（accuracy）、训练时间、CO2排放量。

Experiments: 在4种CNN架构和3种Transformer架构上评估，在3个医学数据集上验证。方法显著减少训练时间，仅带来微小的准确率损失，多数情况下匹配或超越基线性能。CO2排放量降低数个数量级。

Limitations: 1) 仅针对图像分类任务，不涉及目标检测；2) 仅在医学图像上验证，未在遥感/卫星图像上测试；3) 轻量策略针对训练阶段，不解决推理阶段的模型压缩和加速问题；4) 未报告模型参数量、FLOPs、推理延迟等部署关键指标；5) 未讨论在边缘/星上硬件（FPGA, NPU, TensorRT）上的适配性。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限，因为：1) 本文解决的是训练效率问题而非推理效率问题，而"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"的核心是推理阶段的模型压缩与加速；2) 任务类型不同（分类 vs. 检测），场景不同（医学 vs. 遥感）。潜在可借鉴点：margin-based weighted loss的设计思路或许可迁移到遥感小目标检测的损失函数设计中，但需要大幅改造。

Abstract: Deep learning models achieve state-of-the-art image classification but face deployment challenges due to computational costs and energy demands. We propose a lightweight training strategy that adapts normalization layers of the model to the new domain and decouples feature extraction from classifier optimization, reducing overhead by precomputing features only once. A redesigned classifier head with margin-based weighted loss further minimizes ambiguity without end-to-end backpropagation. Evaluated across four CNN architectures (ResNet18, ResNet50, MobileNet, DenseNet121), three Transformer models (ViT, Swin and DeiT) and three medical datasets (Brain Cancer MRI, BreakHis and PatchCamelyon), our approach significantly reduces the required training time with only a marginal accuracy trade-off, often matching or surpassing baseline performance. This efficiency translates to reducing CO2 by orders of magnitude, offering a practical and environmentally sustainable solution for resource-constrained clinical or prototyping environments.

</details>


### [204] [Federated Explainable Artificial Intelligence: Roles, Architectures, Evaluation, and Open Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13045)
*Masoume Gholizade,Fabrizio Ruffini,Pietro Ducange,Francesco Marcelloni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一篇关于联邦可解释人工智能（FedXAI）的综述论文，系统梳理了联邦学习与可解释AI交叉领域的方法、分类体系和挑战。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是联邦学习（FL）与可解释AI（XAI）的交叉综述，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然联邦学习在遥感领域有应用，但本摘要未提及任何遥感场景、目标检测任务或轻量化技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文为综述性质，不针对特定任务或场景。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。综述涵盖模型无关解释方法、可解释联邦模型、解释感知聚合机制等。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。仅提及"communication-efficient XAI"作为未来挑战，但未给出具体方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何部署相关信息，无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。指出缺乏标准化基准和评估指标用于衡量解释质量、稳定性、隐私泄露和计算开销。

Experiments: 综述论文，无实验对比或消融实验。

Limitations: 摘要明确指出关键挑战包括：非IID数据下的可解释性、解释中心的安全威胁、通信高效的XAI、持续FedXAI、领域知识与监管约束的整合。缺乏标准化基准和评估指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。如果课题涉及联邦学习场景下的遥感目标检测，可参考其关于通信效率（communication-efficient XAI）的讨论，但摘要未给出具体技术方案。对于"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"这一核心课题，本文无直接可借鉴点。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) has emerged as a key paradigm for privacy-preserving collaborative model training across distributed and heterogeneous data sources. By keeping raw data local, FL addresses data confidentiality concerns, yet it does not resolve the opacity of modern machine learning models. In parallel, Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) has gained attention for improving transparency, trust, and accountability, particularly in high-stakes domains. Their intersection has given rise to Federated Explainable Artificial Intelligence (FedXAI) paradigm, which aims to jointly satisfy privacy and explainability requirements. This survey provides a systematic review of FedXAI, highlighting the transition of explainability from a post-hoc tool to an integral component of the FL lifecycle. We show how explainability supports aggregation, personalization, robustness, coordination, and system-level decision making. To organize the literature, we introduce a taxonomy that classifies FedXAI methods by the role of explainability, model and explainer types, explanation scope, integration level, FL settings, and data heterogeneity. We review approaches ranging from model-agnostic explanations to interpretable federated models and explainability-aware aggregation mechanisms. We also examine evaluation practices and discuss the lack of standardized benchmarks and metrics for measuring explanation quality, stability, privacy leakage, and computational overhead. Finally, we identify key challenges, including explainability under non-IID data, explanation-centric security threats, communication-efficient XAI, continual FedXAI, and the integration of domain knowledge and regulatory constraints. By consolidating existing work and identifying key gaps, this survey serves as a reference framework for designing trustworthy, transparent, and privacy-preserving federated AI systems.

</details>


### [205] [What Your Model Threw Away and Why You'll Want It Back: Masking, Fingerprinting, and Privacy from Discarded Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13046)
*Zachary P. Bradshaw*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个分析机器学习模型对李群作用输入信息丢弃的数学框架，定义"零纤维"和"稳定子群"来量化模型对对称性的不敏感性，并在分子性质预测和球面图像分类上验证。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文核心是李群对称性分析的理论框架，虽然实验涉及球面图像分类（spherical image classification），但并非遥感/卫星场景，不涉及目标检测，也不涉及模型轻量化或星上部署。球面图像分类与遥感图像分类有本质区别，且论文重点在理论分析而非工程部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：对称性分析、模型信息丢弃量化。应用场景：分子性质预测（SO(3)群）、球面图像分类（PSL(2,C)群）。传感器类型：摘要未说明。目标类别：摘要未说明。不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。框架适用于经典神经网络和变分量子电路（variational quantum circuits），但未给出具体网络结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。仅提到Newton迭代计算零纤维元素的成本"comparable to a few gradient evaluations"。

Datasets and metrics: 实验任务：分子性质预测（SO(3)群）、球面图像分类（PSL(2,C)群）。具体数据集名称和评价指标摘要未说明。

Experiments: 实验验证了框架在数据掩码（data masking）、模型指纹识别（model fingerprinting）、隐私保护计算（privacy-preserving computation）上的应用。未与目标检测方法对比，无消融实验。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 理论性强，工程实用性低；3. 仅在分子和球面图像上验证，未在遥感数据上测试；4. 未讨论计算效率与部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能的间接启发：该框架可用于分析遥感目标检测模型对旋转/平移等对称性的敏感程度，但需要大量适配工作，且与轻量化目标无关。

Abstract: We develop a framework for the information discarded by machine learning models whose inputs carry a Lie group action. Given a representation $π$ of a Lie group $G$ on a space $V$ and a learned function $f\colon V \to \mathbb{R}$, we define two objects measuring the symmetry invisible to $f$. The null fiber at a point $x \in V$ is the set $N_G(f,x) = \{g \in G : f(π(g^{-1}) \cdot x) = f(x)\}$ of group elements whose inverse action on $x$ is undetectable by $f$. When $N_G(f,x)$ is independent of $x$, it coincides with the stabilizer $\mathrm{Stab}_G(f)$, the largest subgroup of $G$ under which $f$ is invariant. For smooth maps to $\mathbb{R}$, the preimage theorem guarantees that null fibers have dimension at least $\dim G - 1$ at generic inputs, regardless of architecture. For compact groups acting on themselves, the Peter--Weyl theorem yields a spectral characterization of both objects in terms of the Fourier coefficient matrices of $f$. We show that null fiber elements can be computed efficiently via Newton iteration on the orbit map, at a cost comparable to a few gradient evaluations. Applications to data masking, model fingerprinting, and privacy-preserving computation are developed and tested experimentally on molecular property prediction under $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ and spherical image classification under the Möbius group $\mathrm{PSL}(2, \mathbb{C})$. The framework applies uniformly to classical neural networks and variational quantum circuits.

</details>


### [206] [Targeted Recovery of Weight-Space Mechanisms From Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13047)
*Antoine Vigouroux,Lee Sharkey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对大模型参数分解（PD）的加速方法——目标参数分解（tPD），通过引入高秩catch-all组件仅分解处理特定输入的成分，在Transformer语言模型上验证了其效率和保真度。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于大语言模型的参数分解与可解释性，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：参数分解（parameter decomposition）与模型可解释性；场景：大语言模型（transformer language models）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: Transformer语言模型（4-block和12-block），非遥感目标检测模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等传统轻量化方法。tPD是一种加速参数分解的计算方法，而非模型压缩或加速推理技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs（仅提及分解所需FLOPs减少至7%）、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：The Pile（文本语料）；评价指标：可复现性、机制保真度（mechanistic faithfulness）、副作用评估；不涉及遥感数据集或目标检测指标。

Experiments: 在toy models和The Pile上训练的Transformer语言模型上验证；在4-block transformer上用7% FLOPs提取CSS-only子模型；在12-block transformer上消融和重写记忆化序列。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感、卫星、目标检测完全无关；2. 方法针对大语言模型的可解释性，不适用于遥感目标检测任务；3. 未提供任何目标检测精度、速度或轻量化部署指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但"targeted decomposition"的思想——仅对感兴趣输入路径进行分解/优化——在概念上可类比于对遥感在轨检测模型进行"任务导向的剪枝"或"输入感知的模型压缩"，即根据特定遥感场景（如舰船检测、飞机检测）只保留相关子网络。不过论文未提供具体可复现的轻量化方法。

Abstract: Parameter decomposition (PD) decomposes neural networks into interpretable computational components that faithfully reflect the original network's operations. However, scaling PD to large models requires vast compute, making it a costly and risky endeavor. Here we propose targeted PD (tPD), which identifies only the components that process specific inputs of interest -- from isolated prompts to large subtasks -- by introducing a high-rank catch-all component that handles all non-target data. We validate tPD on toy models and on transformer language models trained on The Pile, where it recovers reproducible, mechanistically faithful circuits. We extract a CSS-only submodel of a 4-block transformer using 7% of the FLOPs of its published decomposition, and in a 12-block transformer we surgically ablate and rewire memorized sequences, with negligible side effects on other inputs.

</details>


### [207] [Uncertainty-Aware Sequential Decision Rules for Event-Triggered LLM Invocation in Streaming Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13048)
*Zhaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于风险序贯停止的LLM触发策略框架，用于流式推理中决定何时调用大语言模型，并在涡轮风扇退化数据上验证了理论结果。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于流式推理中轻量模型与LLM的触发调度问题，属于通用机器学习/流数据处理范畴，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及模型轻量化（剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS等）或星上/边缘部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：流式推理中的LLM触发策略（sequential stopping problem）；应用场景：工业系统故障诊断（turbofan degradation）；传感器类型：CMAPSS传感器数据（非遥感影像）；目标类别：异常/故障诊断，非目标检测。

Model architecture: 轻量模型 + LLM的双模型架构，核心是触发策略（trigger policy）而非模型结构设计。摘要未说明具体轻量模型和LLM的架构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是何时调用LLM的触发策略，而非对模型本身进行剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化操作。轻量模型仅作为"快速模型"被提及，未说明其轻量化手段。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文涉及LLM调用成本分析（cost sensitivity），但这是经济成本而非计算资源开销。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CMAPSS（turbofan degradation data）；评价指标：regret（次线性遗憾）、诊断质量（grounding score >= 0.75达92.9%）、Pareto AUC；对比基线：RouteLLM-style router、contextual bandits等6种基线。

Experiments: 在CMAPSS数据集上验证了理论假设，消融了风险函数设计，与6种基线对比，分析了成本敏感性和LLM失败模式。所有原则性触发策略均达到alpha<1的次线性遗憾。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 不涉及任何目标检测任务；3. 不涉及模型轻量化技术；4. 实验数据为工业传感器数据，非遥感影像；5. 触发策略框架虽有一定理论深度，但迁移到遥感场景需要完全重新设计。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：（1）"轻量模型+大模型"的级联推理思想可类比"轻量检测器+大模型后处理"的星上-地面协同推理；（2）风险函数驱动的触发策略可类比遥感在轨检测中"何时触发高精度模型/回传数据"的决策问题。但论文本身不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化核心技术。

Abstract: Streaming inference pipelines increasingly pair lightweight fast models with Large Language Models (LLMs) that provide rich semantic understanding at substantial cost. The central question of when to invoke the LLM has received limited formal treatment. We cast this as a risk-based sequential stopping problem, where a trigger policy fires when a risk functional over the observation history exceeds a threshold. Within this framework, we prove six results: a minimum inter-event time bound excluding trigger chattering; optimality of threshold policies via smooth pasting; approximate SPRT guarantees under estimated parameters; O(sqrt(T log T)) regret for stationary streams, extending to O(sqrt((C_T + 1) T log T)) under C_T changepoints; O(1/sqrt(T)) convergence of online gradient descent for adaptive thresholds; and a calibration-to-miss-rate transfer inequality. Several classical trigger families, including event-triggered, optimal stopping, SPRT, CUSUM, and Bayesian triggers, can be expressed as special cases of this framework. On turbofan degradation data (CMAPSS) with real LLM calls, we empirically verify the theoretical assumptions, ablate the risk function design, compare against six baselines including a RouteLLM-style router and contextual bandits, and analyze cost sensitivity and LLM failure modes. The results confirm sublinear regret, with alpha < 1 for all principled triggers; high diagnostic quality, with 92.9 percent of 1600 LLM diagnoses reaching grounding score >= 0.75 under our rubric; and that anomaly-score-driven risk functions dominate alternatives by roughly an order of magnitude on the Pareto AUC.

</details>


### [208] [Gauge-Invariant, Parameter-Insensitive Regularization for Potential Recovery from Flow on Directed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13609)
*Mohammad Forouhesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究有向图上的泊松逆问题，提出规范不变图Dirichlet能量替代岭回归，解决排序坍塌问题，并延伸到图神经网络中防止过平滑。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文核心是图上的逆问题（Poisson problem with Dirichlet boundaries）和规范不变性（gauge invariance），属于图信号处理和图神经网络的理论研究。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文任务是有向图上的势恢复（potential recovery）逆问题，应用场景是点击流数据（clickstream corpora），不涉及遥感场景、目标检测或任何视觉任务。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。论文涉及图神经网络（GNN），特别是深度有向GCN（deep directed GCN），提出每层中和常数模式（neutralizing the constant mode per layer）以防止过平滑。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计、实时推理、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 三个公开点击流语料库（three public clickstream corpora）。评价指标包括秩相关（rank correlation）和动态范围保留比例（dynamic range retention）。不涉及遥感数据集或目标检测评价指标。

Experiments: 实验表明：规范不变估计保留了28%-41%的内部动态范围，而岭回归压缩至0.2%；岭回归导致排序反转（秩相关从+0.81变为-0.42）；规范不变方法在λ四个数量级范围内保持稳定。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关。论文本身是图信号处理/图学习领域的理论工作，不涉及任何视觉检测或嵌入式部署问题。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。唯一可能的间接借鉴点：论文提出的规范不变性思想（gauge invariance）在GNN中防止过平滑的思路，如果未来在遥感目标检测中使用了图神经网络（如场景图理解、点云检测等），可参考其每层中和常数模式的方法。但与本课题"遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化"的核心方向几乎无直接可借鉴点。

Abstract: Recovering a latent potential from observed flow on a directed graph (a discrete Poisson problem with Dirichlet boundaries) is ill-posed, and the standard fix backfires: ridge regularization shrinks toward a gauge-meaningless origin, collapsing and reversing the recovered ordering ($+0.81\to-0.42$ rank correlation against a planted ground truth). The gauge-invariant graph Dirichlet energy removes the hazard and delivers parameter-insensitivity: the estimate is stable across four orders of magnitude in $λ$, whereas ridge inverts the ordering for every $λ>0$. We prove the reduced solve is SPD and preserves dynamic range exactly where ridge collapses it, and localize absorbing boundaries from flow alone via a Poisson residual. The $H^1$ seminorm is classical; what is new is the gauge diagnosis, the parameter-insensitivity it buys, and an ablation showing the result is robust to the extraction method. On three public clickstream corpora the gauge-invariant estimate retains $28$--$41\%$ of the interior dynamic range while ridge collapses to as little as $0.2\%$. The same gauge invariance carries into graph neural networks -- neutralizing the constant mode per layer prevents the oversmoothing that collapses a deep directed GCN -- linking this classical inverse problem to a central question in graph learning.

</details>


### [209] [TSSM: Triaxial State Space Model for Global Station Weather Forecasting with Temporal-Variable-Historical Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13101)
*Songru Yang,Zili Liu,Tao Han,Ben Fei,Fenghua Ling,Lei Bai,Chang Liu,Xiangyang Ji,Zhenwei Shi,Zhengxia Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出三轴状态空间模型(TSSM)用于全球站点天气预报，通过历史增强的时序-变量-历史范式提升长时和极端天气预测精度。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦全球站点天气预报(Global Station Weather Forecasting)，属于气象时间序列预测任务，不涉及遥感图像、卫星对地观测、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：气象时间序列预测（天气预报）；应用场景：全球站点天气预测、极端天气预测；传感器类型：地面气象观测站（in-situ observation networks）；目标类别：无目标检测相关类别。与遥感卫星目标检测无关。

Model architecture: Triaxial State Space Model (TSSM)，三轴状态空间模型，包含Temporal、Variable、Historical三个扫描方向，采用历史增强的Temporal-Variable-Historical范式，将历史样本堆叠为周期对齐批次进行因果预测。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署可行性分析。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Weather-5K（目前最大的站点天气数据集）及human-involved datasets；评价指标：准确率(accuracy)、极端事件指标(extreme event metrics)、长时预测增益(240h增益37.5%，48h×5迭代增益103.5%)、缺失观测鲁棒性(80%缺失下保持>90%性能)。

Experiments: TSSM在Weather-5K上达到SOTA，准确率提升10%，极端事件指标提升61%；在human-involved数据集上获得95%的最佳或次佳结果；长时预测优势显著，240h增益37.5%，48h×5迭代增益103.5%；在80%观测缺失下保持>90%性能（基线<43%）。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。摘要未说明模型复杂度、推理速度等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但TSSM中的状态空间模型(State Space Model)设计思路（多轴扫描、历史增强范式）在理论上可迁移至遥感时序数据处理（如时序遥感变化检测），但与本课题"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"的核心方向关联度极低。

Abstract: Global Station Weather Forecasting (GSWF) is pivotal for localized and extreme weather prediction over key regions. Despite efforts to exploit look-back windows, existing methods show limited accuracy gains and struggle with extreme events and error accumulation. These limitations stem from overreliance on short-term patterns, which are insufficient to capture chaotic weather dynamics, especially under partial observations. To address this problem, we propose a novel Triaxial State Space Model (TSSM) with a history-enhanced Temporal-VariableHistorical paradigm, which incorporates period-aligned historical weather data to compensate for long-term, large-scale periodic, and full-window weather patterns beyond the temporal lookback window. Specifically, TSSM stacks historical samples into period-aligned batches, where forecasting is causally supported by historical and current observations. Temporal, variable, and historical scanning are designed to capture axial temporal dependencies, variable correlations, and historical evolution. This structure is hierarchically shared to model seasonal to extreme events while alleviating misalignment across historical patterns. TSSM achieves SOTA performance on Weather-5K, the largest station weather dataset to date, with 10% and 61% gains in accuracy and extreme event metrics, and obtains 95% best or second-best results on human-involved datasets. Its advantages are more pronounced in long-horizon and iterative forecasting, reaching a 37.5% gain at 240h and up to 103.5% under a 48h times 5 iterative setting. Moreover, TSSM retains > 90% performance under up to 80% missing observations, compared with < 43% for baselines, demonstrating robustness and practical potential for reliable GSWF in global in-situ observation networks.

</details>


### [210] [DAGR: State-Conditioned Goal Representations via Difference-Aware Goal Cross-Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13731)
*Xing Lei,Wenyan Yang,Xuetao Zhang,Donglin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DAGR方法，通过状态条件化的门控交叉注意力机制改进目标编码，提升强化学习中的目标条件策略性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于强化学习中的目标编码（goal-conditioned RL），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：goal-conditioned reinforcement learning（目标条件强化学习）；应用场景：导航（navigation）、操作（manipulation）、拼图（puzzle）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: DAGR（Difference-aware Goal Cross-Attention with Gated Residual）：在任意late-fusion编码器基础上，通过多尺度门控交叉注意力将静态目标嵌入细化为状态条件化嵌入；近恒等门控残差保留基础表示；差异感知目标交叉注意力利用逐token的状态-目标差异图偏置注意力分数。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: OGBench（导航任务）；操作和拼图任务也有实验。具体评价指标摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在OGBench导航任务上DAGR有提升；消融实验表明增益主要来自门控残差（gated residual），而非差异偏置（difference bias）；在操作和拼图任务上，DAGR持平或低于基线方法。

Limitations: DAGR并非通用改进，在操作和拼图任务上表现不佳；方法设计针对强化学习目标编码，与遥感视觉任务无直接关联。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。门控残差和交叉注意力机制本身是通用技术，但论文的应用场景（RL目标编码）与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化差距极大，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned reinforcement learning hinges on how the goal is encoded. Contrastive, metric, temporal-distance, and information-theoretic encoders differ in objective. They still share one trait. None of them sees the current state. Such a state-independent embedding cannot mark which part of the goal still needs action. The policy must then recover that cue by inverting both encoders. We propose DAGR. It refines the static embedding of any late-fusion encoder into a state-conditioned one through multi-scale gated cross-attention. A near-identity gated residual preserves the base representation. Difference-aware Goal Cross-Attention then biases the attention scores using a per-token state-goal discrepancy map. On OGBench, DAGR improves navigation. Our ablations trace the gain to the gated residual, not to the difference bias that names the method. On manipulation and puzzle tasks it matches or falls below the base. DAGR is a structured refinement, not a universal improvement.

</details>


### [211] [Disentangling Knowledge States with Ability and Proficiency Modeling for Knowledge Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13103)
*Duantengchuan Li,Yingqian Bi,Jinsong Chen,Rui Zhang,Mingwen Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向知识追踪（KT）任务的阶段感知框架PAKT，将学生交互序列分解为能力阶段和熟练度阶段，并用多分支Transformer建模，在六个公开数据集上取得SOTA。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文是教育数据挖掘/知识追踪领域的工作，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或模型压缩技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：知识追踪（Knowledge Tracing, KT）；场景：在线教育/智能辅导系统；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 多分支Transformer（multi-branch Transformer）+ type-aware readout module，用于联合捕获阶段特定和整体的知识状态。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向教育场景，不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 六个公开知识追踪基准数据集；评价指标为AUC。

Experiments: 在六个公开数据集上优于基线方法，最大AUC提升1.33%，平均提升0.82%。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于教育数据挖掘领域，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Knowledge tracing (KT) aims to predict students' future performance by modeling their evolving knowledge states from historical interactions. Existing KT methods usually treat the raw interaction sequence as a unified behavioral process, overlooking the phase-specific nature of learning behaviors. Our preliminary observations show that students are more likely to correctly answer previously failed knowledge concepts after sufficient practice, suggesting a transition from ability-building to proficiency-oriented learning. Motivated by this, we propose Phase-Aware Knowledge Tracing (PAKT), a KT framework that decomposes student interactions into ability and proficiency phases based on the tailored decomposition mechanism. To effectively exploit the decomposed sequences, we design a multi-branch Transformer with a type-aware readout module to jointly capture phase-specific and holistic knowledge states. We further provide a causal analysis to reveal the confounding bias caused by entangling complex learning behaviors in phase-agnostic KT models. Extensive experiments on six public benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms representative baselines, with a maximum AUC gain of 1.33% and an average gain of 0.82%.

</details>


### [212] [Algebraic Representability as the Limiting Regime of Grokking: An Exactly Solvable Model with Holomorphic Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13749)
*Chon-Fai Kam,Xavier Cadet,Miloud Bessafi,Frederic Cadet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究两层神经网络在模运算任务上的"grokking"现象，发现当激活函数为z^k时，网络输出被限制在(k+1)维特征子空间中，导致任务要么立即成功要么彻底失败，不存在记忆化到泛化的延迟转变。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文是纯理论机器学习研究，探讨神经网络在模运算任务上的grokking现象和代数表示能力，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感/卫星相关任务。任务为模运算( modular arithmetic )上的分类/预测，属于纯数学/理论ML任务。传感器类型、目标类别均不涉及。

Model architecture: 两层神经网络( two-layer networks )，激活函数为全纯单项式 sigma(z)=z^k ( holomorphic monomial activation )，隐藏宽度可变。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等内容。

Onboard deployability: 完全不涉及。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 模运算任务，编码为roots of unity。585次实验运行(585 runs)，准确率指标(99.8% accuracy匹配代数预测)。无遥感数据集。

Experiments: 585次实验验证代数理论预测，准确率99.8%。展示了从表示失败→记忆无泛化→grokking的连续路径，以及瓶颈消融实验(bottleneck ablation)。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化完全无关。该论文是纯理论工作，不提供任何可迁移的工程方法或部署方案。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文研究的是网络容量与grokking现象的理论极限情况，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: Neural networks trained on modular arithmetic exhibit grokking, a delayed transition from memorisation to generalisation known to depend on model capacity: too little and the network memorises slowly or not at all, too much and it generalises almost immediately. What happens at the extreme of this spectrum, when the architecture's expressible function class collapses to a finite-dimensional algebraic variety? We study two-layer networks with a holomorphic monomial activation sigma(z)=z^k, trained on modular tasks encoded via roots of unity. Here the network output, regardless of hidden width, is confined to a (k+1)-dimensional subspace of characters of (Z_p)^2, an O(k/p^2) slice of the full function space. We give a complete algebraic characterisation of this subspace: a task is representable if and only if its discrete Fourier support lies on the diagonal u+v = k (mod p), which for linear-phase targets reduces to the arithmetic criterion m+n=k. This is not merely a constraint on eventual generalisation but on memorisation itself: because the outputs are algebraically confined, a non-representable target cannot be fit even on the training set, and we prove a positive lower bound on the training loss, independent of width. Across 585 runs the algebraic prediction matches the observed outcome with 99.8% accuracy, with no memorisation regime and no grokking; outcomes split cleanly into instant success and outright failure. This binary behaviour is the limiting case of the capacity-grokking relationship: when the expressible class shrinks to a fixed algebraic object, the question of when a network will grok dissolves into whether it can represent the target at all. A bottleneck ablation connects this extreme to standard networks, tracing a continuous path from representational failure, through memorisation without generalisation, to grokking with a shrinking gap as capacity grows.

</details>


### [213] [STKAN: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Spatio-Temporal Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13108)
*Sicong Lai,Yuehong Hu,Siru Zhong,Si Qiao,Yuxuan Liang,Guangyin Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STKAN，将泰勒多项式KAN模块引入时空预测架构，用于交通流量预测。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是城市交通时空预测（spatio-temporal forecasting），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关场景，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题完全无关，不推荐阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：时空预测（spatio-temporal forecasting）；场景：交通流量预测（traffic forecasting）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: STKAN架构：引入泰勒多项式KAN（Taylor-polynomial Kolmogorov–Arnold Network）模块，用于空间和时间token混合。包含可学习的软节点分组分配机制（learnable soft node-group assignment mechanism）、分组空间混合（group-wise spatial mixing）、时序依赖建模、空间和时间自注意力层。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 5个交通预测基准数据集（five traffic forecasting benchmarks），具体数据集名称摘要未说明；评价指标摘要未说明具体名称。

Experiments: 在5个交通预测基准上取得有竞争力的性能，优于所评估的MLP变体。消融实验和详细对比结果摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或检测方法。仅非线性函数逼近器设计（KAN模块）的思路可能对一般性神经网络架构设计有启发，但与课题核心需求不匹配。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。KAN（Kolmogorov–Arnold Network）作为一种替代MLP的非线性函数逼近器，理论上可尝试替换遥感检测模型中的全连接层或特征融合模块，但论文本身未提供任何轻量化或检测相关的实验验证，且交通预测与遥感目标检测的任务差异巨大，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Real-world traffic data exhibit heterogeneous spatial correlations and nonlinear temporal dynamics, posing substantial challenges for accurate spatio-temporal forecasting. Existing approaches have developed increasingly sophisticated graph, attention, and decomposition architectures, while the influence of the underlying nonlinear function approximator has received comparatively less attention. In this work, we propose STKAN, a spatio-temporal forecasting architecture that introduces Taylor-polynomial Kolmogorov--Arnold Network modules into spatial and temporal token mixing. STKAN first constructs high-level spatial representations through a learnable soft node-group assignment mechanism, applies group-wise spatial mixing, and subsequently models temporal dependencies over the compressed sequence. Spatial and temporal self-attention layers are further employed to capture long-range interactions. Experiments on five traffic forecasting benchmarks show that STKAN achieves competitive performance and performs better than the evaluated MLP-based variant in the tested settings. These results suggest that the design of nonlinear function approximators can serve as a useful complement to architectural design in spatio-temporal forecasting.

</details>


### [214] [Heavy-Tailed Flow Matching via Random Clocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13841)
*Zhouhao Yang,Yezhen Wang,Kenji Kawaguchi,Vladimir Braverman,Haoyang Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于随机时钟的重尾流匹配框架(HTFM)，通过将重尾源分布建模为时钟条件高斯分布的混合，改善扩散/流匹配模型对重尾数据的生成质量。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于生成式模型（flow matching）对重尾数据分布的建模改进，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然摘要中提及了imbalanced image datasets和weather fields，但核心问题是概率生成建模，而非遥感图像理解或部署优化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：生成式建模（flow matching），处理重尾数据分布。应用场景：不平衡图像数据集、金融回报、极端天气。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: HTFM框架：将重尾源分布建模为随机时钟条件的高斯混合。时钟路径用截断logsignature特征编码，使速度场适应条件空间。基础模型为flow matching，非检测模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：2D imbalanced α-stable mixtures、CIFAR10-LT（长尾CIFAR-10）、HRRR天气场数据。评价指标：mode coverage、sample quality、tail-statistic recovery、NFE（采样步数）。

Experiments: 在2D重尾混合数据、CIFAR10-LT、HRRR天气场上，HTFM在模式覆盖、样本质量和尾部统计恢复上优于高斯流匹配及其他重尾基线方法，且保持了低NFE采样优势。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感目标检测完全无关；2. 未讨论任何轻量化或部署问题；3. 仅在生成任务上验证，未涉及检测任务；4. 未说明模型计算开销。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的间接联系是：遥感目标检测中正负样本也存在长尾/不平衡问题，但本文是生成式方法而非检测方法，且未涉及轻量化，因此对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接借鉴意义很小。

Abstract: Heavy-tailed data arise in many domains where rare events carry disproportionate importance, such as imbalanced image datasets, financial returns, and weather extremes. Standard diffusion and flow-matching models typically begin from Gaussian noise or Gaussian source distributions, which yield tractable training targets but provide a poor inductive match for heavy-tailed data. We propose Heavy-Tailed Flow Matching via Random Clocks (HTFM), a framework that portrays heavy-tailed sources as mixtures of clock-conditioned Gaussian sources. Conditioning on a given clock path, the source distribution and flow are Gaussian; marginalizing over the clock gives a Gaussian scale mixture covering Gaussian, $α$-stable, and Student-t families. To make the clock-conditioned vector field practical, we encode the path-valued clock using truncated logsignature features, allowing the velocity field to adapt to the realized conditional space with negligible overhead. Empirically, on 2D imbalanced $α$-stable mixtures, CIFAR10-LT, and HRRR weather fields, HTFM improves mode coverage, sample quality, and tail-statistic recovery over Gaussian flow matching and competitive heavy-tailed baselines, while retaining the low-NFE sampling advantage of flow matching. Moreover, the random-clock formulation further provides a practical tail-control interface: by varying only the clock law or tail parameter, the same architecture can calibrate the ``heaviness'' of generated tails across different distribution families.

</details>


### [215] [A Hybrid Mamba for Audio-Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13110)
*Yi Wang,Yinfeng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Samba模型，用Mamba状态编码器替代传统GRU，用于音视频导航任务，在Matterport3D和Replica数据集上提升导航成功率。

Research relevance: 1分，基本无关。论文研究的是音视频导航（audio-visual navigation），属于具身智能/机器人领域，与遥感卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化均无直接关系。虽然提及"lower computational cost"，但并非针对遥感在轨部署场景的轻量化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：音视频导航（audio-visual navigation）；场景：室内环境导航；传感器：音频+视觉；目标类别：无目标检测任务。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: Samba模型，核心模块：Mamba State Encoder (M-SE) 替代GRU进行时序聚合，Audio Mamba Encoder (AME) 捕获频谱图全局时频依赖。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明具体的轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。仅提及"lower computational cost"，但未给出参数量、FLOPs等具体指标。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。场景为室内导航，非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Matterport3D、Replica；评价指标：导航成功率（Success Rate, SR）。

Experiments: 在Matterport3D上SR提升11.3%对比SOTA；在Replica数据集上性能增益更明显。摘要未说明消融实验、对比方法细节。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测方向完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术细节。Mamba架构虽在序列建模上有优势，但本文未涉及遥感图像目标检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。Mamba架构（状态空间模型）作为近年来的新兴序列建模方法，理论上可探索用于遥感时序数据处理或轻量级backbone设计，但本文未涉及遥感场景，也无轻量化部署的具体方案，借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Since the paradigm centered on convolutional neural networks and recurrent architectures was established in 2020, the fundamental backbone networks for audio-visual navigation have undergone no essential changes for more than five years, making them inadequate to support efficient representation of dynamic multimodal sequences. This paper proposes Samba(A Hybrid Mamba for Audio-Visual Navigation). It uses the adaptive selection-enabled Mamba State Encoder (M-SE) to replace conventional GRUs for temporal aggregation, and constructs an Audio Mamba Encoder (AME) to remedy the limitations of convolutional operators in capturing global time-frequency dependencies in spectrograms. Experiments demonstrate that Samba exhibits exceptional generalization performance when facing unheard sound sources and unseen scenes. On the Matterport3D dataset, it improves the navigation success rate (SR) by 11.3\% compared with existing state-of-the-art models, and the performance gain is even more pronounced on the Replica dataset, which features finer scene structures. Such modernized architectural reconstruction unlocks stronger embodied representation capabilities at a lower computational cost, thereby providing a highly robust technical pathway for paradigm evolution in the field of audio-visual navigation.

</details>


### [216] [Linear Independent Component Analysis via Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14081)
*Ashutosh Jha,Michel Besserve,Simon Buchholz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Wasserstein距离的ICA算法OT-ICA，用平方Wasserstein距离替代传统负熵代理函数来度量非高斯性，实现独立成分分析。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署完全无关。论文研究的是独立成分分析（ICA）这一经典信号处理方法，应用场景为EEG伪影去除和计量经济学价格发现，属于无监督学习/信号处理领域。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：独立成分分析（ICA）；场景：EEG信号处理、计量经济学；传感器类型：无；目标类别：无。

Model architecture: 基于Wasserstein距离的ICA优化框架，通过梯度优化寻找线性投影。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何部署相关信息（参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等均未提及）。

Datasets and metrics: 模拟数据、EEG数据、计量经济学数据；评价指标未明确说明。

Experiments: 在模拟数据上优于基于代理函数的传统ICA方法；在EEG伪影去除和价格发现任务上验证了实用性。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的检测或轻量化技术。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于信号处理/无监督学习范畴，不涉及目标检测、遥感图像处理或模型轻量化中的任何核心技术。

Abstract: Linear Independent Component Analysis (ICA) recovers jointly independent source signals from their linear mixtures. To achieve this, classical ICA algorithms attempt to maximize non-Gaussianity, measured by negentropy, which is linked to independence by information theory. Because exact negentropy optimization is intractable, they rely on proxy contrast functions, such as fourth-order cumulants, and parametric log-likelihoods. We propose instead to measure non-Gaussianity using the squared Wasserstein distance $W_2^2$ to a standard Gaussian. We prove that the Wasserstein distance between a standard normal distribution and linear projections of the data is maximized when the projection recovers an independent component. Based on this observation, we propose the OT-ICA algorithm which finds this projection by gradient-based optimization. Empirical evaluation on simulated data shows that OT-ICA outperforms proxy-based methods for different distributions of the latent variables. Application to EEG artifact removal and econometric price discovery confirm OT-ICA can be used for applied ICA tasks without distributional assumptions.

</details>


### [217] [CoDiffGRN: Rethinking Gene Regulatory Network Inference via the BEELINE-KGC Benchmark and Co-evolutionary Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13120)
*Jiaze Song,Runhao Zhao,Minghao Xu,Bin Cui,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG
