TL;DR: 该论文研究基因调控网络（GRN）推断，提出基于共进化离散扩散框架的归纳式图补全方法，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。论文研究的是单细胞转录组学数据中的基因调控网络推断，属于计算生物学/生物信息学领域。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：基因调控网络（GRN）推断；场景：单细胞转录组学数据分析；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：基因、转录因子（TF）、调控关系。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 提出Co-evolving Discrete Diffusion框架，联合建模离散化的基因表达状态和调控关系；引入TF-ALL Subgraph Sampling (TASS) 用于可扩展训练。与遥感检测模型无关。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 提出新基准Benchmark，采用inductive gene-holdout split（归纳式基因留出划分）和知识图谱补全指标（knowledge graph completion metrics）。具体数据集名称摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在提出的Benchmark上，所提方法在novel regulatory discovery（新调控关系发现）任务上显著优于现有方法，消融实验验证了设计有效性。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。摘要未说明计算效率、推理速度等部署相关信息。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，无直接可借鉴点。但可抽象思考：该论文将问题重新定义为inductive ranking-centric graph completion，这种"从全局分类到排序优先"的问题重构思路，在遥感目标检测中（如从大量候选框中筛选高置信度目标）可能有间接启发，但关联度极低。

Abstract: Inferring gene regulatory networks (GRNs) from single-cell transcriptomic data is crucial for biological discovery, yet existing approaches suffer from a fundamental misalignment with real-world needs. Researchers typically seek a small set of high-confidence regulatory interactions for experimental validation, often involving previously unseen genes. However, current benchmarks rely on transductive splits with global classification metrics, while prevailing models struggle to generalize under inductive settings. To bridge this gap, we reformulate GRN inference as an inductive, ranking-centric graph completion problem and introduce \textbf{\benchmark}, a new benchmark that incorporates an inductive gene-holdout split together with knowledge graph completion metrics to better evaluate top-ranked predictions. Building on this, we propose \textbf{\method}, the first co-evolutionary discrete diffusion framework that jointly models biologically coherent discretized gene expression states and regulatory interactions for robust inductive generalization and improved top-ranked regulatory discovery. We further introduce TF-ALL Subgraph Sampling (TASS) for scalable training. Extensive experiments on {\benchmark} show that {\method} establishes new state-of-the-art performance, significantly outperforming existing methods in novel regulatory discovery, and ablation studies further verify the effectiveness of our design.

</details>


### [218] [ShortOPD: Recovering Pruned LLMs with Short-to-Long On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13124)
*Qingyu Zhang,Qianhao Yuan,Hongyu Lin,Yaojie Lu,Xianpei Han,Le Sun,Xiang Li,Ming Xu,Jiarui Li,Xiuyin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出短到长在线策略蒸馏（ShortOPD）方法，解决结构化剪枝后的大语言模型在自由生成任务中性能崩溃的问题，通过检测重复后缀并动态调整rollout长度来加速恢复训练。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦大语言模型（LLM）的结构化剪枝与生成质量恢复，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或任何视觉任务，属于NLP/生成式AI领域。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向基本无关，仅结构化剪枝+蒸馏的方法论有极有限的跨领域借鉴价值，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：结构化剪枝后LLM的自由生成质量恢复；应用场景：数学、代码、开放式文本生成；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于Transformer的大语言模型（LLM），具体架构摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 结构化剪枝（structured pruning）+ 知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation, KD），具体为On-Policy Distillation (OPD) 及其改进版本ShortOPD（短到长在线策略蒸馏调度）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅报告了训练时间（8.5小时 vs 35.9小时）和rollout token数减少71%，但这些是训练开销而非推理部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：数学、代码、开放式生成任务（具体数据集名称摘要未说明）；评价指标：pass@1, pass@k, 以及生成质量分数（具体指标名称摘要未说明）。

Experiments: 主要结果：ShortOPD将压缩模型得分提升至未恢复值的约9倍，比标准恢复方法（SFT无KD、KD、SeqKD）提升1.6-4.4倍；以1/4训练时间和71%更少rollout token达到固定8192-token rollout horizon的效果。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对LLM生成任务，与遥感视觉目标检测完全无关；2. 结构化剪枝+蒸馏方法是否适用于CNN/Transformer-based检测器未验证；3. 未报告推理延迟、参数量、FLOPs等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 1. 结构化剪枝+在线策略蒸馏的思路可借鉴：在遥感检测模型剪枝后，可用原始模型作为教师对剪枝模型进行on-policy蒸馏恢复；2. "短到长"训练调度策略（先短序列后长序列）可类比为"先简单样本后难样本"的训练策略，用于遥感检测模型剪枝后的微调恢复；3. 检测重复模式并提前截断低信息量序列的思想，可类比为在遥感检测中识别并跳过低信息量背景区域，加速训练。

Abstract: Structured pruning is a hardware-friendly way to compress LLMs, but it is mostly validated on multiple-choice recognition tasks, while the same compressed checkpoints can collapse on the free-form generation that deployment actually requires. Two observations trace this gap. First, greedy \textsc{pass}@$1$ nearly vanishes after compression, yet \textsc{pass}@$k$ recovers substantially under repeated sampling: useful generations are demoted, not erased. Second, the recoverable regime fails mainly through suffix repetition. Recovery should therefore train on the compressed model's own on-policy states with dense token-level supervision, which On-Policy Distillation (OPD) provides by reusing the pre-compression model as a frozen teacher. However, long on-policy rollouts spend early recovery budget on low-information repetitive suffixes, delaying loss descent. To mitigate this waste, we propose \textbf{\shortopd}, a short-to-long OPD schedule that detects teacher-confirmed repetitive suffixes, treats the surviving prefix as each rollout's effective length, and allocates future rollout budgets to the effective lengths the policy can currently use. Across math, code, and open-ended generation, \shortopd\ raises the compressed model's score to about $9\times$ its unrecovered value and $1.6$--$4.4\times$ standard recovery recipes (SFT w/o KD, KD, and SeqKD), and it matches a fixed $8192$-token rollout horizon within two points using a quarter of the training time ($8.5$ vs.\ $35.9$ hours) and $71\%$ fewer rollout tokens. We hope this recipe helps move structured pruning beyond marginal gains on perplexity and multiple-choice benchmarks, a step closer to deployment-ready generation quality.

</details>


### [219] [HEDGEHOG: Hierarchical Evaluation of Drug Generators Through Rigorous Filtration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13155)
*Daria A. Ryabchenko,Pavel Gurevich,Shamil Kadyrov,Daria Frolova,Kseniia Fedisheva,Sergei A. Nikolenko,Alexander Shapeev,Marina A. Pak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HEDGEHOG，一个面向分子生成模型的六阶段工业级过滤基准，用于评估生成分子的药物化学合理性、合成可行性和三维对接质量。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于药物发现中的分子生成模型评估，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：分子生成模型评估；应用场景：早期药物发现（hit identification）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体的分子生成模型架构，仅提及评估了23个分子生成器，涵盖三类模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。本文讨论的是分子生成模型的评估基准，与模型压缩、加速、轻量化无关。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。本文与星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、能耗等完全无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：230,000个生成分子；评价指标：六阶段过滤（预处理、理化描述符筛选、结构告警与图合理性检查、合成可行性、对接与结合亲和力估计、三维构象与相互作用检查）。

Experiments: 23个分子生成器在标准化协议下评估，仅0.65%的初始分子通过所有六阶段过滤。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的局限性分析。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。本文属于计算化学/药物发现领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化研究方向无任何交集。

Abstract: Generative molecular models can support early drug discovery by proposing new candidate compounds de novo. In practice, useful candidates must balance target-relevant activity, synthetic accessibility, physicochemical properties, and other multiparameter design constraints. However, metrics commonly used to evaluate molecular generators only weakly reflect whether the generated compounds are medicinally plausible and suitable for downstream computation. This can produce false positives in model evaluation, incorrect assumptions, and inefficient use of computational resources. We introduce HEDGEHOG, a unified six-stage filtration benchmark that is inspired by industrial hit identification workflows: (i) preprocessing; (ii) physicochemical descriptor screening; (iii) structural alerts and graph-sanity checks; (iv) synthesis feasibility; (v) docking and binding affinity estimation; and (vi) three-dimensional pose and interaction checks. We evaluate 23 molecular generators across three model classes under a standardized protocol. Across 230,000 generated molecules, only 0.65% of initial molecules survive all stages. Our results expose a central limitation of current molecular generators: molecules that appear acceptable under isolated criteria rarely satisfy medicinal chemistry, synthesis, docking, and 3D pose filters simultaneously.

</details>


### [220] [SteinGate: Tail-Sensitive Safe Reinforcement Learning via Stein Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13175)
*Yassine Chemingui,Chenhua Fan,Honghao Wei,Janardhan Rao Doppa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SteinGate，一种基于核化Stein散度的边界感知分布安全证书，用于安全强化学习中检测和规避罕见但灾难性的尾部风险事件。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于安全强化学习（Safe RL）中的约束违反检测与规避，属于机器人/自动驾驶/控制领域的决策安全问题，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或星上部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 安全强化学习中的约束满足与风险规避；连续控制基准任务（continuous-control benchmarks）；不涉及遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体网络结构。核心组件为基于Kernelized Stein Discrepancy（KSD）的非参数安全证书（SteinGate），用于评估策略rollout成本分布与安全参考分布的一致性。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理或边缘部署相关技术。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 连续控制基准任务（continuous-control benchmarks）；评价指标为训练期间约束违反的频率与严重程度（frequency and severity of constraint violations）以及累积回报（returns）。

Experiments: 在连续控制基准上，SteinGate相比SOTA基线显著降低了训练期间约束违反的频率和严重程度，同时保持了有竞争力的回报。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关；2. 仅在连续控制任务上验证，未涉及图像或遥感数据；3. 未讨论计算开销或部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但可抽象思考：KSD作为一种非参数分布一致性检验方法，理论上可迁移到遥感目标检测模型在轨部署时的输出分布漂移监测（如检测置信度分布是否偏离地面参考），但论文本身未朝此方向展开，需要大量额外工作。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning typically enforces safety by bounding expected cumulative costs, a criterion that often fails to detect rare but catastrophic tail events. To overcome these limitations, this paper introduces SteinGate, a boundary-aware distributional safety certificate that replaces fragile tail fitting with a robust consistency check using Kernelized Stein Discrepancy while accounting for boundary atoms induced by clipped costs. SteinGate evaluates whether observed policy rollout costs remain consistent with a safe reference distribution, providing a non-parametric safety certificate. This certificate is used to dynamically adapt the learning regime: favoring reward-improving policy updates when rollouts remain consistent with the safe reference and switching to recovery behavior when the cost tail deviates. Experiments on continuous-control benchmarks demonstrate that SteinGate significantly reduces both the frequency and severity of constraint violations during training while maintaining competitive returns relative to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [221] [Concurrent Image Understanding and Generation: Self-Correcting Coupled Markov Jump Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13188)
*Minh-Quan Le,Armand Comas,Alexandros Lattas,Stylianos Moschoglou,Pedro Vélez,Amit Raj,Aaron Germuth,Thabo Beeler,Dimitris Samaras,Di Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种自校正耦合马尔可夫跳跃过程（SC-CMJP）框架，用于多模态联合生成（文本+图像），通过跨模态注意力加权置信度实现模态间相互校正，并引入CO₂Jump无训练单遍采样器。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于多模态联合生成（文本+图像），属于生成式AI/多模态学习领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多模态联合生成（joint multimodal generation），包括图像理解、图像编辑、视觉推理（迷宫求解、数独求解）。应用场景：通用视觉与语言生成。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于Masked Diffusion Models (MDMs)，提出Self-Correcting Coupled Markov Jump Processes (SC-CMJP)框架，引入跨模态注意力加权的置信度函数作为模态间转移速率，并设计remasking jump机制。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。论文提出的CO₂Jump是training-free sampler，但目的是提升生成质量而非模型压缩或加速。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文未涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 自建数据集：JEdit-1M, JMaze-200K, JNono-200K，含分布内和分布外基准。评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标名称。

Experiments: CO₂Jump在图像理解、图像编辑、视觉推理（迷宫和数独求解）任务上达到最佳联合生成性能。采样器性能随去噪步数单调提升，证明跨模态耦合的累积收益。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 未讨论任何检测任务；3. 未涉及模型效率、推理速度或部署约束；4. 方法复杂度较高（多模态耦合+跳跃过程），不适合资源受限场景。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下思路可间接参考：1）跨模态注意力加权机制的思想或可迁移到遥感多模态（如光学+SAR）特征融合中；2）remasking/自校正机制或可用于检测结果的跨模态一致性校验。但整体方向差异较大，不建议作为重点参考。

Abstract: Human cognition does not separate understanding and generation. A teacher at a whiteboard speaks and draws $\textit{together}$, each modality reshapes the other. In this paper, we bring this coupled loop to artificial systems. Masked Diffusion Models (MDMs) are ideally suited to this task, yet existing samplers either decode text and image interleavedly or independently update them in parallel branches that share only previous-step history, but not the other modality's latest decisions $\textit{within}$ the same step; combined with MDMs' inability to remask, cross-modal contradictions are neither detected nor repaired. We introduce $\textbf{Self-Correcting Coupled Markov Jump Processes (SC-CMJP)}$, a framework in which one modality's transition rates are functionals of the other modality's confidence score, as weighted by cross-modal attention. Furthermore, a remasking jump retracts commitments the moment cross-modal evidence turns against them. In conjunction with SC-CMJP, we introduce $\texttt{CO}_\texttt{2}\texttt{Jump}$ (Self-$\underline{\text{CO}}$rrecting $\underline{\text{CO}}$upled $\underline{\text{Jump}}$), a novel training-free single-pass sampler for joint multimodal geneneration. For training and evaluation purposes, we have created and will release three large-scale joint multimodal generation corpora: $\text{JEdit-1M}$, $\text{JMaze-200K}$, $\text{JNono-200K}$, with matching in- and out-of-distribution benchmarks. $\texttt{CO}_\texttt{2}\texttt{Jump}$ achieves best joint performance for image understanding and editing as well as visual reasoning (maze and nonogram solving). The performance of the sampler scales monotonically with the number of denoising steps, evidence that the benefits of cross-modal coupling $\textit{compound}$ across the trajectory. Project page: https://coupled-jump.github.io

</details>


### [222] [EMAGN: Efficient Multi-Attention Graph Network via Learned Clustering for Scalable Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13241)
*Mingxing Xu,Rakesh Chowdary Machineni,Ke Liu,Xi Cheng,Chengqi Lu,Xin Hu,Lyuhao Chen,Xiangyu Li,Junwei You,Oliver Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种线性化空间注意力机制的图网络EMAGN，通过可学习聚类矩阵将注意力复杂度从O(N²d)降至O(NMd)，用于交通预测任务。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于城市交通预测（traffic forecasting），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更未提及星上部署或轻量化模型压缩方法。虽然其注意力线性化方法在效率提升上有一定通用借鉴意义，但场景和任务均不匹配。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，仅注意力线性化思路有极有限的通用参考价值，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：交通预测（traffic forecasting）；场景：城市道路交通；传感器类型：道路传感器（PEMS-BAY, METR-LA数据集）；目标类别：无（非检测任务）。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: EMAGN（Efficient Multi-Attention Graph Network），基于GMAN骨架，引入可学习聚类矩阵C_k和C_v对key和value向量进行自适应分组，实现线性化空间注意力。

Lightweight method: 注意力机制线性化（attention linearisation），通过聚类矩阵将复杂度从O(N²d)降至O(NMd)，属于算法层面的效率优化，而非模型压缩（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未提及星上/边缘部署。但报告了GPU显存降低58%（从11GB溢出到可运行）、训练时间减少32%、推理时间减少38%，这些效率指标对部署有参考价值，但场景非遥感卫星。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：PEMS-BAY, METR-LA（交通流数据集）；评价指标：MAE（平均绝对误差），报告了相对全注意力GMAN的MAE差距为2.7-3.2%。

Experiments: 与GMAN（全注意力）、Linformer、Performer对比；EMAGN在精度接近（MAE差2.7-3.2%）的情况下，训练时间减32%，推理时间减38%，GPU显存减58%；在K=16注意力头时GMAN在11GB GPU上OOM而EMAGN可运行。

Limitations: 1. 场景为交通预测，非遥感/卫星/目标检测，领域差异大；2. 未涉及模型参数量、FLOPs等轻量化核心指标；3. 未讨论在边缘设备或星上硬件的部署可行性；4. 未涉及目标检测任务中的小目标、旋转框等遥感特有挑战。

Ideas for my research: 1. 注意力线性化思路（聚类矩阵分组）可借鉴到遥感目标检测的Transformer backbone中，降低自注意力的计算和显存开销，有利于星上部署；2. 其显存降低58%的效果对资源受限的星上平台有吸引力；3. 但需注意：交通预测的图结构注意力与遥感图像的目标检测注意力机制差异较大，直接迁移需重新设计；4. 该方法的聚类矩阵设计思路可启发遥感场景中针对空间冗余的注意力加速方案。

Abstract: Traffic forecasting is highly challenging due to complex and nonlinear spatial and temporal dependencies. Self-attention mechanisms have been widely adopted to model dynamic and long-range dependencies, achieving state-of-the-art performance, but suffer from limited scalability due to quadratic computational and memory complexity. To address this, we propose an Efficient Multi-Attention Graph Network (EMAGN) that linearises the spatial attention mechanism itself, inspired by the theory of fast high-dimensional Gaussian filtering. Two learned clustering matrices C_k and C_v adaptively group key and value vectors into M super-clusters, reducing complexity from O(N^2 d) to O(NMd) without sacrificing the flexibility of attention for dynamic dependency modelling. Experimental results on PEMS-BAY and METR-LA show that EMAGN achieves accuracy within 2.7-3.2% MAE of full-attention GMAN while reducing training time by 32%, inference time by 38%, and GPU memory by 58%. Critically, at K=16 attention heads, full-attention GMAN runs out of memory on a standard 11 GB GPU entirely while EMAGN continues to operate, demonstrating a categorical expansion of feasible model configurations. EMAGN also surpasses Linformer and Performer in both accuracy and efficiency within the same backbone, owing to its traffic-network-aware adaptive clustering.

</details>


### [223] [Reassessing Muon for Matrix Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13246)
*Ali Parviz,Gal Mishne,Alex Cloninger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Muon优化器在低秩矩阵分解问题上的表现，发现其并不一致优于AdamW，质疑其在大规模深度学习中的优势来源。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文讨论的是通用深度学习优化器（Muon vs AdamW），不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署中的任何核心问题。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。不涉及遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。仅提及低秩矩阵分解这一简化问题，未涉及任何目标检测网络结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何模型压缩/加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。仅提及在低秩矩阵分解问题上进行控制实验，未使用任何遥感或目标检测数据集。

Experiments: 在低秩矩阵分解任务上对比Muon与AdamW，发现Muon并不一致优于精心调参的自适应基线，且部分优势对超参数敏感。

Limitations: 论文本身聚焦优化器理论分析，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无可借鉴的局限性信息。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。Muon优化器本身可能对遥感模型训练有潜在影响，但本文结论（Muon并不一致优于AdamW）反而提示在遥感目标检测任务中不必盲目追求新型优化器。对轻量化课题无直接可借鉴点。

Abstract: Muon has recently emerged as a strong optimizer for large-scale deep learning, where it reshapes gradient updates through approximate orthogonalization and has been reported to outperform Adam and AdamW in large language model training. Its empirical success has motivated a growing body of theoretical work that interprets Muon as steepest descent under the spectral norm. Yet it remains unclear which of Muon's advantages stem from its update rule itself and which are artifacts of the scale, architecture, and data of modern deep networks. In this work, we isolate the optimizer from these confounding factors by studying Muon on a simple, well-understood, and spectrally structured problem: low-rank matrix factorization. Through a controlled comparison against carefully tuned adaptive baselines, we find that Muon does not consistently outperform AdamW in this setting and that several previously reported advantages are sensitive to hyperparameter choices. Our results provide a more nuanced picture of when spectrum-aware orthogonalization is beneficial and argue for evaluating modern optimizers on controlled problems in addition to end-to-end benchmarks.

</details>


### [224] [Deconstructing Actor-Critic: A Large-scale Empirical Study of Design Components for Practitioners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13274)
*Haseeb Shah,Lingwei Zhu,Adam White,Martha White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究强化学习中的actor-critic算法组件设计决策（策略更新方式、动作分布表示、梯度估计方法等）对控制任务可靠性和超参数敏感性的影响，基于真实水处理厂的控制任务进行了超过33,000次实验分析。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是强化学习算法组件设计（actor-critic算法），应用场景是水处理厂控制，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化或在轨部署等核心内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：强化学习控制（reinforcement learning control）；应用场景：真实水处理厂（real water treatment plant）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: actor-critic算法，涉及策略更新方式、动作分布表示（Gaussian vs. bounded distributions）、梯度估计方法（pathwise gradient estimators）、更新频率等组件设计。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：基于真实水处理厂导出的控制任务（control task derived from a real water treatment plant）；评价指标：变异性（variability across runs）、超参数敏感性（sensitivity to hyperparameters）、可靠性（reliability）。

Experiments: 超过33,000次实验，分析了actor-critic算法各组件设计对可靠性和超参数敏感性的影响。发现Gaussian动作分布+pathwise梯度估计器是最不可靠的配置之一，而有界分布（bounded distributions）+自适应更新调度（adaptive update schedules）在广泛设置下保持鲁棒。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 仅基于单一水处理厂控制任务，结论的泛化性有限；3. 未涉及任何视觉感知或目标检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与本人课题无直接可借鉴点。如果强行寻找间接联系，强化学习中的"轻量化"设计思路（如减少超参数调优成本、提高算法鲁棒性）可类比于模型轻量化中的自动化调优，但关联非常薄弱，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is increasingly being considered for controlling real-world systems, from fusion plasma and autonomous vehicles to drug discovery and drinking water treatment, where reliability is essential and tuning budgets are limited. Actor-critic algorithms share a set of design decisions, such as how the policy is updated, how it represents the distribution over actions, how its gradient is estimated, and how often it is updated relative to the value estimator. Using a control task derived from a real water treatment plant, we analyze over 33,000 experiments to determine how these components affect variability across runs and sensitivity to hyperparameters. Common defaults, such as Gaussian action distributions with pathwise gradient estimators, are among the least reliable configurations, whereas bounded distributions with adaptive update schedules remain robust across a wide range of settings. These findings offer empirical guidance to practitioners across scientific and engineering domains for understanding and making component-level decisions when adapting actor-critic methods to new real-world control settings.

</details>


### [225] [Tabular Foundation Models for Discrete Choice Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13314)
*Liu Liu,Dan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨表格基础模型(TFMs)在离散选择问题中的应用，提出将选择集依赖和消费者异质性编码到行式学习框架中，在酸奶扫描面板数据上取得优于层次贝叶斯估计的效果。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是市场营销/运筹学中的消费者离散选择建模，属于计量经济学和需求估计领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文属于市场营销/计量经济学领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：离散选择建模（discrete choice modeling）；应用场景：市场营销与运营管理中的需求估计；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：消费者选择（酸奶品牌选择）。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs)，基于in-context learning的行式学习框架，编码了choice-set dependence和individual-level heterogeneity。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。文中提到运行速度比层次贝叶斯快16倍，但这属于方法本身的效率优势，而非模型压缩/加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到运行速度比层次贝叶斯快16倍，但这是在消费者选择数据集上的计算效率，与星上部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：yogurt scanner panel（酸奶扫描面板数据）；评价指标：holdout log-likelihood（留出对数似然）、hit rate（命中率）。与遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、NWPU VHR-10等）完全无关。

Experiments: 最佳重构方法在holdout log-likelihood上优于层次贝叶斯估计8%，在hit rate上优于3.6%，运行速度快16倍。个体异质性编码是预测精度的主要驱动因素。在中等数据量（每人10-40次购买）下优势最大。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提取任何与遥感目标检测或模型轻量化相关的局限性信息。

Ideas for my research: 本文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题无直接可借鉴点。唯一可能间接相关的思路是：论文通过编码个体异质性（individual-level heterogeneity）来提升基础模型在特定任务上的表现，这种"任务特定编码"的思想在遥感领域可能类比为"针对遥感图像特性（如旋转不变性、尺度变化、背景杂波）设计专用编码模块"，但关联度极低。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) generate predictions on structured data via in-context learning, without task-specific estimation. We ask whether TFMs can be effectively applied to discrete choice, a central demand estimation framework in marketing and operations, and find that directly applying TFMs yields limited performance. The gap is structural: TFMs assume row-independent observations, whereas discrete choice is inherently set-valued and subject to persistent consumer preference heterogeneity. We propose a reformulation that encodes both choice-set dependence and individual heterogeneity within a row-based learning framework. Evaluated on a yogurt scanner panel, individual-level heterogeneity encoding is the dominant driver of predictive accuracy. The best reformulation outperforms hierarchical Bayesian estimation by 8\% in holdout log-likelihood and 3.6\% in hit rate, running 16 times faster, a practical advantage for large-scale demand estimation. The advantage is largest in the medium-data regime (10--40 purchase occasions per consumer), where parametric Bayesian shrinkage most distorts estimates for atypical consumers. Fine-tuning on population choice data provides additional gains for consumers with shallow purchase histories, where in-context learning has limited individual-specific signal to condition on. These results establish a principled approach for applying foundation models to consumer choice problems more broadly.

</details>


### [226] [Accuracy-Preserving Stability Regularization for Large-Scale Retail Demand Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13331)
*Jize Li,Jiani He,Dishu Yang,Dingyan Shang,Jingjing Liu,Shiqi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出在零售需求预测的训练过程中加入时序稳定性正则化项，以改善相邻预测值的平滑性，同时保持点预测精度基本不变。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦零售需求预测（retail demand forecasting），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关领域；不涉及目标检测（object detection）、小目标检测、旋转框检测等任何检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、边缘部署等任何模型压缩或加速方法。唯一可能间接相关的是"训练时正则化"这一思想，但应用场景和问题定义完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：零售需求预测（retail demand forecasting）；应用场景：补货、产能、劳动力、运输规划；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，仅提及"stability-aware hybrid model"结合了近期需求嵌入（recent-demand embeddings）与日历、价格、层级、商品、门店等特征，以及XGBoost作为对比基线。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。论文核心是训练时正则化（training-time regularization），用于提升预测稳定性，而非模型压缩或加速。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：M5 demand series（1000、3000、4000系列规模）；评价指标：Forecast Stability Score（稳定性指标）、RMSE（点精度指标）。

Experiments: 稳定性感知混合模型相比XGBoost在三个规模上分别提升Forecast Stability Score 6.91%、6.66%、7.68%，RMSE变化在0.72%以内（3个随机种子）。与事后指数平滑（post-hoc exponential smoothing）对比，训练时正则化在保持点精度方面更优。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。论文本身局限：仅验证了M5数据集，未说明在其他零售数据集上的泛化性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但"训练时正则化"这一思想在概念层面可迁移：在遥感目标检测模型轻量化中，可以考虑在训练过程中加入针对推理效率或部署约束的正则化项（如FLOPs正则化、延迟正则化），而非仅在训练后做剪枝/量化。不过本文的具体方法（时序稳定性正则化）与遥感目标检测的问题定义差异极大，直接借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Retail demand forecasts are reused across replenishment, capacity, labor, and transportation planning cycles. Point-error objectives do not constrain abrupt movement between adjacent forecasts, while post-hoc smoothing acts only after model fitting. We ask whether a training-time penalty on consecutive within-series movement can improve horizontal forecast-path stability without materially changing point accuracy. The penalty is evaluated in a temporal-structured pipeline combining recent-demand embeddings with calendar, price, hierarchy, item, and store features. On selected M5 demand series at 1000, 3000, and 4000-series scales, the stability-aware hybrid model improves Forecast Stability Score over XGBoost by 6.91%, 6.66%, and 7.68%, respectively, while RMSE changes remain within 0.72% across three random seeds. Post-hoc exponential smoothing attains lower raw movement but incurs a larger RMSE cost; training-time regularization preserves more point accuracy and performs favorably under normalized stability. These findings extend forecast evaluation from point-error minimization toward an accuracy-stability trade-off perspective for operational retail forecasting.

</details>


### [227] [Agora: Collective and Permissionless Internet-Scale Pretraining of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13332)
*Gil Avraham,Violetta Shevchenko,Hadi Mohaghegh Dolatabadi,Karol Pajak,James Snewin,Harry Xi,Rodney O'Donnell,Thalaiyasingam Ajanthan,Sameera Ramasinghe,Chamin Hewa Koneputugodage,Shamane Siriwardhana,Alexander Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Agora系统，利用互联网连接的异构消费级GPU进行大语言模型分布式训练，实现多方可信协作训练（Protocol Learning），并展示了8.6B参数模型Pluralis-8B的预训练。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦大语言模型（LLM）的分布式训练系统设计，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然涉及分布式训练和通信效率优化，但场景、任务、数据模态均与遥感卫星在轨目标检测无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：大语言模型分布式预训练；场景：去中心化、异构、互联网连接的消费级GPU集群；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: Pluralis-8B：8.6B参数大语言模型，基于pipeline parallelism（流水线并行）进行模型分片，每个参与者仅持有模型的一个stage。

Lightweight method: 不涉及模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。论文关注的是分布式训练系统的通信效率优化和容错设计，而非模型本身的压缩或加速。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。参数量8.6B，远超出星上部署可行性范围。训练速度~170k tokens/s，4.2 tokens per TFLOP，效率为集中式H100基线的63%。但这些指标针对LLM训练，与遥感目标检测推理无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：FineWeb-Edu（500B tokens）；评价指标：训练吞吐量（tokens/s）、计算效率（tokens per TFLOP）、收敛质量（与集中式参考运行的差距）。

Experiments: Pluralis-8B在40天内由330个贡献节点（主要为消费级GPU，通过互联网连接，可随时加入/离开）完成训练，达到集中式H100基线63%的计算效率，收敛质量接近集中式参考运行。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。论文未涉及任何遥感数据、目标检测任务、模型压缩或边缘部署内容。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能的间接启发：分布式训练中的通信效率优化思路（如带宽高效的pipeline并行）在极端边缘场景（如卫星星座协同推理）中可能有理论参考价值，但论文本身未涉及遥感或推理部署场景，需自行跨领域迁移。

Abstract: Training large language models at the multi-billion to trillion parameter scale is confined to datacenters, where data-parallel (DP) and model-parallel (MP) techniques presume homogeneous accelerators, high-speed interconnects, and a single orchestrating entity. Frontier model development is thereby concentrated among the few groups able to assemble such clusters. Meanwhile, an enormous pool of compute remains unusable for training: consumer and professional GPUs that are heterogeneous, preemptible, individually owned, and connected only by the internet. We present Agora, a system that makes efficient use of this compute. Agora combines bandwidth-efficient pipeline-parallel model sharding over internet-grade links with multi-party, fault-tolerant collective operations. Each participant holds only one stage of the model, and no single party ever possesses the full weights. We term this setup Protocol Learning: it enables collectively trained, collectively owned models, opening a path to open-source frontier training with economic sustainability. This report presents the outcome of a research effort spanning communication-efficient parallelism, asynchronous optimization, and fault-tolerant systems design. It culminates in the first demonstration of its kind: Pluralis-8B, an open, permissionless pretraining run of an 8.6B-parameter model on 500B tokens of FineWeb-Edu. The model was trained over 40 days by 330 contributor nodes, predominantly consumer GPUs on internet connections, joining and leaving throughout. The run sustained ~170k tokens/s and 4.2 tokens per TFLOP of pooled compute, 63% of the efficiency of a centralized H100 baseline, and converged to within a small margin of a centralized reference run.

</details>


### [228] [Structured Reinforcement Learning for Bayesian Persuasion : Application to Intelligent Interactive Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13576)
*Merlin Paul,Anup Aprem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于贝叶斯说服的在线结构化强化学习框架，用于智能领航车与联网车辆之间的交互式驾驶信号策略设计，实现动态交通管理中的路线协调。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是智能交通系统中的交互式驾驶与信号策略设计，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱图像、空间在轨智能等遥感领域内容；也不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等视觉检测任务；更不涉及模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、边缘/星上部署等压缩加速技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：交互式驾驶中的信号策略设计与强化学习；应用场景：动态交通管理、车道选择；传感器类型：摘要未说明（涉及实时交通数据，但非遥感传感器）；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: MAPL（结构化策略学习算法）、SQP（超模Q学习算法），基于强化学习的信号策略框架，非视觉模型架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文关注的是信号策略的计算效率（computationally efficient signaling strategy），但方法本质是强化学习算法优化，与模型剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化技术无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未提及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。虽然提到"computationally efficient"，但这是针对强化学习策略的计算复杂度，而非边缘/星上部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数值分析基于实时交通数据的车道选择应用场景；评价指标为旅行奖励的cost efficiency（所提方法相比现有方法提升30%成本效率）。未使用任何遥感标准数据集。

Experiments: 数值分析表明所提方法在优化领航车和联网车辆的旅行奖励方面，相比现有信号策略设计方法成本效率提升30%。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的技术参考。论文本身未讨论视觉检测、模型压缩或星上部署等核心问题。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于智能交通系统中的强化学习与博弈论方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在问题定义、技术路线、评价体系上均无交集。

Abstract: Interactive driving, wherein an intelligent lead vehicle equipped with real-time traffic data coordinates route choices of connected vehicles, offers a promising approach to dynamic traffic management. To address the challenge of harmonising decisions, this paper considers the strategic information revealing framework of Bayesian persuasion. Here, the principal (lead vehicle) aims to guide the agent's (connected vehicle) partially observable sequential decision making towards its own objectives by selectively revealing information, such as real-time traffic ahead, using signals. However, the agent's farsighted response to maximize its long-term reward, renders the principal's signaling strategy design computationally challenging. We propose an online structured reinforcement learning framework to synthesize computationally efficient signaling strategy which is persuasive for a far-sighted agent. The main contributions of the paper are as follows: (i) For a monotonic agent with approximate best response, we propose MAPL, a structured policy learning algorithm for faster online learning, (ii) Identification of sufficient conditions for the supermodular structure of the Q function of the principal for a monotonic agent, (iii) Identification of sufficient conditions to ensure the persuasiveness of the principal's signaling strategy, (iv) Supermodular Q learning for Principal (SQP), which leverages the supermodular structure of principal's action value to synthesize computationally efficient signaling strategy that is persuasive for a monotonic learning agent, (v) Numerical analysis considering a real-time application of Bayesian persuasive driving for lane selection demonstrates that the proposed method is 30% cost efficient for optimising travelling rewards of both the lead and connected vehicle compared to the existing methodologies for signaling strategy design.

</details>


### [229] [Weight Feedback Computes the Jacobian Transpose Locally in Modern Deep Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13380)
*Junlong Shen,Xingyu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WF-Act-PC方法，通过将预测编码(PC)中的Jacobian转置乘积分解为三个局部项，消除了PC中最后一个非局部操作，使PC在深层网络上性能超越经典PC方法并接近BP。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于生物启发的预测编码(PC)训练算法，属于机器学习/神经科学交叉方向，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：图像分类（CIFAR-10/100, Tiny-ImageNet）；应用场景：生物启发式学习算法；传感器类型：无；目标类别：通用图像分类（非遥感场景）。

Model architecture: VGG-9, ResNet-18等标准分类网络；核心创新在于训练算法（WF-Act-PC），而非网络结构设计。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化方法。论文讨论的是训练过程中的计算局部性（locality），而非模型压缩或推理加速。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文关注训练阶段的计算局部性，而非推理阶段的轻量化部署。

Datasets and metrics: CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, Tiny-ImageNet；评价指标：分类准确率（accuracy）；训练设置：50 epochs, 5 seeds。

Experiments: WF-Act-PC在CIFAR-10上超越iPC（经典PC基线）2.7-22.3个百分点；在深层CIFAR-10架构上匹配或超越调优后的BP基线（VGG-9: 93.57% vs 92.43%; ResNet-18: 92.76% vs 91.54%）；在Tiny-ImageNet上表现良好，但在深层CIFAR-100 VGG上落后于调优BP。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测场景；2. 不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）；3. 实验仅在小型通用图像分类数据集上进行，未验证在遥感大图或复杂检测任务上的有效性；4. 方法依赖权重对称性（weight symmetry）和谱范数控制等假设，实际部署中可能受限。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：1. 如果未来研究在轨训练（on-orbit learning），PC的局部更新特性可能对星上持续学习有参考价值；2. 但本文方法目前仅验证在分类任务上，且未涉及任何轻量化或遥感场景，短期内对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接帮助很小。

Abstract: Predictive Coding (PC) offers a biologically motivated alternative to backpropagation via local weight updates, yet routing error between layers still relies on an autograd Jacobian-transpose ($J^\top$) product - the last non-local operation in PC. We show that this dependency is largely avoidable. For any layer $f(x)=\mathrm{Act}(\mathrm{Norm}(L(x)))$ with frozen normalization statistics, the exact $J^\top$ factors into three locally available terms, $J^\top v = L^\top(s \odot σ'(z) \odot v)$, where $σ'$ is the activation derivative, $z$ is the pre-activation, and $s=γ/σ_{\mathrm{run}}$ is the normalization gain. Prior weight-feedback methods omitted both corrections; restoring them closes the transport gap for this layer class. Locality here holds up to three assumptions, which we state upfront: weight symmetry ($L^\top$ mirrors the forward operator, as assumed by all PC), a soft spectral-norm control that is not synapse-local, and a nearest-neighbour approximation for MaxPool. Substituting the identity into PC yields WF-Act-PC, which removes the autograd backward pass from error transport. On CIFAR-10/100 (50 epochs, 5 seeds), WF-Act-PC is the only PC method whose accuracy improves with depth, surpassing iPC - the strongest classical PC baseline - by 2.7-22.3 pp on CIFAR-10. With both methods tuned per architecture, it matches or exceeds a comparably-tuned backpropagation baseline on the deeper CIFAR-10 architectures (VGG-9: 93.57% vs. 92.43%; ResNet-18: 92.76% vs. 91.54%) and on the harder Tiny-ImageNet benchmark, while trailing tuned BP on the deeper CIFAR-100 VGG cells. Our WF-Act-PC implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/jlshen025/pcax

</details>


### [230] [Where Should RL Post-Training Compute Go? Model Size, Search, Learning, and Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13389)
*Patrick Wilhelm,Odej Kao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究GRPO后训练中固定FLOP预算下计算资源分配策略（模型大小、搜索、学习、反馈之间的权衡），提出RACE诊断协议。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于大语言模型（LLM）的RL后训练计算资源分配问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化模型压缩技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：RL后训练计算预算分配策略分析；应用场景：大语言模型（Qwen2.5）的GRPO训练；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: LoRA微调的Qwen2.5策略模型（大语言模型架构），非视觉/遥感模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等模型轻量化方法。论文讨论的是训练阶段计算预算分配（模型大小vs训练步数vs搜索次数vs反馈计算），而非模型压缩或推理加速。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何星上/边缘部署相关信息。不涉及参数量、FLOPs（仅作为训练预算度量）、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。仅提及使用FLOPs作为计算预算度量单位。

Experiments: 在LoRA适配的Qwen2.5策略上实验，发现最优分配策略随模型大小、计算预算、奖励系统和评估目标变化。提出RACE诊断协议用于识别分配策略。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。论文讨论的是LLM后训练阶段的资源分配，而非推理阶段的模型压缩或边缘部署。

Ideas for my research: 弱借鉴点：论文中"固定预算下资源分配"的视角可类比到星上部署中"固定算力/功耗预算下模型精度与速度的权衡"，但方法本身不直接适用。无实质性可借鉴的轻量化技术或遥感检测方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) post-training is increasingly used to adapt foundation models for reasoning, planning, and feedback-driven robot-learning pipelines, but constrained post-training resources are often summarized by a single total FLOP budget. We study the fixed-budget decision problem behind this practice: under the same post-training budget, should one use a larger policy, train a smaller policy longer, generate more rollout search, or spend compute on stronger reward feedback? We introduce a FLOP-accounting framework for GRPO post-training that decomposes compute into rollout/search, policy-update/learning, and reward- or feedback-model evaluation. Across LoRA-adapted Qwen2.5 policies, we find conditional allocation frontiers: the best observed allocation changes with model size, compute budget, reward system, and evaluation target. Same-FLOP model-size comparisons show that model choice and training allocation are coupled because larger policies consume more per-token compute and therefore buy fewer updates or rollouts under the same budget. Reward systems also change the accounting: rule-based rewards spend nearly all non-update compute on policy rollouts, while PRM-style feedback allocates a visible part of the budget to reward-model inference. We present RACE as a diagnostic pilot-grid protocol, not a guarantee of held-out improvement, for identifying allocation regimes before expensive validation runs; our results suggest that RL post-training papers should report total FLOPs together with how compute is divided among model size, search, learning, and feedback.

</details>


### [231] [Self-Improving is Often Sudden: Enlightenment-style Finetuning for Large-Scale Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13395)
*Jing-Xiao Liao,Tianwei Zhang,Yu-Hao Jiang,Feifei Zhang,Hang-Cheng Dong,Feng-Lei Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种名为Enlightenment的无训练后调优范式，通过修改关键模块/层的捷径连接（不更新权重）来激发大模型的潜在能力，适用于LLM和VLM。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于大语言模型和视觉-语言模型的无训练后调优方法，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化（剪枝/量化/蒸馏等）核心问题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大模型后调优（post-tuning）；应用场景：LLM和VLM的通用能力提升；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 针对LLM提出attention head-mixing shortcuts（注意力头混合捷径），针对VLM在解码器残差连接上应用轻量标量调制因子。整体为训练-free的后调优范式。

Lightweight method: 论文自称"training-free"（无需训练），但这不是传统意义上的模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。它不减少参数量、FLOPs或推理延迟，而是通过修改连接方式来提升性能。与轻量化无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该方法虽声称training-free，但需要修改模型结构，在星上部署场景下实用性存疑。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标，仅提到"across diverse benchmarks and models"。

Experiments: 摘要未提供具体实验结果数值，仅声称"remarkable performance improvements"。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星场景；2. 不涉及目标检测任务；3. 不涉及模型轻量化核心问题（参数量、计算量、推理速度）；4. 缺乏具体实验数据和对比结果；5. 方法依赖大模型架构，与遥感在轨部署的小模型/轻量模型场景不匹配。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴价值极低。唯一可能相关的点是"training-free"思想——如果未来在遥感在轨场景中需要快速调整模型而不重新训练，这种捷径修改思路或许有启发，但论文本身与遥感目标检测轻量化无直接关联。

Abstract: The pursuit of autonomously self-improving models has attracted growing interest in the era of large-scale foundation models. Drawing inspiration from the concept of "enlightenment" or "aha moment" in human brain, we hypothesize that large models exhibit an analogous enlightenment phenomenon-a latent capacity for sudden capability boost. Then, we propose Enlightenment, a novel training-free post-tuning paradigm for large-scale models. Our approach modifies shortcuts for key modules/layers without weight updates, while existing training-free ones predominantly manipulate attention weights. We introduce two architecture-specific instantiations: i) For large language models, we propose attention head-mixing shortcuts that recalibrate attention weights by linking the initial attention head's output to all other target heads, modulated by an adaptive scaling factor initialization strategy. ii) For vision-language models, we apply a lightweight scalar-modulated factor to residual connections in the decoder layers, regulating information flow. Extensive experiments show that Enlightenment efficiently unlocks the latent potential of pre-trained networks, yielding remarkable performance improvements across diverse benchmarks and models.

</details>


### [232] [PiVoT: A Variational Solution for Real-time Large-scale Multi-object Detection and Tracking under Heavy Clutter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13891)
*Runze Gan,Qing Li,Simon J. Godsill,Mike E. Davies,James R. Hopgood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PiVoT，一种基于变分推理的贝叶斯多目标检测与跟踪方法，用于噪声雷达点云，无需训练即可实现端到端检测跟踪，在汽车雷达数据集上达到实时性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于汽车雷达（automotive radar）点云的多目标检测与跟踪，属于地面自动驾驶感知场景，不涉及遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR/高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心要素，也不涉及在轨部署或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多目标检测与跟踪（multi-object detection and tracking）；应用场景：汽车雷达（automotive radar），数据稀缺场景；传感器类型：雷达点云（noisy point clouds, Doppler measurements）；目标类别：摘要未明确说明具体目标类别，泛指雷达探测对象。

Model architecture: PiVoT基于贝叶斯框架和泊松测量模型（Poisson measurement models），采用变分推理（variational inference）进行联合推理，包括目标状态、形状、存在概率、数据关联和测量率。关键创新包括：birth pruning、二次到线性复杂度缩减、高效Doppler泊松模型。

Lightweight method: 论文未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等典型轻量化方法。其效率提升来源于算法层面的变分推理创新（如quadratic-to-linear complexity reductions），而非模型压缩或加速技术。属于算法效率优化，非轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提到real-time operation on full-scale modern automotive radar datasets，但未给出具体参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署指标。且应用场景为汽车雷达而非星上/边缘部署，不涉及FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等硬件加速。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：full-scale modern automotive radar datasets（具体名称摘要未说明）；评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标，仅提及performance comparable to a deep-learning detection benchmark。

Experiments: PiVoT在挑战性场景中显著优于现有贝叶斯跟踪器，展现出对上千个目标的可扩展性、对视觉上不可区分的杂波的鲁棒性，以及在完整汽车雷达数据集上的实时运行能力，性能与深度学习检测基准相当。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星场景完全无关，不涉及遥感图像、SAR、高光谱等；2. 不涉及任何模型轻量化技术（剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）；3. 无参数量、FLOPs、延迟等部署关键指标；4. 方法基于贝叶斯框架，与深度学习目标检测模型差异较大，可迁移性有限。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。可参考的点：1. 其quadratic-to-linear complexity reduction思路可用于优化遥感检测模型中的注意力机制或特征融合模块的计算复杂度；2. 其birth pruning策略可类比于遥感小目标检测中的候选框筛选策略；3. 但整体方法（贝叶斯变分推理）与深度学习轻量化目标检测范式差异较大，直接借鉴价值不高。

Abstract: Multi-object detection and tracking from noisy point clouds remain challenging in many data-scarce radar applications. Current Bayesian trackers based on Poisson measurement models offer a training-free solution but struggle to achieve accuracy and efficiency under severe clutter, large object populations, and full-resolution Doppler point clouds. We address this with PiVoT, a fast, clutter-resilient multi-object tracker for both positional and Doppler measurements. PiVoT performs end-to-end detection and tracking of a large and time-varying number of objects without external clustering or detectors, through joint inference of object states, shapes, existence probabilities, data association, and measurement rates. Its efficiency is driven by several variational inference innovations, such as theoretically justified birth pruning, quadratic-to-linear complexity reductions for exact updates, and a computationally efficient Doppler Poisson model. Experiments show that PiVoT substantially outperforms existing Bayesian trackers in challenging scenes, while also demonstrating exceptional scalability to a thousand objects, robustness to clutter visually inseparable from objects, and real-time operation on full-scale modern automotive radar datasets, where it attains performance comparable to a deep-learning detection benchmark as a training-free joint detector and tracker.

</details>


### [233] [Is the Statistical Advantage Worth the Cost? An Empirical Comparison of KANs and MLPs for Structured Data Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13413)
*Matthew Steven P. Toledo,Justine Raphael H. Jacinto,Vivekjeet Singh Chambal,Rodolfo C. Camaclang,Jamlech Iram N. Gojo Cruz,Reginald Neil C. Recario*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对KAN与MLP在结构化表格分类任务上进行了实证对比，发现KAN在精度上优于MLP但参数量和计算复杂度显著更高。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。该论文研究的是KAN与MLP在结构化表格分类任务上的对比，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。虽然提到了参数量和计算复杂度的权衡，但讨论的是通用表格数据而非遥感视觉任务，且未涉及任何遥感特有的问题（如旋转目标、小目标、多光谱/SAR数据等）。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：结构化表格分类（binary, multiclass, multilabel, ordinal）。应用场景：通用机器学习，非遥感/卫星。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: KAN（Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks）和MLP（Multi-Layer Perceptrons）两种架构对比。KAN采用基于自适应样条（adaptive spline-based mappings）的激活函数替代固定激活函数。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。仅指出MLP在资源受限环境下更高效，KAN参数量和计算复杂度更高。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。仅定性提到KAN具有"substantially higher parameter and computational complexity"，MLP是"robust and efficient option for resource-constrained environments"。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：12个公开结构化表格数据集（具体名称摘要未说明）。评价指标：test accuracy、F1-Score，并进行了paired hypothesis testing和effect size analysis（Cohen's d = -0.46）。

Experiments: KAN在二分类和多分类任务上统计显著优于MLP，整体aggregate advantage显著。但effect size为中等（d=-0.46），说明优势有限且以更高复杂度为代价。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对结构化表格数据，未涉及图像、遥感等视觉模态；2. 未报告具体参数量、FLOPs、推理时间等量化指标；3. 未进行轻量化或部署相关实验；4. 未讨论在边缘/星上场景的可行性。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。虽然KAN作为一种新型架构可能在未来被探索用于遥感目标检测，但本文仅针对表格数据，且KAN的高复杂度与轻量化需求相悖。如果未来有研究将KAN应用于遥感视觉任务并探索其轻量化变体，才可能具有参考价值。

Abstract: This study presents an empirical benchmarking comparison between Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) and Multi-Layer Perceptrons (MLPs) on structured tabular classification tasks. Motivated by the growing interest in KANs as an alternative function-approximating architecture, we evaluate their out-of-the-box performance on twelve publicly available datasets spanning binary, multiclass, multilabel, and ordinal problems. Both models were trained under standardized preprocessing, architecture, and fixed hyperparameter settings, with performance assessed using test accuracy and F1-Score, paired hypothesis testing, and effect size analysis. Results show that KANs statistically outperform MLPs in binary and multiclass domains and achieve a significant aggregate advantage across all datasets. However, the observed medium effect size (d = -0.46) raises an important cost-benefit consideration: while KANs offer superior generalization through adaptive spline-based mappings, this advantage comes with substantially higher parameter and computational complexity relative to the MLP baseline. These findings suggest KANs are the preferred choice for high-precision applications, while MLPs remain a robust and efficient option for resource-constrained environments. Future work should extend this analysis to additional data modalities to further refine these architectural selection criteria.

</details>


### [234] [EXPLORE: Exploration with Guided Search for Analog Topology Generation using Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13416)
*Guanglei Zhou,Chen-Chia Chang,Yikang Shen,Jonathan Ku,Isaac Jacobson,Jingyu Pan,Yiran Chen,Xin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EXPLORE框架，将蒙特卡洛树搜索与Transformer解码结合，用于模拟电路拓扑的自动化生成，提升复杂电路设计成功率。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于模拟电路拓扑自动化设计（analog circuit topology design），属于电子设计自动化（EDA）领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：模拟电路拓扑生成；场景：电子设计自动化；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于Transformer的序列到序列解码器，结合蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）进行测试时搜索增强。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及模型轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 6-component benchmark电路基准；评价指标：成功率（success rate）、均方误差（MSE）。

Experiments: 在6-component基准上，EXPLORE将成功率从单次生成的12%和采样-过滤基线的33%提升至65%，在相同搜索预算下MSE降低超过20%。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化信息。

Ideas for my research: 无直接借鉴点。该论文属于EDA领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: Automating analog circuit topology design is essential to reduce the extensive manual effort required to meet increasingly diverse and customized application demands. Recent advances have applied sequence-to-sequence fine-tuning on pretrained language models to directly generate circuit topologies from user specifications in a single pass. However, these one-shot generation methods failed to generate complex circuits due to their exponentially growing search spaces and limited training datasets. In this paper, we present EXPLORE, a search-enhanced framework that integrates simulator-guided Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) with transformer-based decoding to enable test-time scaling for analog topology generation. By leveraging language-model priors and bypassing high-confidence structural tokens, EXPLORE allocates expensive simulator budget primarily toward topology-altering decisions during search. On a 6-component benchmark at a tight tolerance of 0.01, EXPLORE raises the success rate from 12% for one-shot generation and 33% for a sampling-and-filter baseline to 65%, and lowers MSE by over 20% relative to sampling-and-filter under the same search budget. These results establish EXPLORE as the first framework to integrate structured test-time search with LM decoding for analog topology generation, and a practical step toward scaling LLM-driven design automation.

</details>


### [235] [OrDA: Orthogonal Disentanglement of Access Habits Framework for Homepage Marketing Block Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13420)
*Lingxiao Zhang,Xiaobo Li,Tao Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OrDA框架，通过正交解耦访问习惯与内容兴趣，消除推荐系统中的伪正样本偏差。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是推荐系统中的点击偏差解耦问题，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：推荐系统点击率预测；应用场景：首页营销块推荐、租房推荐；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 双塔结构（dual-tower）+ 门控分配层（gated allocation layer）+ 正交正则化（orthogonal regularization）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 大规模推荐系统数据集（具体名称未说明）；UCTR（用户点击率）；在线AB测试。

Experiments: 在线AB测试显示UCTR提升5.64%，优于SOTA方法。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术细节。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于推荐系统领域，与遥感目标检测轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Clicks on homepage marketing blocks are driven by a dual-mechanism of content interest and access habits. However, habitual clicks often create Pseudo-Positives in marketing slots, where position advantage masks mediocre content quality, leading to biased recommendation ecosystems. We propose a framework called Orthogonal Disentanglement of Access habits (OrDA) to purify interest signals. OrDA utilizes a dual-tower structure with a gated allocation layer to adaptively route features and minimize interference. To ensure rigorous separation, we employ orthogonal regularization to constrain the latent interest and habit manifolds to be geometrically perpendicular. OrDA performs causal intervention (do-calculus) during inference to rank items solely by purified interest scores. Empirical online evaluations on large-scale datasets demonstrate that OrDA effectively eliminates access-habit bias, outperforming state-of-the-art methods in predictive accuracy. Online AB test 5.64% shows user click-through rates (UCTR) improvement on the Zhima homepage marketing block, Zhima rent-floor recommendation.

</details>


### [236] [Temperature Scaling Is Not Enough: Calibration Gaps Under Human Label Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13423)
*Wisdom Dogah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究温度缩放（temperature scaling）在软标签（soft label）场景下的校准失效问题，发现硬标签校准存在系统性软标签校准差距，且该差距随模型规模增大而增大。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于深度学习模型的后验概率校准（post-hoc calibration）方法，讨论温度缩放（temperature scaling）在软标签分布下的失效问题。完全不涉及遥感（remote sensing）、卫星（satellite）、目标检测（object detection）、轻量化（lightweight）或在轨部署（onboard deployment）等任何相关主题。数据集使用CIFAR-10H和ChaosNLI，均为通用视觉/语言领域数据集。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：模型校准（model calibration）；应用场景：通用深度学习分类任务；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。摘要未说明遥感相关场景。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。仅提及三种模型规模（three model scales per modality），但未给出具体网络名称或结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是后验校准方法（temperature scaling, multiclass isotonic regression），与模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等完全无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。仅提及模型规模（model scale）对校准差距的影响，但未给出具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CIFAR-10H（视觉领域，含人类标注软标签分布）、ChaosNLI（自然语言推理，含SNLI和MNLI两个子集，含软标签分布）。评价指标：Brier Score（校准差距），对比硬标签校准与软标签oracle校准之间的Brier Score差距。

Experiments: 在视觉和语言两个模态共9种配置下实验，发现硬标签校准在软标签场景下存在系统性校准差距（Brier Score差距0.002~0.134），该差距在视觉领域和SNLI子集上随模型规模单调增长，语言领域差距（均值0.079）远大于视觉领域（均值0.003）。多类别等渗回归（multiclass isotonic regression）作为第二基线得到相同定性结论。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化，与课题方向无直接关联；2. MNLI子集上出现规模排序反转（scale-ordering reversal），作者认为该子集上模型准确率接近随机水平，结论不具说服力；3. 仅讨论校准问题，未涉及模型压缩或加速。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：在遥感目标检测中，如果使用软标签（如多标注者不一致的舰船/飞机标注），温度缩放等校准方法可能失效，需注意校准协议的选择。但该论文未涉及遥感场景，也未讨论检测任务，因此对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接借鉴意义很小。

Abstract: Temperature scaling is the dominant post-hoc calibration method in modern deep learning. Its theoretical justification rests on an assumption that is rarely stated explicitly: that ground-truth labels are one-hot and deterministic. In practice, labels are frequently soft, crowd-sourced, or genuinely distributional, reflecting real disagreement among human annotators rather than annotation noise. We study whether temperature scaling retains its calibration properties when this assumption is violated, and whether any resulting degradation depends on model scale. Using CIFAR-10H and ChaosNLI, two publicly available datasets with human-annotated soft label distributions, we evaluate three model scales per modality under both hard one-hot and soft distributional label targets. Across all nine configurations we find a positive soft-label calibration gap: temperature scaling calibrated on hard labels consistently underperforms an oracle calibrated directly on soft labels, with Brier Score gaps ranging from 0.002 to 0.134. The gap grows monotonically with model scale in the vision domain and on the SNLI-derived split of ChaosNLI, and is substantially larger in the language domain (mean gap 0.079) than in vision (mean gap 0.003). A scale-ordering reversal on the MNLI-derived split remains after matched-domain training; we treat it as inconclusive for the scale hypothesis and attribute it primarily to near-chance accuracy on that split. As a second post-hoc baseline, multiclass isotonic regression yields the same qualitative conclusion: positive soft-label gaps in all nine configurations, and larger gaps in language than in vision. These findings suggest that calibration protocols built on majority-vote labels systematically misstate model reliability wherever label ambiguity is structural, with direct consequences for deployment in safety-critical settings.

</details>


### [237] [Data-Efficient Adaptation of LLMs via Attention Head Reweighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13425)
*Tuomas Oikarinen,Zixiao Chen,Charlotte Siska,Tsui-Wei Weng,Chandan Singh,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Attention Head Reweighting (AHR)方法，通过为每个注意力头学习一个标量权重来高效适应LLM到新文本分类任务，在少样本场景下以极少的可训练参数超越LoRA。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于大语言模型(LLM)的少样本文本分类任务，属于自然语言处理(NLP)领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。虽然涉及参数高效微调（可视为一种轻量化适应方法），但应用场景、数据类型（文本 vs 遥感图像）、任务目标（文本分类 vs 目标检测）均与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：少样本文本分类；应用场景：安全等领域的文本分析；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：文本分类任务，不涉及视觉目标。

Model architecture: 基于Transformer的LLM，核心操作是对每个注意力头学习一个可训练的标量权重（reweighting），仅修改约0.0001%的模型参数。

Lightweight method: 参数高效微调（Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning），具体为Attention Head Reweighting (AHR)，每个注意力头仅学习一个标量，可训练参数比LoRA少200-1000倍。但这属于NLP领域的微调方法，并非面向边缘/星上部署的模型压缩技术（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：多种开源文本分类数据集（具体名称摘要未说明）；评价指标：文本分类准确率等（具体指标摘要未说明）。

Experiments: 在少样本场景下，AHR在多个文本分类数据集上优于LoRA等基线方法，且可训练参数减少200-1000倍。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对NLP文本分类任务，与遥感视觉任务无关；2. 未涉及目标检测、旋转框检测等遥感核心任务；3. 未讨论模型推理速度、FLOPs、部署硬件适配等工程问题；4. 少样本学习设定与遥感在轨场景的实时推理需求不匹配。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：注意力头功能特化的思想（不同注意力头关注不同特征）可类比到遥感检测模型中的通道/特征层重要性分析，但方法本身（为每个头学一个标量）是针对LLM文本任务的，直接迁移到遥感目标检测模型的意义不大。

Abstract: Learning effectively from limited data is critical in domains like security where labeled examples are scarce. Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated some capabilities for data-efficient learning, especially through parameter-efficient adaptation methods, but continue to struggle when faced with few samples for difficult tasks. To meet this challenge, we propose Attention Head Reweighting (AHR), a data-efficient method that adapts LLMs to new text-classification tasks by learning only a single scalar per attention head. This drastically reduces the number of parameters that need to be learned by making use of the functional specialization of individual attention heads. Experiments on diverse open-source text classification datasets show that AHR can outperform standard baselines like LoRA when learning from limited samples, despite having 200-1000x fewer trainable parameters, as our AHR only modifies ~0.0001% of the model's parameters. In addition, our learned weights are easy to interpret and can be analyzed to better understand the mechanisms and attention heads responsible for in-context learning abilities in LLMs.

</details>


### [238] [PUe: Biased Positive-Unlabeled Learning Enhancement by Causal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13428)
*Xutao Wang,Hanting Chen,Tianyu Guo,Yunhe Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于归一化逆概率加权（NIPW）的PU学习增强框架（PUe），解决正负样本标签分布不均导致的标注偏差问题，在MNIST、CIFAR-10、ADNI上验证了有效性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于PU（Positive-Unlabeled）学习这一通用机器学习问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测、轻量化、在轨部署等核心主题。虽然摘要中出现了"SAR-PU"字样，但这里的SAR指的是"Selected At Random"（随机选择）假设，而非Synthetic Aperture Radar（合成孔径雷达）。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"课题方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：PU二分类（Positive-Unlabeled binary classification）；应用场景：通用机器学习，非遥感场景；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。基于Bekker等人的SAR-PU propensity-weighted框架，引入归一化倾向性评分和归一化逆概率加权（NIPW），结合正则化深度倾向性评分估计，并与现代cost-sensitive PU方法集成。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩或加速方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MNIST、CIFAR-10、ADNI（医学影像数据集）；评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标，仅提及"improvements over several PU baselines under non-uniform label distributions"。

Experiments: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10、ADNI三个数据集上，与非均匀标签分布下的多个PU基线方法进行了对比，PUe框架取得了改进。摘要未提供具体数值结果。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化方向完全无关；2. 仅在通用小规模数据集（MNIST、CIFAR-10）和医学影像（ADNI）上验证，未在遥感图像上测试；3. 未讨论计算效率或部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义非常有限。唯一可能的间接联系是：遥感目标检测中也可能存在正样本标注不足、负样本（背景）大量未标注的情况（即PU学习场景），但本文方法（NIPW、归一化倾向性评分）主要解决标签分布偏差问题，与轻量化、在轨部署无关。不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Positive-Unlabeled (PU) learning aims to achieve high-accuracy binary classification with limited labeled positive examples and numerous unlabeled ones. Existing cost-sensitive-based methods often rely on strong assumptions that examples with an observed positive label were selected entirely at random. In fact, the uneven distribution of labels is prevalent in real-world PU problems, indicating that most actual positive and unlabeled data are subject to selection bias. Building on the SAR-PU propensity-weighted framework of Bekker et al., we study a PU learning enhancement (PUe) framework using normalized propensity scores and normalized inverse probability weighting (NIPW). PUe's main contributions are a normalized inverse-probability-weighted PU risk formulation; additional theoretical analyses of normalized sample-weight error and common PU estimators under biased labeling; regularized deep propensity-score estimation; integration with modern cost-sensitive PU methods; and support for selectively labeled negative classes. Experiments on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and ADNI demonstrate improvements over several PU baselines under non-uniform label distributions.

</details>


### [239] [Discrete Diffusion Models: A Unified Framework from Tokenization to Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13431)
*Ye Yuan,Weien Li,Rui Song,Zeyu Li,Haochen Liu,Xiangyu Kong,Zixuan Dong,Linfeng Du,Zipeng Sun,Weixu Zhang,Jiaxin Huang,Changjiang Han,Yonghan Yang,Zichen Zhao,Xiuyuan Hu,Haolun Wu,Yankai Chen,Fengran Mo,Jikun Kang,Bowei He,Philip S. Yu,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个统一的概念框架，从离散状态空间构建的角度审视离散去噪扩散模型（DDMs），分析不同公式化方法（转移矩阵、掩码/吸收态、分数/比率方法）的设计权衡，并指出未来研究方向。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文是纯生成式模型（离散扩散模型）的理论框架性工作，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署中的任何一个核心要素。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文是离散扩散模型的理论综述/框架性工作，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：离散数据生成（文本、离散序列等）的理论框架分析；应用场景：通用生成式AI；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 离散去噪扩散模型（DDM），包括transition-matrix、masking/absorbing-state、score/ratio-based等多种公式化方法。论文从离散状态空间构建（tokenization方案、词汇拓扑、领域特定结构字母表）的角度统一分析这些方法。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。论文讨论的是离散扩散模型的理论框架和设计空间，不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。论文是理论框架性工作，未提及实验设置。

Experiments: 摘要未说明实验结果或对比实验。论文是概念性框架分析，不包含实验验证。

Limitations: 该论文是纯理论框架工作，缺乏实验验证；与遥感、目标检测、轻量化部署完全无关，无法直接应用于在轨遥感场景。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。如果一定要找关联点：离散扩散模型中的"迭代全局精炼"（iterative global refinement）思想，或许可以启发遥感目标检测中的后处理优化或检测结果精炼策略；但整体上该论文与在轨遥感目标检测轻量化课题几乎没有交集。

Abstract: Discrete denoising diffusion models (DDMs) have recently emerged as a compelling alternative to autoregressive (AR) modeling for discrete data, offering parallel generation and iterative global refinement capabilities. Unlike continuous diffusion, where the state space is fixed, DDMs are fundamentally shaped by how the discrete state space is constructed: the tokenization scheme, the vocabulary topology, and domain-specific structural alphabets. This work introduces a unified conceptual framework that views discrete diffusion models through the construction of the underlying discrete state space. Within this framework, existing formulations, including transition-matrix, masking/absorbing-state, and score/ratio-based approaches, emerge as different instantiations of a common design space. The framework further exposes common design trade-offs across training objectives, inference algorithms, scaling behavior, systems optimization, and evaluation protocols, suggesting several promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [240] [Local Redundancy: An Information-Theoretic Measure of Plasticity from Synthetic Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13432)
*Jiaxuan Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于信息论的"局部冗余度"指标来衡量神经网络的可塑性（plasticity），用于预测模型在持续学习和迁移学习中的下游性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于神经网络的可塑性度量（plasticity），属于持续学习/迁移学习的理论性研究，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：持续图像分类、时间序列迁移学习；应用场景：通用机器学习；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，讨论的是通用神经网络的局部参数族（local model family）的可塑性度量。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 实验任务：持续图像分类、时间序列迁移学习；评价指标：下游任务性能（具体数据集名称摘要未说明）。

Experiments: 实验表明局部冗余度比现有指标（有效秩、死亡神经元比例、权重范数）能更好地预测下游性能，并可用于预训练检查点选择。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。该论文是纯理论性的可塑性度量研究，不涉及任何遥感场景、目标检测任务或模型压缩部署问题。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。如果未来研究涉及持续学习/增量学习场景下的遥感目标检测模型（例如卫星不断接收新类别数据），该论文提出的局部冗余度指标可能用于评估模型在新任务上的适应能力。但与本课题"轻量化"核心方向无直接关联。

Abstract: Plasticity -- a neural network's ability to adapt to new tasks -- is critical for continual and transfer learning. Existing measures, such as effective rank, dead neuron fraction, and weight norm, lack theoretical grounding and correlate poorly with performance on new tasks. We introduce local redundancy, an information-theoretic measure derived from universal compression theory. We define local redundancy as the worst-case redundancy of a local model family -- parameters in an infinitesimal neighborhood along gradient directions -- and show this is a principled measure of plasticity. Although local redundancy is intractable to compute exactly, we prove that the expected squared gradient norm on a synthetic memorization task provides an efficiently computable lower bound. Experiments on continual image classification and time series transfer learning demonstrate that local redundancy predicts downstream performance better than existing measures and enables pretraining checkpoint selection where validation loss plateaus.

</details>


### [241] [Distributionally Robust and Safe Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13436)
*Ahmed Aboudonia,Naira Hovakimyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种分布鲁棒且安全的模仿学习框架，结合Taylor级数模仿学习(TaSIL)和分布鲁棒自适应控制，处理策略诱导和不确定性诱导的分布偏移，并在无人机避障任务上验证。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。虽然涉及无人机(UAV)和不确定环境，但核心是模仿学习的分布鲁棒性与安全性问题，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分，与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：模仿学习(Imitation Learning)下的安全决策控制；应用场景：无人机在不确定环境中的避障任务；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：摘要未说明（非目标检测任务）。

Model architecture: 框架包含两个核心组件：1) Taylor Series Imitation Learning (TaSIL) — 处理策略诱导的分布偏移；2) 分布鲁棒自适应控制 — 处理不确定性诱导的分布偏移。整体为控制与学习的联合框架，非视觉检测模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。虽然无人机场景有边缘部署潜力，但论文未讨论。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。仅在UAV案例研究中验证，但未给出定量指标。

Experiments: 摘要仅提及在UAV案例上验证了方法有效性（无人机在不确定环境中执行任务并避开不安全区域），未给出具体实验结果、对比方法或消融实验。

Limitations: 1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2) 未涉及任何视觉感知或目标检测任务；3) 未讨论模型效率或部署可行性；4) 仅在UAV仿真/案例上验证，缺乏遥感场景实验。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的交叉点是：如果未来在轨遥感任务涉及星上自主决策（如卫星自主调整观测目标），其分布鲁棒性思想可能有一定参考价值，但与当前"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接关联性极低。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL) has achieved remarkable success in complex decision-making tasks. However, its performance is highly sensitive to distribution shifts, which can pose significant safety risks. We propose a distributionally robust and safe IL framework that explicitly addresses both policy-induced and uncertainty-induced distribution shifts. Our approach develops a unified framework leveraging Taylor Series Imitation Learning (TaSIL) to mitigate policy-induced shifts and distributionally robust adaptive control to handle uncertainty-induced shifts. This architecture enables the formulation of an IL problem that optimizes performance under distributional uncertainty while systematically accounting for safety constraints. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach on an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) case study where the UAV performs a task in an uncertain environment while avoiding unsafe regions.

</details>


### [242] [PQFA: Parallel Quantum Feature Augmentation of Fused Representations for Multimodal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13466)
*Mingzhu Wang,Yun Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出并行量子特征增强（PQFA）框架，在融合后的多模态特征上使用浅层变分量子电路进行后融合增强，在MM-IMDb和N24News上以极少参数提升多模态分类性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦多模态学习（文本+图像）的后融合增强，使用量子-经典混合方法，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱/多光谱图像、目标检测或轻量化部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多模态分类（文本+图像）；应用场景：通用多模态理解（MM-IMDb电影评论、N24News新闻）；传感器类型：无遥感传感器；目标类别：无遥感目标。摘要未说明任何遥感相关场景。

Model architecture: 使用冻结的RoBERTa（文本编码器）和ViT（图像编码器），通过双向交叉注意力、注意力池化和自适应门控融合得到融合特征，再将融合特征振幅编码到多个并行浅层变分量子电路中，量子测量输出与经典表示拼接后用于预测。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等传统轻量化方法。虽然量子分支参数量少（约2.2K vs MLP的24.0K），但这是量子-经典混合框架本身的特性，并非针对遥感模型轻量化设计，且量子电路在真实星上部署的可行性极低。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。量子电路在模拟噪声环境下测试，未涉及真实硬件部署或边缘/星上推理场景。量子计算硬件目前不适用于星上实时推理。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MM-IMDb、N24News（多模态分类数据集，非遥感数据集）；评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标（如准确率等），仅提到"consistently outperforms"；实验设置：控制相同编码器、融合骨干、数据划分、投影维度和增强输出宽度进行对比。

Experiments: 主要结果：PQFA优于无量子增强的融合骨干和宽度匹配的MLP增强基线；缺失模态实验显示对文本或视觉输入不完整时鲁棒性提升；消融实验表明改进不能由随机特征映射、增加经典宽度或未训练的量子变换复现；量子态诊断显示在模拟噪声水平下性能稳定。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感场景和目标检测任务；2. 量子电路在真实硬件上的推理效率和部署可行性未讨论；3. 仅在模拟噪声下测试，未在真实量子硬件上验证；4. 参数量虽少但量子计算资源开销可能很大；5. 与在轨遥感目标检测的轻量化需求完全脱节。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能值得注意的点是：量子分支以极少的可训练参数（2.2K）实现了性能提升，这种"参数高效增强"的思路在理论上可启发轻量化设计，但量子计算硬件在星上部署的可行性在可预见的未来几乎不存在，不建议作为在轨轻量化的研究方向。

Abstract: Most multimodal learning methods improve how heterogeneous representations are aligned and fused, while post-fusion enhancement remains less explored. We propose Parallel Quantum Feature Augmentation (PQFA), a hybrid quantum-classical framework that applies multiple shallow variational quantum circuits to fused multimodal features. Text and image representations extracted by frozen RoBERTa and ViT encoders are processed through bidirectional cross-attention, attentive pooling, and adaptive gated fusion. The fused feature is then amplitude-encoded into parallel quantum circuits, whose measurement readouts are concatenated with the classical representation for prediction. We evaluate PQFA on MM-IMDb and N24News through controlled comparisons using the same encoders, fusion backbone, data splits, projection dimension, and augmentation output width. PQFA consistently outperforms both the fusion backbone without quantum augmentation and a width-matched MLP augmentation baseline, while using approximately 2.2K augmentation parameters compared with 24.0K for the MLP branch. Missing-modality experiments further show improved robustness when textual or visual inputs are incomplete, with particularly clear gains when the more informative textual modality is severely degraded. Controlled ablations and feature-space analyses indicate that the improvement cannot be reproduced by random feature mappings, increased classical width, or untrained quantum transformations. Quantum-state diagnostics additionally show stable predictive performance across the tested simulated noise levels and distinct branch-specific transformations of the encoded states. These results establish PQFA as an effective and parameter-efficient strategy for post-fusion augmentation in hybrid quantum-classical multimodal learning.

</details>


### [243] [Explainable Artificial Intelligence for Anomaly Detection in Banking Transactions: An Internal Audit Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13469)
*Anupa Lodhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个面向银行交易异常检测的可解释AI框架，使用Isolation Forest + SHAP + Streamlit仪表盘，在合成数据集上取得0.91精度和0.88召回率。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦银行金融交易异常检测与可解释AI，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感领域内容；也不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等视觉检测任务；虽然提到"lightweight"（轻量级Streamlit仪表盘），但这是指前端展示的轻量化，与模型剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等模型轻量化技术无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，无需阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：银行交易异常检测（anomaly detection）；场景：内部审计流程；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：银行交易欺诈行为。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: Isolation Forest (iForest) 无监督异常检测模型 + SHAP解释层 + Streamlit前端仪表盘。不涉及任何视觉模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要中"lightweight"仅指Streamlit仪表盘的轻量级前端实现，并非模型层面的轻量化技术（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。无模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。讨论的是金融合规场景下的部署，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：合成银行交易数据集（synthetic banking dataset）；评价指标：Precision=0.91, Recall=0.88，与三个无监督基线对比。与遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、NWPU VHR-10等）完全无关。

Experiments: 在合成数据集上iForest+SHAP框架以0.91 precision和0.88 recall优于三个无监督基线；专家反馈确认特征级解释提升了审计人员信心和决策质量。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术内容；2. 使用合成数据而非真实数据，泛化性存疑；3. 仅涉及异常检测而非目标检测；4. 无模型轻量化技术细节。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于金融AI可解释性方向，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题在任务、场景、技术路线、评价体系上均无交集。不建议参考。

Abstract: The banking sector increasingly relies on automated systems to monitor electronic transactions for signs of fraud, yet conventional rule-based approaches struggle with high false-positive rates and offer no justification for their outputs, limiting their utility for compliance teams. This paper introduces an Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) framework tailored for banking transaction anomaly detection within internal audit workflows. An Isolation Forest (iForest) model performs unsupervised anomaly scoring, while a SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations) layer provides transaction-level, feature-attributed explanations grounded in cooperative game theory [8]. A lightweight Streamlit dashboard renders these outputs in a form accessible to audit professionals without machine learning expertise. Evaluation on a synthetic banking dataset yields 0.91 precision and 0.88 recall, outperforming three unsupervised baselines. Expert feedback confirms that feature-level explanations measurably improve auditor confidence and decision quality. The framework advances the practical deployment of accountable, transparent AI in regulated financial environments.

</details>


### [244] [DeepLoop: Depth Scaling for Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13491)
*Shuzhen Li,Yifan Zhang,Jiacheng Guo,Quanquan Gu,Mengdi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepLoop方法，解决循环Transformer（Looped Transformers）中参数共享导致的残差缩放问题，通过形式化访问对齐系数并调整DeepNorm指数来实现稳定训练，主要面向语言模型。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于NLP领域的循环Transformer训练稳定性问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：语言模型训练稳定性分析；应用场景：GPT-style语言模型（GPT-2 small/medium）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: Looped Transformers（循环Transformer），Post-LN DeepNorm架构，通过重复使用同一组物理块来增加未折叠深度而不增加存储参数。

Lightweight method: 论文涉及参数共享（weight sharing/tying）——通过循环使用同一组物理块来减少参数量，但这并非面向轻量化部署的剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS等方法，而是NLP中提升模型深度效率的手段。未涉及星上部署相关轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然参数共享可减少存储参数，但循环推理会增加计算开销，不直接适用于星上实时推理场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：GPT-2 small和GPT-2 medium规模的语言模型训练；评价指标：validation loss, downstream accuracy。

Experiments: 实验表明：当无物理块被重复访问时DeepLoop效果中性；当循环深度激活时，DeepLoop改善了验证损失和下游准确率。

Limitations: 1. 仅验证了NLP领域的语言模型，未涉及视觉/遥感任务；2. 循环推理增加推理延迟，不适合实时在轨场景；3. 未讨论模型在边缘/星上部署的可行性；4. 未涉及目标检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。但参数共享（weight tying）作为一种减少存储参数量的思路，理论上可启发遥感目标检测模型的轻量化设计——例如在检测骨干网络中共享某些层的权重以减少模型存储。然而，循环推理带来的计算开销增加与在轨实时推理需求相悖，需谨慎评估。

Abstract: Looped Transformers scale sequential computation by applying a compact stack of physical blocks for multiple rounds, increasing unrolled depth without increasing stored parameters. This reuse changes the residual-scaling problem: in an untied Transformer, each residual branch receives and applies its own parameter update, whereas in a looped Transformer one shared update aggregates gradients from repeated visits and is read back by those same visits in the next linearized forward pass. We formalize this tied-depth effect through a first-order perturbation bound controlled by a visit-alignment coefficient $κ_R$. The bound recovers the DeepNorm exponent when visits decorrelate, but in the conservative aligned regime it requires the exponent to increase from $1/4$ to $1/2$ as loop count grows at fixed physical depth. The resulting method, \textbf{DeepLoop}, keeps the Post-LN DeepNorm architecture and sets $α=(2N)^{1/2}$ and $β=(8N)^{-1/2}$ for unrolled depth $N$. On GPT-style looped language models at GPT-2 small and GPT-2 medium scale, DeepLoop is neutral when no physical block is revisited and improves validation loss and downstream accuracy once recurrent depth is activated. These results show that stable recurrent depth requires residual scaling rules that account for parameter visits, not only nominal layer count.

</details>


### [245] [A VAE-Driven Multi-Task Satellite-Aided Semantic Communication Framework for 6G-Enabled Connected Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13494)
*S. M. Abtahiul Alam,Niloy Das,Apurba Adhikary,Yu Qiao,Zhu Han,Choong Seon Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于VAE的多任务语义通信框架，用于卫星辅助自动驾驶中的交通标志重建与分类，实现87%-98%的带宽压缩。

Research relevance: 2分——弱相关。论文涉及卫星信道通信和交通标志识别，但核心是语义通信框架而非遥感目标检测模型，且不涉及模型轻量化（剪枝/量化/蒸馏等）。卫星仅作为通信链路背景，非遥感成像任务。

Reading priority: 1分——与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关。核心是通信层面的语义编码，非遥感成像目标检测，也未涉及模型轻量化技术。仅卫星信道背景有微弱关联，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：语义通信（semantic communication）+ 交通标志重建与分类（traffic sign reconstruction & classification）；应用场景：卫星辅助自动驾驶（satellite-assisted autonomous driving）；传感器类型：摘要未说明（非遥感成像传感器）；目标类别：交通标志。

Model architecture: 基于变分自编码器（VAE）的多任务语义通信框架，使用概率潜变量表示（probabilistic latent representations）替代确定性自编码器，端到端联合优化重建与分类任务。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及模型轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。带宽压缩是通过语义通信（仅传输任务相关特征）实现的通信层面压缩，而非模型压缩。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅报告了带宽压缩率（87.23%-98.17%）和在不同信噪比下的稳定性，这是通信层面的指标，非模型部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标：带宽压缩率（bandwidth reduction 87.23%-98.17%）、不同信噪比下的稳定性能（stable performance across varying SNR）。

Experiments: 摘要仅报告了带宽压缩率范围（87.23%-98.17%）和在不同信噪比下的性能稳定性，未提供具体数值对比或消融实验。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感在轨目标检测无直接关系，属于通信领域工作；2. 未涉及任何模型轻量化技术；3. 未报告模型复杂度、推理速度等部署关键指标；4. 交通标志识别场景与遥感目标检测（舰船/飞机/车辆）差异大；5. 未说明在真实卫星信道上的实验验证。

Ideas for my research: 1. 语义通信的思想（只传输任务相关特征而非完整信号）可启发遥感在轨场景中"星地协同"的带宽优化思路，但与本课题"模型轻量化"核心方向关联弱；2. VAE的概率潜变量表示方法可借鉴用于遥感特征压缩，但需结合轻量化模型设计；3. 整体借鉴价值有限，建议优先阅读遥感目标检测轻量化相关论文。

Abstract: The development of smart transportation systems and the introduction of 6G wireless communication technologies have significantly changed vehicle network topologies. Future connected autonomous vehicle (CAV) networks require bandwidth-efficient, reliable, and low-latency communication for safety-critical applications such as traffic sign recognition and decision-making.
