Conventional communication systems transmit raw data regardless of task relevance, which is inefficient in resource-constrained satellite channels where uplink bandwidth is scarce and propagation losses are large. Semantic communication addresses this limitation by transmitting task-relevant information instead of full signal representations. It extracts and conveys essential semantic features and leverages deep learning to optimize task performance at the receiver. Therefore, we present a Variational Autoencoder (VAE)-based multi-task semantic communication framework for satellite-assisted autonomous driving. Unlike deterministic autoencoder-based methods, the proposed model uses probabilistic latent representations for more robust and efficient encoding. The learned features are transmitted over noisy wireless channels to perform traffic sign reconstruction and classification. The framework is trained end-to-end to jointly optimize both tasks. Results show that the proposed approach achieves significant bandwidth reduction of up to 87.23\% to 98.17\% while maintaining stable performance across varying signal-to-noise ratio conditions.

</details>


### [246] [Factorized Spectral Representations for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13498)
*Junyi Wu,Dan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FaStR方法，利用CP分解将转移核建模为三模张量，通过噪声对比学习获得分解式谱表示，用于样本高效的深度强化学习。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于深度强化学习中的谱表示学习与样本效率问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：连续控制中的表示学习（representation learning for continuous control）；应用场景：深度强化学习（deep reinforcement learning）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: FaStR基于CP分解（CANDECOMP/PARAFAC decomposition）将转移核分解为三个模态的特征映射，分别对应state encoder、action encoder、next-state encoder，三者共同构成谱表示。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 实验任务：高维运动控制任务（high-dimensional locomotion tasks）；评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标，提及样本效率提升和state encoder在actuator shift下的迁移能力。

Experiments: FaStR在高维运动控制任务上取得最大增益，其动力学与分解结构对齐；学到的state encoder可在actuator shift下完整迁移，仅需重新训练action encoder。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关。论文未涉及任何视觉目标检测、遥感图像处理或模型压缩部署内容。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于深度强化学习中的表示学习，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题的研究范式、任务目标和技术路线均不匹配。

Abstract: Learning a compact model of the world from interaction data is central to sample-efficient deep reinforcement learning. Spectral representation methods have become the leading paradigm for representation learning in continuous control by taking a matrix view of the transition kernel, with state-action pairs on one side and next states on the other, and learning a low-rank factorization through self-supervised contrastive objectives. We take this view one step further. The transition kernel is naturally a three-mode tensor over states, actions, and next states, and a CP decomposition gives one feature map per mode. We propose FaStR, which fits this decomposition with a noise contrastive objective, producing separate state, action, and next-state encoders that together form a single spectral representation. The factored form yields a smaller hypothesis class, and the sample size needed for representation learning shrinks by a factor that scales with the smaller of the state and action dimensions. Empirically, FaStR delivers its largest gains on high-dimensional locomotion tasks whose dynamics align with the factored structure, and the learned state encoder transfers intact across actuator shift while only the action encoder is retrained.

</details>


### [247] [CDS: Counterfactual Directionality Score for Structured Interventions in Spatial Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13508)
*Humaira Anzum,Md Ishtyaq Mahmud,Jagan Mohan Reddy Dwarampudi,Tania Banerjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于结构化反事实干预的图模型框架，用于量化节点群体间的方向性影响，主要应用于空间生物学系统（如细胞-细胞相互作用）。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于空间生物学系统中的细胞间方向性影响量化，属于图神经网络在生物信息学中的应用，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：方向性影响量化（directional influence estimation）；应用场景：空间生物学系统（spatial biological systems）、空间转录组学（spatial transcriptomics）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：细胞节点（cell populations）。

Model architecture: Neighbor Influence Model (NIM)——基于局部邻域预测节点状态的图模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：合成空间图数据（synthetic spatial graphs with known directional structure）、空间转录组学数据（spatial transcriptomics data）；评价指标：Counterfactual Directionality Score (CDS)、校准性（calibration under null conditions）、鲁棒性（robustness to confounding signals）。

Experiments: 合成数据实验表明CDS能恢复方向性影响、在零假设下校准良好、对混杂信号鲁棒；空间转录组学初步结果揭示生物上合理且一致的相互作用。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的检测或轻量化方法。论文仅针对生物学空间数据分析。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。论文不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署等任何与课题相关的内容。

Abstract: Quantifying directional influence between node populations is a fundamental problem in graph-based modeling, particularly in spatial biological systems where cell-cell interactions shape functional outcomes. Existing approaches based on attention, attribution, or correlation capture associations but do not provide a principled framework for evaluating directional effects under controlled perturbations. We introduce a framework for structured counterfactual interventions in graph-based models to estimate directional influence between node types. Our approach trains a Neighbor Influence Model (NIM) to predict node states from local neighborhoods and applies constrained interventions that modify neighborhood composition while preserving key spatial and structural properties. We define the Counterfactual Directionality Score (CDS), which measures the change in predicted node state induced by targeted perturbations, and provide a theoretical interpretation of CDS as a finite-difference measure of local intervention sensitivity. To obtain valid uncertainty estimates, we introduce a core-level bootstrap procedure that accounts for dependencies within spatial samples. Experiments on synthetic spatial graphs with known directional structure show that CDS recovers directional influence, remains well calibrated under null conditions, and is robust to confounding signals, while preliminary results on spatial transcriptomics data reveal biologically plausible and consistent interactions across tissue cores.

</details>


### [248] [ExTernD: Expanded-Rank Ternary Decomposition Ternary LLM PTQ with Accuracy Approaching Any Quantization Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13511)
*Chethan Reddy G. P*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向大语言模型(LLM)的扩展秩三元分解(ExTernD)后训练量化方法，通过将权重矩阵分解为两个三元因子矩阵和一个实数缩放向量，并扩展内秩以逼近全精度精度。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于大语言模型(LLM)的模型量化压缩，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或遥感图像理解。虽然量化方法本身是轻量化技术的一种，但论文针对的是LLM的权重矩阵分解，而非遥感目标检测模型（如CNN或ViT-based检测器），且未讨论任何遥感场景或部署约束。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，仅量化分解思路有极有限的借鉴价值，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM后训练量化/权重分解；应用场景：大语言模型压缩；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于三元分解的权重矩阵近似：A ≈ B·diag(D)·C，其中B,C ∈ {-1,0,+1}，D ∈ R^k为实数缩放向量。内秩k = μ·min(m,n)可扩展(μ>1)。

Lightweight method: 后训练三元分解量化(Post-training Ternary Decomposition)。通过扩展内秩(μ>1)来补偿量化误差，支持连续调节有效比特宽度(bpw)和因子稀疏度。属于模型量化/分解类压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及有效比特宽度(bpw)为5.1-5.7 bpw，内存和计算随μ连续缩放。未讨论任何边缘/星上部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Wikitext-2；评价指标：困惑度(Perplexity)；模型：Gemma-4-E2B, Qwen3.5-4B。不涉及遥感数据集。

Experiments: ExTernD在Qwen3.5-4B上(μ=3)达到Wikitext-2困惑度10.10，对比bf16的9.78（+3.2%），接近Q4_K/Q5_K精度水平(~5.7 effective bpw)。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对LLM的权重矩阵分解，未讨论对CNN/Transformer检测器的适用性；2. 未涉及遥感图像或目标检测任务；3. 未讨论推理速度、硬件部署等实际效率指标；4. 未与剪枝、蒸馏等其他轻量化方法对比。

Ideas for my research: 1. 三元分解+扩展秩的思想可借鉴：将权重矩阵分解为两个三元因子矩阵，通过扩展秩补偿量化误差，这一思路可能适用于遥感检测模型的卷积层或全连接层量化；2. 后训练量化的"无需重训练"特性对星上部署有吸引力，但需验证在检测模型上的有效性；3. 连续调节bpw和稀疏度的能力有助于在精度-效率之间精细权衡，适合星上资源动态调整场景。但整体借鉴价值有限，因为方法针对LLM设计，且未在检测任务上验证。

Abstract: We introduce ExTernD (Expanded-rank Ternary Decomposition), a post-training factorization of each LLM weight matrix $A \in \mathbb{R}^{m \times n}$ into $A \approx B \mathrm{diag}(D) C$ with ternary factors $B \in \{-1,0,+1\}^{m \times k}$, $C \in \{-1,0,+1\}^{k \times n}$ and a real scale vector $D \in \mathbb{R}^k$. The inner rank $k = μ\min(m,n)$ is deliberately expanded beyond full rank ($μ> 1$), so that components past full rank correct the quantization error of earlier ones. We prove the residual decreases monotonically in $k$ and can be driven below any $\varepsilon > 0$: ExTernD approaches bf16 accuracy arbitrarily closely, which no ternary scheme with a fixed plane count can do. Memory and compute scale continuously with $μ$, and factor sparsity continuously with a threshold $τ$, so an accuracy target is hit exactly rather than rounded to the next bit-width. ExTernD matches Q4_K's per-matrix accuracy at 5.2-5.5 effective bpw (5.1-5.5 with importance weighting) on Gemma-4-E2B and Qwen3.5-4B, and a full Qwen3.5-4B conversion at $μ= 3$ reaches 10.10 wikitext-2 perplexity against 9.78 for bf16 (+3.2%), placing it near the Q4_K/Q5_K accuracy band at ~5.7 effective bpw.

</details>


### [249] [Clustering algorithms for multivariate wind farm SCADA data filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13544)
*Nicolò Italiano,Vasilis Pettas,Tuhfe Göçmen,Nicolaos A. Cutululis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较多种聚类算法在风电场SCADA数据自动滤波中的效果，提出针对无标签数据的评估指标，并给出模型校准建议。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦风电场SCADA数据滤波（异常检测/数据清洗），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：SCADA数据自动滤波（异常检测/数据清洗）；场景：风电场运维；传感器类型：SCADA系统（非遥感卫星传感器）；目标类别：无目标检测任务。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型结构，仅提及多种聚类算法（cluster-based methods）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：三台海上风电机组的SCADA数据，10分钟统计值，多数据通道；评价指标：针对无标签数据设计的滤波精度指标（具体名称摘要未说明）。

Experiments: 对比多种聚类算法与人工滤波的精度；结果表明聚类方法能检测明显和细微异常，精度高于人工滤波，但不同模型差异较大，仍需专家参与。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感、目标检测或模型压缩信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。论文主题（风电场SCADA数据滤波）与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: During wind farm operation, Supervisory Control and Data Acquisition (SCADA) systems record numerous anomalies, transients, and specific operational modes, leading to large datasets. However, for a wide range of applications, only measurements corresponding to normal operation are required and, therefore, the SCADA data must be filtered. For this purpose, several methods have been proposed to automate and replace manual filtering conducted by experts via visual inspection of the data. In this paper, we compare the filtering accuracy of multiple clustering algorithms against manual filtering, introducing evaluation metrics that are suitable for unlabeled data and robust across potential applications. Based on the results, we provide recommendations for generalizing model calibration to different datasets and discuss potential use cases for each model. The models are applied to the SCADA data of three turbines of an existing offshore wind farm, using 10-minute statistics across multiple data channels. In addition to the anomalies and operational modes typically recorded, the dataset presents a large number of non-evident outliers due to several field tests. Overall, the results highlight the importance of extending the analysis beyond the power curve, both in feature selection and in the design of evaluation metrics. In most cases, cluster-based methods are able to detect both evident and subtle outliers, achieving higher accuracy than manual filtering. However, the accuracy and the amount of data retained vary considerably depending on the model, and expert involvement remains necessary, though to a reduced extent compared to manual filtering.

</details>


### [250] [From Novice to Expert: Cost-Aware Bandits for Evolving Worker Performance in Crowdsensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13546)
*Yin Huang,Qingsong Liu,Jie Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究移动群智感知中预算约束下的在线工人招募问题，提出一种基于结构化bandit的学习框架，建模工人感知质量随经验增长并收敛的动态过程，同时学习未知成本。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是移动群智感知（Mobile Crowdsensing）中的在线工人招募问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心场景；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘/星上部署等任何轻量化或部署技术。属于泛机器学习/强化学习（bandit）方法在移动感知场景的应用，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：在线工人招募（online worker recruitment）；应用场景：移动群智感知（Mobile Crowdsensing），如交通监控、环境感知；传感器类型：智能手机；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。摘要未说明具体目标类别。

Model architecture: 结构化bandit模型（structured bandit model），每个工人的期望奖励（感知质量）随参与次数按未知的"先增后收敛"函数演化，每个工人有未知的期望成本。提出成本感知在线学习框架（cost-aware online learning framework），联合学习演化奖励轨迹和异质成本，检测性能饱和，分配有限预算。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何模型轻量化方法。摘要未提及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标：长期感知效用（long-term sensing utility）。实验方式：通过extensive experiments验证，与忽略经验驱动动态或假设已知成本的baseline对比。

Experiments: 摘要仅说明通过大量实验验证了所提方法相比baseline有一致改进（consistent improvements），但未给出具体数值结果、对比方法名称或消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 摘要未讨论模型的计算开销或部署可行性；3. 仅针对移动群智感知场景，未考虑遥感图像处理或星上计算约束。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：bandit框架中"学习动态演化+检测饱和"的思路，理论上可类比于在轨模型自适应更新中检测模型性能何时饱和、是否需要重新训练或微调，但与本课题核心（轻量化目标检测模型）关联度很低。

Abstract: Mobile crowdsensing (MC) recruits mobile users to perform sensing tasks using their smartphones, enabling large-scale applications such as traffic monitoring and environmental sensing. A fundamental challenge is online worker recruitment under uncertainty, where the platform must learn workers' sensing performance while operating with a limited budget. Existing learning-based MC recruitment methods typically assume that each worker's sensing quality is stationary with a fixed mean over time. In practice, however, worker performance often improves with experience and eventually stabilizes, while the incurred sensing cost can be unknown in advance due to time-varying device and context states. In this paper, we study a budget-constrained online recruitment problem in which the platform selects one worker in each round, observes the sensing quality and incurred cost, where the expected sensing quality of each worker increases with experience and eventually converges to a plateau, and repeats until the budget is exhausted. We formulate this problem as a structured bandit model where each worker's expected reward evolves according to an unknown increasing-then-converging function of its participation count, and each worker has an unknown expected cost. We develop a cost-aware online learning framework that jointly learns evolving reward trajectories and heterogeneous costs, detects performance saturation, and allocates the limited budget to maximize long-term sensing utility. We provide theoretical performance guarantees and validate the proposed approach through extensive experiments, demonstrating consistent improvements over baselines that ignore experience-driven dynamics or assume known costs.

</details>


### [251] [The SIGReg Objective as Variational Free Energy: A Theoretical Active-Inference Account of JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13612)
*Fabio Arnez,Alexandra Gomez-Villa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论层面将JEPA（联合嵌入预测架构）的训练目标与Active Inference变分自由能联系起来，分析了不同非对比正则化器对熵估计的影响，并证明了在特定条件下JEPA目标等价于信息瓶颈。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文是纯理论工作，讨论的是世界模型（world model）的变分自由能形式化，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化或任何部署相关话题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何具体视觉任务或应用场景，属于无监督/自监督学习理论。

Model architecture: JEPA（Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture），讨论其训练目标（预测损失+加权嵌入正则化）与Active Inference自由能的关系。涉及四种非对比正则化器：VICReg、LogDet、PairDist、SIGReg。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。不涉及任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文为纯理论推导，无实验数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。论文明确说明所有预测为理论结果，留待后续实证检验。所有证明在附录A中，核心代数结果在Lean 4中机器验证。

Limitations: 1. 纯理论工作，无任何实验验证；2. 与遥感、目标检测、轻量化部署完全无关；3. 即使未来有应用潜力，也需大量工程转化工作。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。如果一定要找可借鉴点：SIGReg正则化器能消除先验失配间隙，使目标成为精确信息瓶颈，这种理论分析思路或许可用于理解遥感目标检测中特征表示的正则化设计，但需要大量转化工作。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) are the dominant design for latent world models, yet they are usually justified by empirical performance rather than a normative principle. We show that the choice of anti-collapse regulariser determines whether a JEPA's training objective, a prediction loss plus a weighted embedding regulariser, is a valid Active Inference (AIF) variational free energy. We organise four non-contrastive regularisers (VICReg, LogDet, PairDist, and SIGReg) into an entropy-estimator hierarchy indexed by a prior-miscalibration gap, and show that the gap's sign, whether the estimator bounds the latent entropy from above or below, decides whether the AIF surprise bound survives: VICReg and LogDet are unsafe upper bounds, PairDist a safe lower bound, and SIGReg eliminates the gap. We then prove a correspondence theorem: under the standard constant-noise encoder model and successful SIGReg enforcement (isotropic-Gaussian embeddings), the gap vanishes, the objective becomes an exact information bottleneck, the surprise bound is preserved, and the latent goal cost becomes an exact proxy for AIF pragmatic value, whereas VICReg leaves an irreducible second-order anisotropy term. We extend the correspondence to multi-step expected free energy, ensemble epistemic value, and a learned-policy regime, and we identify the one AIF term no current JEPA world model computes: the state-epistemic value, a future-state coverage signal. The predictions differ in kind, not degree, and are stated here as theoretical consequences left for empirical test in separate work; full proofs are in Appendix A, and the algebraic core of every result is machine-verified in Lean 4 (Appendix D).

</details>


### [252] [FastCentNN: Accelerating Centroid Neural Network with Entropy Proxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13613)
*Le-Anh Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FastCentNN，通过基于质心移动总量的早分裂策略加速Centroid神经网络的无监督聚类训练，在保持聚类质量的同时减少运行时间。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接或间接关系。论文研究的是无监督聚类算法CentNN的训练加速问题，属于无监督学习/聚类算法方向，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署等任何与本研究课题相关的内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：无监督聚类（unsupervised clustering）；应用场景：通用机器学习聚类任务；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。摘要未说明具体数据集类型，仅提及synthetic 2D datasets和high-dimensional datasets。

Model architecture: CentNN（Centroid Neural Network）的加速变体FastCentNN。核心机制：基于每epoch质心移动总量（total centroid movement per epoch）作为训练熵代理（training entropy proxy），引入早分裂策略（early splitting strategy），支持绝对阈值和阶段相对移动阈值（absolute and stage-relative movement thresholds）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是训练阶段的加速（training runtime reduction），而非模型推理阶段的轻量化或压缩。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等任何模型轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何与星上/边缘部署相关的信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：合成2D数据集（synthetic 2D datasets）和高维数据集（high-dimensional datasets），具体名称摘要未说明。评价指标：聚类质量（clustering quality，具体指标未说明）和运行时间（runtime）。

Experiments: 在合成2D数据集上运行时间减少最多16%（up to 16%），在高维数据集上减少约5%（about 5%），聚类质量与CentNN相当（comparable）。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关；2. 仅针对训练阶段加速，不涉及推理效率；3. 加速幅度有限（5%-16%）；4. 仅在合成数据集和通用高维数据集上验证，未在遥感或视觉任务上测试。

Ideas for my research: 基本无可借鉴点。该论文属于无监督聚类算法的训练加速，与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题在任务类型（聚类 vs. 检测）、应用场景（通用 vs. 遥感卫星）、优化目标（训练加速 vs. 推理轻量化）三个维度上均不匹配。

Abstract: Centroid neural network (CentNN) is an unsupervised competitive learning algorithm in which centroid splitting is triggered only after strict local stabilization, often leading to prolonged low-movement training phases before model expansion. This report proposes FastCentNN, an accelerated variant that addresses this inefficiency by introducing an early splitting strategy based on the total centroid movement per epoch, which serves as a training entropy proxy. As a result, FastCentNN reduces unnecessary reassignment epochs while preserving the original winner-loser learning dynamics. FastCentNN supports both absolute and stage-relative movement thresholds, allowing the splitting criterion to remain either fixed or adaptive throughout training. Experiments on some benchmark datasets show that FastCentNN consistently achieves clustering quality comparable to CentNN while reducing runtime by up to 16% on synthetic 2D datasets and about 5% on high-dimensional datasets. FastCentNN therefore provides a practical and efficient drop-in replacement for CentNN, retaining its online adaptive learning behavior while offering a simple and interpretable speed-stability trade-off through configurable splitting thresholds.

</details>


### [253] [How the Hessian-Spectrum of Neural Networks Depends on Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13631)
*Jasraj Singh,Enea Monzio Compagnoni,Antonio Orvieto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上推导了线性网络Hessian矩阵的特征值，并分析了损失景观与优化动力学，与遥感、目标检测、轻量化均无直接关系。

Research relevance: 1分，基本无关。论文聚焦深度学习理论中的Hessian矩阵特征值分析，属于优化理论与损失景观的基础研究。完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测任务、模型轻量化或边缘部署等任何与本研究方向相关的内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及任何具体的视觉任务或应用场景，仅讨论线性网络的Hessian理论分析。

Model architecture: 线性网络（linear networks），任意宽度和深度。不涉及卷积、注意力、检测头等目标检测常用结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。仅提及"datasets with an arbitrary number of samples, features, and labels"作为理论设定，未使用任何遥感或视觉基准数据集。

Experiments: 通过理论推导得到Hessian特征值与类别样本比例的关系，并在简化假设逐步放松的条件下进行实验验证。未与任何目标检测或轻量化方法进行对比。

Limitations: 论文本身是理论性工作，局限性在于仅分析了线性网络，对非线性网络的分析较为初步。与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。Hessian矩阵分析理论上可用于理解损失景观的sharpness，间接与剪枝（如基于Hessian的剪枝）或量化感知训练有关，但本文仅讨论线性网络且未涉及任何压缩方法，借鉴价值极低。不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: The Hessian matrix is an important quantity of interest when it comes to studying the loss landscape and optimization dynamics in deep learning, as well as designing measures of generalization, second-order learning algorithms, etc. Prior works have focused on empirical results or pursued a theoretical treatment under overly simplified settings. In this work, we derive the eigenvalues of the Hessian of linear networks with arbitrary widths and depths, and datasets with an arbitrary number of samples, features, and labels. Importantly, for classification tasks with MSE loss, we identify that the sharpness of the solution is directly related to the maximum proportion of samples belonging to any class. We empirically validate our predictions and systematically analyze the effects of shedding the impractical assumptions one at a time, as well as incorporating nonlinearities. We observe that our predictions are considerably robust in most cases, allowing us to extend our conclusions to more practical learning setups.

</details>


### [254] [Consensus as Privileged Context for Label-Free Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13643)
*John Gkountouras,Josip Jukić,Ivan Titov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CANON（共识锚定自蒸馏）方法，利用大语言模型多次采样输出中的多数答案作为密集token级监督信号，实现无标签条件下的推理能力提升。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦大语言模型（LLM）的推理能力提升与无标签自蒸馏方法，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化/部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型推理能力提升（数学/科学推理）；应用场景：NLP领域；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于大语言模型（LLM），具体架构摘要未说明。核心是自蒸馏框架：教师模型为冻结的模型快照，以共识锚定（consensus-anchored）方式生成密集token级监督信号。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文方法（自蒸馏）虽名为"distillation"，但属于无标签训练方法，并非模型压缩或轻量化意义上的知识蒸馏（即不涉及将大模型知识迁移到小模型）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数学和科学推理基准（具体名称摘要未说明）；评价指标：pass@1（单次采样准确率），majority vote准确率。

Experiments: CANON在pass@1上提升最多12个百分点；比无标签强化学习方法高出6个百分点，且计算量仅为后者的1/7；在无标签数据上训练后可迁移到未见过的基准，性能接近使用真实标签的训练方法。

Limitations: 1. 完全面向NLP/LLM领域，与遥感视觉任务无关；2. 不涉及任何模型轻量化或边缘部署技术；3. 方法依赖LLM的多次采样生成多数答案，计算开销在推理时可能较大。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：1. 自蒸馏思想（teacher-student框架）可迁移到遥感目标检测模型的半监督/无监督训练中；2. 共识机制（multiple solutions + majority vote）可类比为遥感检测中的多模型集成或测试时增强（TTA）；3. 但论文本身不涉及轻量化，且面向NLP而非视觉，直接借鉴意义不大。

Abstract: Sampling multiple solutions and returning the majority answer is among the most reliable ways to improve the reasoning accuracy of large language models without labels, and a growing family of methods converts this consensus signal into training supervision. However, existing approaches use consensus only in restricted forms: as a filter that selects solutions for fine-tuning, as a preference between answers, or as a scalar reward for reinforcement learning, discarding most of the information that the agreeing solutions contain. We present CANON (Consensus-ANchored self-distillatiON), a label-free training method that turns consensus into dense, token-level supervision. For each unlabeled prompt, CANON samples multiple solutions, extracts the majority answer, and conditions a frozen snapshot of the model on a solution that reaches it; this consensus-anchored teacher then supervises the model on its own rollouts at every token. Experiments on mathematical and scientific reasoning benchmarks show that CANON improves pass@1 by up to 12 points, outperforming label-free reinforcement learning by 6 points at a seventh of its compute and approaching a teacher conditioned on gold solutions; trained on pooled unlabeled data, it transfers to held-out benchmarks, matching training methods that use gold labels. Analysis suggests that the improvements are not pure distribution sharpening: after training, the model solves problems it previously never solved in 32 attempts, and its majority vote itself becomes more accurate.

</details>


### [255] [Maximally Robust Satisficing Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13652)
*Samuli Kinnunen,Petrus Mikkola,Antti Niskanen,Arto Klami*

Main category: cs.LG

Abstract: Many design tasks can be cast as black-box function optimization, enabling use of Bayesian optimization to find an ideal design with minimal number of trials. However, often we do not actually need the optimum but instead a sufficiently good solution is enough, for instance a material that is durable enough for its intended use. In most cases there are multiple satisfactory solutions, forming a superlevel set of the function, raising a key question of which one to prefer. We answer this by explaining why robustness to input perturbations that may occur when the solution is deployed is a good criterion and by introduce a Bayesian optimization method that efficiently finds satisficing solutions that are robust to maximally large perturbations. In contrast to previous works, we assume the inputs can be accurately controlled during optimization, but will be perturbed after the deployment.

</details>


### [256] [The Hyperspherical Geometry of CLIP Latent Space: A Semantic Mixture Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13660)
*Zijie Yu,Gaowen Liu,Ramana Rao Kompella,Philip S. Yu,Yue Song*

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 未讨论计算开销和推理效率；3. 仅验证了CLIP场景，未在遥感数据上实验。

Abstract: Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) representations form a semantic embedding space governed by cosine similarity, reflecting an intrinsic hyperspherical geometry. However, existing probabilistic interpretations typically rely on Gaussian assumptions, which fail to capture this directional and multimodal structure. We propose a principled density model for the CLIP latent space based on Mixtures of von Mises-Fisher (MovMF) distributions defined on the unit hypersphere. Using the Expectation-Maximization (EM) algorithm, we efficiently learn a probabilistic model in which each mixture component corresponds to a coherent semantic concept. This formulation yields a closed-form likelihood naturally aligned with hyperspherical geometry, enabling accurate and interpretable density estimation. Empirically, our model significantly improves long-tailed and out-of-distribution detection and provides a natural semantic decomposition, representing each embedding as a sparse probabilistic combination of interpretable concepts. These results suggest that CLIP latent space is more faithfully characterized as a hyperspherical semantic mixture rather than an isotropic Gaussian, establishing a simple and geometrically consistent probabilistic framework for modeling and understanding multimodal representations. Project page is available at https://xiaoyuzhizi.github.io/movmf-clip/.

</details>

*Hao Qin,Chicheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向上下文组合半老虎机（CCSB）问题的通用奖励函数近似算法SquareCB.Comb，实现了极小极大最优遗憾界。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体神经网络结构。算法SquareCB.Comb基于凸优化采样组合动作，不涉及深度学习模型架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 理论分析为主，摘要未说明具体实验数据集。评价指标为累积遗憾（cumulative regret）的遗憾界（regret bound），与目标检测的mAP、FPS等指标无关。

Experiments: 理论证明为主，摘要未说明具体实验对比或消融实验。给出了遗憾界O(√(m A T log|F|))的理论保证。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。该工作聚焦在线学习理论，不涉及视觉感知任务。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于在线学习理论方向，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: We study the contextual combinatorial semi-bandit (CCSB) problem with general reward function approximation. At each round, the learner observes a context, selects a combinatorial action consisting of a subset of basic arms, and receives the reward of each selected arm; the goal is to maximize the cumulative reward over time. We propose SquareCB.Comb, a computationally efficient algorithm that, at each round, solves a convex optimization problem to sample a combinatorial action that balances exploration and exploitation. SquareCB.Comb scales to large arm sets and imposes no structural assumptions on the action set beyond a cardinality bound of $m$ on each combinatorial action. We prove that SquareCB.Comb achieves a minimax optimal regret bound of $O(\sqrt{m A T \log |\mathcal{F}|})$, where $A$ is the number of arms, $m$ is the maximum number of arms in a combinatorial action, $T$ is the time horizon, and $\mathcal{F}$ is the reward function class. In the realizable setting, this bound matches the state-of-the-art regret guarantees achieved by policy search-based algorithms in the more restricted slate recommendation settings, while simultaneously generalizing to arbitrary combinatorial action structures and general reward function approximation.

</details>


### [258] [Microstructure-Conditioned Surrogate Models for Graded Multiscale Optimization of Mycelium Composites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13688)
*J. Storm,I. B. C. M. Rocha,S. Schyck,K. Masania,F. P. van der Meer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于超网络的条件混合物理-数据代理模型，用于多尺度力学仿真和微观结构优化，以加速可持续材料设计。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是可持续材料（菌丝-木屑复合材料）的多尺度力学仿真与微观结构优化，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Model architecture: 混合物理-数据代理模型（hybrid physics-data surrogate），使用超网络（hypernetwork）根据微观结构变量进行条件化建模。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未涉及星上/边缘部署相关信息，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 使用菌丝-木屑复合材料的小样本数据集进行训练和验证；采用FE²全阶仿真作为对比基准；优化指标为峰值应力降低百分比（降低42%）。

Experiments: 在功能梯度多尺度圆盘上验证，与随机微观结构相比峰值应力降低42%；进一步将网络条件化扩展到制造变量。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无任何可借鉴的检测或轻量化技术。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于材料科学与计算力学领域，不涉及计算机视觉、目标检测或模型压缩加速技术。

Abstract: Emerging sustainable materials increasingly rely on engineered hierarchy and microstructure to achieve control of their properties and mechanical behavior. Optimizing these materials with controllable microstructures requires efficient multiscale simulations. Data-driven surrogate models for the microscale can accelerate multiscale simulations, but require large amounts of data even for a fixed microstructure. When a range of microstructures is considered, as is the case in multiscale optimization, even more data is needed to train a surrogate. To overcome this challenge, we condition a hybrid physics-data surrogate on microstructural variables using a hypernetwork. This approach enables accurate predictions of multiscale mechanical behavior for a mycelium-woodchip composite material, even when trained on small datasets. The conditioned surrogate makes multiscale simulations of functionally graded structures tractable, and we validate it against a full FE^2 simulation. We optimize a graded multiscale disk, and reduce the peak stress by 42% compared to one with a random microstructure. Then, we go one step further, conditioning the network directly on manufacturing variables that can have a complex influence on the microstructure. This is a practical route to engineer the microscale for desired macroscale behavior. This contribution highlights the benefits of microarchitectured structures and demonstrates how conditioned surrogate models enable their multiscale optimization, which will accelerate the development and design of future sustainable materials and structures.

</details>


### [259] [Conditional Invertible Neural Networks for Data-Driven UAV Control: A 2-D Proof of Concept](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13703)
*Christian Wittke,Stephan Myschik,Oliver Niggemann*

TL;DR: 本文研究条件可逆神经网络(cINN)作为多旋翼无人机控制器的概率逆动力学模型，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。论文研究多旋翼无人机控制（multirotor control），涉及可逆神经网络（cINN）和增量非线性动态逆（INDI），与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化均无任何关联。唯一可能的弱关联是"无人机"与遥感平台有关，但论文聚焦于飞行控制而非遥感成像或目标检测。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：多旋翼无人机概率逆动力学建模与控制；场景：平面X8共轴多旋翼飞行器控制；传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。摘要未说明任何遥感相关任务。

Model architecture: 条件可逆神经网络（cINN），使用有理二次样条耦合（rational-quadratic spline coupling）和可逆线性混合（invertible linear mixing）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。cINN本身是可逆网络结构，但论文未从轻量化角度讨论。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及开环和闭环控制场景的误差指标，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：未说明具体数据集名称，使用仿真控制场景；指标：R²=0.944，平均CRPS=0.0915，对数概率误差相关性ρ=-0.60，位置RMSE（9.7 vs 9.5 m），跟踪可接受率47%。

Experiments: 开环复现R²=0.944；15个闭环场景下位置RMSE与INDI教师模型相当（9.7 vs 9.5 m），47%场景跟踪可接受；失败模式分为大机动下的姿态发散和高频参考下的相位滞后。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或目标检测方法。论文本身局限在于控制失败模式分析（命令带宽和数据覆盖），但对本课题无参考价值。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。论文方向（多旋翼控制）与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化完全正交。可逆神经网络（cINN）本身是一种生成式模型结构，但论文未讨论其在遥感或目标检测中的应用，也未涉及任何轻量化技术。

Abstract: We investigate conditional invertible neural networks (cINNs) as probabilistic inverse-dynamics models for multirotor control. For a planar X8 coaxial multicopter, we learn $p(u \mid s_t, c_t)$ from an incremental nonlinear dynamic inversion (INDI) teacher using rational-quadratic spline coupling and invertible linear mixing. Open-loop reproduction reaches $R^2 = 0.944$, mean CRPS 0.0915, and log-probability-error correlation $ρ= -0.60$. Over 15 closed-loop scenarios, position RMSE matches INDI (9.7 vs. 9.5 m), with 47 percent tracking acceptably; failures separate into attitude divergence under aggressive steps and phase lag under high-frequency references, isolating command bandwidth and data coverage as dominant failure mechanisms.

</details>


### [260] [Constraint-Driven Model Optimization: An Industry Framework for Selecting Compression and Acceleration Techniques in Modern Machine Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13735)

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种约束驱动的多目标模型优化决策框架，将量化、剪枝、知识蒸馏、PEFT等优化技术统一到五个约束维度（数据可用性、延迟预算、内存预算、精度容忍度、重训练预算）下，为工业部署场景提供系统性选型指导。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关。虽然涉及轻量化技术，但属于通用系统工程方法论，没有遥感场景、目标检测任务或星上部署的任何针对性内容，不建议优先阅读。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。虽然框架包含"延迟预算"和"内存预算"两个约束维度，但未给出任何数值或遥感/星上部署相关的分析。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果。仅提到"provide optimization pipelines for four representative industrial scenarios"，但未给出定量对比。

Limitations: 1. 完全缺乏遥感/卫星场景的针对性，未考虑星上部署的独特约束（如辐射环境、通信带宽、能源限制、在轨更新困难等）；2. 未涉及目标检测任务，更不用说旋转框检测、小目标检测等遥感特有挑战；3. 框架停留在方法论层面，缺乏具体可复现的算法或模型；4. 未提供任何定量实验数据支撑。

Ideas for my research: 1. 其"约束驱动的多目标优化"思路可借鉴：在轨轻量化可形式化为"精度-延迟-功耗-星上存储"的多目标优化问题；2. 五个约束维度（数据可用性、延迟、内存、精度容忍度、重训练预算）可映射到星上场景，但需增加"功耗预算"和"抗辐射约束"等维度；3. 该框架的"将技术映射到约束而非算法类别"的思路，可用于指导在轨部署时选择剪枝/量化/蒸馏等不同策略。整体借鉴价值有限，因为缺乏遥感领域的具体性。

Abstract: The rapid deployment of machine learning systems across cloud, edge, and enterprise environments has brought model optimization to the forefront of systems-engineering. Despite a rich literature spanning quantization, pruning, knowledge distillation, parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT), and inference-time optimization, practitioners are often left navigating these techniques through heuristics rather than principled methodology. We argue that optimization should be formulated as a constraint-driven, multi-objective engineering decision and introduce a unified framework that characterizes any production deployment along five interacting constraint dimensions: data availability, latency budget, memory budget, accuracy tolerance, and retraining budget. Building on this taxonomy, we synthesize empirical gains reported across the research literature and map them to operational constraints rather than algorithmic categories. To ensure practical relevance, we selected these techniques by reviewing recent literature for methods that report measurable improvements against critical deployment bottlenecks. We propose a prescriptive decision framework and provide optimization pipelines for four representative industrial scenarios to illustrate it in practice. To the best of our knowledge, this work provides one of the first structured attempts to formalize model optimization as a constraint-aware, multi-objective engineering process, synthesizing quantitative evidence from the research literature.

</details>


### [261] [Implementations of Quantum and Classical Topology-Aligned Architectures for Molecular Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13737)
*James T. Pegg,Hubert Okadome Valencia,Ronin Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种拓扑对齐归纳偏置的量子/经典图神经网络，用于量子化学小样本参数高效学习，在QM9数据集上以64个可训练参数达到约0.91 AUC。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦量子化学分子性质预测（HOMO-LUMO、偶极矩），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及星上部署或模型轻量化（虽然涉及参数高效学习，但面向的是量子化学领域的小样本约束，而非边缘/星上部署场景）。

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：分子性质二分类（HOMO-LUMO gap、dipole moment）；应用场景：量子化学；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：分子。

Model architecture: Iso-QGNN（变分量子电路）和 Iso-CGNN（经典消息传递图神经网络），架构设计将原子映射到固定计算单元寄存器，化学键决定参数共享的成对交互。

Lightweight method: 参数高效学习（parameter-efficient learning），通过拓扑对齐归纳偏置（topology-aligned inductive bias）实现仅64个可训练参数。但这不是传统意义上的剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法，而是面向量子化学小数据场景的架构设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量之外的部署信息（FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等均未提及）。仅提到64个可训练参数，但这是针对QM9分子数据集，与遥感卫星在轨部署场景无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：QM9；任务：HOMO-LUMO gap二分类、dipole moment二分类；指标：AUC。

Experiments: 64个可训练参数下，gap任务测试AUC约0.88（量子）和0.91（经典），dipole任务约0.78（两者）；约250个训练分子达到90%渐近性能；梯度范数训练中保持稳定。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴。摘要未说明模型在遥感图像上的表现、推理速度、部署约束等关键信息。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但可抽象借鉴的思路：拓扑对齐归纳偏置（topology-aligned inductive bias）的思想——即让模型架构与数据固有结构对齐以实现参数高效。在遥感场景中，可类比思考是否能让检测模型架构与遥感图像的几何/空间结构（如旋转不变性、尺度多样性、地物空间分布模式）对齐，从而在更少参数下保持性能。但此思路非常间接，且论文本身不涉及目标检测或遥感，借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: For low-data and resource-constrained regimes typical of quantum chemistry, parameter-efficient learning is a key objective. Here, we propose a topology-aligned inductive bias in which the model architecture mirrors the molecular bond graph: atoms map to a fixed register of computational units, and bonds determine which pairs interact through shared learnable parameters. This principle is instantiated in two architectures: a variational quantum circuit (Iso-QGNN), and a parameter-matched classical message-passing model (Iso-CGNN). The models are benchmarked on HOMO-LUMO and dipole moment binary classification tasks over the QM9 benchmark. With 64 trainable parameters, the implementations achieve test AUCs of approximately 0.88 (quantum) and 0.91 (classical) on the gap task, and close to 0.78 (both) on the dipole task. The models reach 90% of asymptotic performance within about 250 training molecules and gradient norms remain stable throughout training. These results indicate that the topology-aligned inductive bias is the active ingredient driving parameter efficiency at QM9 scale, with implications for matched-baseline benchmarking in quantum machine learning.

</details>


### [262] [MxGPS: Multiplex Graph Transformers for a Power Grid Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13763)
*Charilaos Papaioannou,Ioannis Tsantilas,Dimitris Giannakakos,Vasilis Michalakopoulos,Sotiris Pelekis,Vangelis Marinakis,Arsam Aryandoust,Antonello Monti,Ricardo J. Bessa,Perdo P. Vergara,Jochen Cremer,Elissaios Sarmas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MxGPS多任务图Transformer，通过联合训练静态状态估计和潮流计算任务，解决电力图神经网络在拓扑偏移下的过拟合问题，实现拓扑无关泛化。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦电力系统图神经网络（GNN）的拓扑过拟合问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心要素；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、边缘部署等轻量化方法。虽然提及参数量（1.6M），但这是电力领域模型，方法（多任务联合训练防过拟合）与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：电力系统静态状态估计（SSE）和潮流计算（PF）；场景：电力电网拓扑分析；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: MxGPS（Multiplex GPS）：多路图Transformer，包含共享节点编码器（shared node encoder）+ K个任务专用GPS分支（task-specialised GPS branches）+ 跨分支注意力模块（cross-branch attention module）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。模型参数量1.6M（比GridFM基线少12倍），但这是模型本身规模小，并非通过剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化技术实现。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。参数量1.6M，但无FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署指标。且该模型面向电力系统，非遥感卫星在轨场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：4种未见拓扑（14-bus, 24-bus, 162-bus, 300-bus）；评价指标：边界违规率（BVR, Boundary Violation Rate）；实验设置：3折滑动窗口交叉验证（3-fold sliding-window cross-validation）。

Experiments: MxGPS在4种零样本潮流拓扑上均达到0% BVR；对比方法在拓扑偏移下PF误差退化190%-1400%，MxGPS仅退化39%；参数量1.6M（比GridFM基线少12倍）。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，方法不可直接迁移；2. 仅针对电力系统拓扑偏移问题，场景高度特化；3. 摘要未说明推理速度、计算开销等通用效率指标。

Ideas for my research: 1. 多任务联合训练防止过拟合的思路（共享编码器+任务专用分支）在遥感多任务场景（如同时检测+分割）中可能有借鉴价值，但需重新设计；2. 跨分支注意力机制可参考用于遥感多尺度特征融合；3. 但整体而言，该论文对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接借鉴意义有限。

Abstract: Single-task fine-tuning of graph neural networks (GNNs) for power grid problems exhibits a systematic failure mode: models that achieve the lowest in-distribution error degrade the most under topology shift. We term this topology overfitting: the tendency of task-specific gradient signals to encode relational structure particular to the training topologies rather than the underlying physics, causing models to fail on unseen grids despite strong in-distribution performance. To expose and address this failure mode, we introduce MxGPS (Multiplex GPS), a multiplex graph transformer that runs K task-specialised GPS branches over a shared node encoder, jointly trained on Static State Estimation (SSE) and AC Power Flow (PF) via a self-supervised pre-training and multi-task fine-tuning protocol, with a cross-branch attention module evaluated in ablation. The joint SSE+PF objective forces the shared encoder to simultaneously satisfy complementary gradient signals, preventing it from overfitting to topology-specific relational structure. Under a 3-fold sliding-window cross-validation spanning four unseen topologies (14-, 24-, 162-, and 300-bus), MxGPS attains 0% boundary violation rate (BVR) on all four zero-shot Power Flow topologies. Critically, models with substantially lower in-distribution PF error degrade by 190% to 1400% under topology shift, whereas MxGPS degrades by only 39%, an inversion that directly implicates topology overfitting as the failure mechanism rather than insufficient model capacity. With only 1.6M parameters (12x fewer than the GridFM reference baseline), MxGPS demonstrates that multi-task joint training is a principled and parameter-efficient mechanism for topology-agnostic generalisation in power grid foundation models.

</details>


### [263] [Mono-Z Dark Matter Search with Neural Spline Flows Using CMS Run 2015D Open Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13771)
*Hitesh Rasineni,Bhavishya Chebrolu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用CMS 2015D开放数据和Neural Spline Flow似然比评分方法，在mono-Z末态中搜索暗物质信号，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文是高能物理领域暗物质搜索实验，使用CMS对撞机数据和Neural Spline Flow进行似然比统计推断。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测、遥感场景理解；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT。与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：暗物质搜索（高能物理）；应用场景：CMS对撞机实验；传感器类型：CMS探测器（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：暗物质信号（非视觉目标）。摘要未说明任何遥感或目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: 使用Neural Spline Flow（神经样条流）分别对标准模型背景和暗物质信号密度进行建模，构建似然比检验统计量。摘要未说明网络具体结构参数。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。该工作运行于地面高性能计算环境，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CMS Run 2015D open data（积分亮度2.32 fb⁻¹）及简化模型蒙特卡洛模拟；评价指标：95%置信水平信号强度参数μ的上限（observed/expected）；实验设置：μμ和ee两个通道联合profile似然比拟合。

Experiments: 标量中介子μ<0.0177（期望0.0018），矢量中介子μ<0.0362（期望0.0039），轴矢中介子μ<0.0498（期望0.0069）。观测上限弱于期望，归因于高MET区域背景建模残差，而非暗物质信号证据。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可参考的局限性分析。摘要本身指出高MET区域背景建模存在残差导致观测上限弱于期望。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于高能物理实验数据分析，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在问题定义、数据模态、模型目标、部署约束上均无交集。Neural Spline Flow作为密度估计方法虽可用于异常检测，但摘要中未涉及任何遥感或轻量化内容，不建议作为参考。

Abstract: We report a search for dark matter (DM) produced in association with a leptonically decaying \(Z\) boson at \(\sqrt{s}=13\) TeV using CMS Run 2015D open data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of \(2.32\,\mathrm{fb}^{-1}\) together with simplified-model Monte Carlo simulation. Events are selected in the mono-\(Z\rightarrow\ell^+\ell^-\) final state in both the \(μμ\) and \(ee\) channels. Forty kinematic observables are extracted from MINIAOD and MINIAODSIM, cleaned with physics-motivated selections, and reduced to a 37-dimensional feature vector. Five Neural Spline Flows are trained independently to model Standard Model background and mediator-specific DM signal densities. The per-event test statistic is constructed from the log-likelihood ratio between the signal and background density estimates, providing sensitivity across the full kinematic phase space without requiring a hard upper \(\mathrm{MET}\) threshold. A simultaneous profile-likelihood fit combining the two channels yields observed (expected) 95\% confidence level upper limits on the signal-strength parameter of \(μ<0.0177\) (\(0.0018\)) for the scalar mediator, \(μ<0.0362\) (\(0.0039\)) for the vector mediator, and \(μ<0.0498\) (\(0.0069\)) for the axial-vector mediator. The observed limits are weaker than expected because of a residual high-\(\mathrm{MET}\) background-modeling discrepancy rather than evidence for a DM signal. To our knowledge, this is the first application of Neural Spline Flow likelihood-ratio scoring to a mono-\(Z\) dark matter search using CMS Run 2015D open data simultaneously in the \(μμ\) and \(ee\) channels.

</details>


### [264] [NodeImport: Imbalanced Node Classification with Node Importance Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13837)
*Nan Chen,Zemin Liu,Bryan Hooi,Bingsheng He,Jun Hu,Jia Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于平衡元集重要性度量的节点分类框架NodeImport，用于解决图神经网络中的类别不平衡问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于图神经网络（GNN）上的节点分类任务和类别不平衡问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化均无任何关联，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：图节点分类（node classification）；场景：通用图数据上的类别不平衡场景；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于GNN（Graph Neural Network）架构，具体使用的GNN类型（如GCN、GAT等）在摘要中提及为"popular GNN architectures"，但未指定具体结构。核心模块是重要性度量（importance measurement）和节点筛选框架NodeImport。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标，仅提及"across multiple datasets"和"demonstrating its superiority over existing baselines"。

Experiments: 摘要未提供具体实验结果数值，仅声称在多个数据集上优于现有基线方法。

Limitations: 摘要未讨论局限性。与本研究方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无直接关联。其提出的类别不平衡处理方法（基于元集的重要性度量）在理论上可迁移到遥感目标检测中的类别不平衡问题（如舰船vs小目标），但摘要中未涉及检测任务或轻量化，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: In real-world applications, node classification on graphs often faces the challenge of class imbalance, where majority classes dominate training, resulting in biased model performance. Traditional GNNs often struggle in such scenarios, as they tend to overfit to majority classes while underrepresenting minority classes. Existing solutions, which either prioritize nodes based on class size or synthesize new nodes for minority classes, often fall short of effectively addressing this imbalance issue. This paper introduces an approach to class-imbalanced node classification by utilizing a balanced meta-set for importance measurement, where a training node is considered significant if it enhances model performance under an unbiased setting. Our method identifies important nodes that can counteract class imbalance and utilizes them for model training, allowing for fine-grained and dynamic node selection throughout the training process. We theoretically derive a formula to directly assess node importance, reducing computational overhead and providing an intuitive threshold for node selection. Guided by this metric, we develop a novel framework that filters valuable labeled, unlabeled, and synthetic nodes that enhance model performance in an unbiased context. A key advantage of this framework is its separation of the synthetic node generation process from the filtering process, ensuring compatibility with various node generation methods. Furthermore, we introduce a strategy to construct a high-quality meta-set that closely approximates the overall feature distribution, ensuring robust representation of each class. We evaluate our framework, NodeImport, across multiple datasets using popular GNN architectures, demonstrating its superiority over existing baselines. Our results highlight the flexibility and effectiveness of the framework in mitigating class imbalance, leading to improved outcomes.

</details>


### [265] [Relevance-Aware Rule: Structural Deletion of Irrelevant Conditions in Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13874)
*Jung-Sik Hong,Jeongeon Lee,Min Kyu Sim,Sangheum Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于决策树分裂结构机制的不可靠条件（IRC）删除框架，用于简化决策树规则而不损失可靠性。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。论文聚焦于决策树的可解释性简化（去除冗余条件），属于传统机器学习/可解释AI范畴，不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或模型轻量化内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及任何遥感、卫星、目标检测或特定应用场景。任务为决策树规则简化。

Model architecture: 决策树（Decision trees），具体为二叉树分裂结构。不涉及任何深度学习模型或目标检测网络。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文提出的IRC删除框架是一种规则简化方法，与模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要仅提及实验证实所提框架能在不损失原始树可靠性的前提下实现显著规则简化，未给出具体数值结果或对比方法。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设置。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文研究的是决策树规则简化，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化（涉及CNN/Transformer、剪枝/量化/蒸馏等）属于完全不同的技术路线和研究范畴。

Abstract: Decision trees generate interpretable if--then rules, yet they contain irrelevant conditions (IRCs). These IRCs arise from the structural mechanism of tree splitting and persist even in modern optimal sparse tree induction algorithms. Existing IRC deletion methods overlook this structural mechanism; therefore, they either preserve the original tree too loosely to remain reliable, or too strictly to achieve meaningful simplification. This study provides theoretical foundations for reliable IRC deletion by establishing theorems and propositions related to the underlying IRC mechanism. The key finding is that a binary split shifts class proportions in opposite directions relative to the parent. Specifically, an increase in the class-1 proportion along one branch necessitates an increase in the class-0 proportion along its sibling, thereby generating a C1-link and a C0-link. Based on this structural fact, we propose a structural IRC deletion framework. Relative to each leaf, links that increase the leaf-class proportion are matched, whereas links that increase the proportion of the opposite leaf-class are mismatched. These mismatched links are flagged as structurally suspicious IRC candidates. Rather than deleting them outright, the framework rigorously diagnoses their relevance by assessing prediction reliability. It selectively deletes conditions that are structurally and empirically irrelevant, while strictly protecting those whose deletion would reduce the rule's reliability. Experimental results confirm that the proposed framework achieves substantial rule simplification without sacrificing the reliability of the original tree.

</details>


### [266] [AI-Augmented Adaptive Digital Twin Modeling for Brain Tumor Evolution Prediction and Treatment Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13877)
*Wenxi Liu,Michael Trimboli,Xianqi Li*

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。该框架面向临床治疗规划，非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：387条合成肿瘤轨迹，每条120步演化（基于患者数据生成的合成轨迹，非真实临床纵向数据）；评价指标：掩膜体素级均方误差(MSE)、Dice重叠系数、最终肿瘤负荷。

Experiments: (1) 混合RD-残差模型相比纯RD基线：掩膜MSE降低84.3%，Dice提升43.5%；(2) 在线DT更新相比非更新混合模型：MSE降低45.9%，Dice提升9.6%；(3) MPC调度相比固定治疗方案：最终肿瘤负荷降低22.4%。

Limitations: (1) 仅在合成数据上验证，未使用真实临床纵向数据；(2) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴其方法；(3) 未讨论计算效率、实时推理能力；(4) 3D残差学习模块的计算开销可能较大。

Ideas for my research: 尽管论文与遥感在轨目标检测无关，但以下思路可能有间接启发：(1) 物理信息引导的混合建模思想——在遥感目标检测中可考虑将遥感物理先验（如大气散射模型、SAR成像机理）与数据驱动模型结合；(2) 在线自适应更新机制——在轨场景中模型可针对新数据分布进行在线微调，类似数字孪生更新；(3) 模型预测控制(MPC)的优化框架——可类比于星上资源（算力、带宽、能耗）的调度优化问题。但整体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Brain tumor progression exhibits spatially heterogeneous growth, patient-specific treatment response, and complex interactions with surrounding anatomy, making accurate long-term prediction challenging. We propose an AI-augmented adaptive digital twin (DT) framework for brain tumor evolution prediction and treatment scheduling. The framework integrates an interpretable reaction--diffusion (RD) model, a 3D residual learning module for model-form correction, patient-specific DT updating during recursive rollout, and model predictive control (MPC) for constrained chemotherapy and radiotherapy scheduling. Experiments on 387 synthetic tumor trajectories with 120-step evolution show that the baseline RD model captures tumor location and overall temporal behavior but underestimates heterogeneous tumor burden during long-horizon prediction. Hybrid RD--residual modeling reduces masked voxel-wise mean squared error by 84.3% and increases Dice overlap by 43.5% relative to the RD baseline under dense simulated observations. Online DT updating further reduces mean squared error by 45.9% and improves Dice overlap by 9.6% compared with the non-updated hybrid model. In MPC-based scheduling simulations, the updated DT controller reduces final tumor burden by 22.4% relative to a fixed treatment schedule under the terminal-burden objective. Together, these results demonstrate a unified framework for patient-specific initialization, mechanistic modeling, adaptive learning, and constrained treatment optimization. Although validated using patient-data-informed synthetic trajectories rather than clinical longitudinal data, the proposed framework establishes a foundation for future translation to real-world adaptive treatment planning.

</details>


### [267] [Task-Oriented Sensing and Covert Transmissions for Collaborative Multi-AUV Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13880)
*Xueyao Zhang,Chenyang Yan,Bo Yang,Xuelin Cao,Zhiwen Yu,Bin Guo,George C. Alexandropoulos,Merouane Debbah,Chau Yuen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SVR-MARL框架，面向水下自主潜航器（AUV）在隐蔽协同任务中的信息价值感知与分布式协同策略学习，不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦水下AUV隐蔽协同任务中的多智能体强化学习（MARL）与通信优化，不涉及遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱图像，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多AUV隐蔽协同定位与跟踪（cooperative localization and tracking）；应用场景：水下隐蔽作战；传感器类型：被动声学感知（passive observation）；目标类别：摘要未说明具体目标类别。与遥感卫星场景无关。

Model architecture: SVR-MARL框架，基于多智能体强化学习（MARL），利用实际信息表征信息对协同任务的效用。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。仅提及通过多AUV协同定位与跟踪案例验证方法有效性。

Experiments: 摘要未提供定量实验结果、对比方法或消融实验数据。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关。论文面向水下AUV隐蔽协同场景，传感器模态（被动声学）、任务目标（协同定位跟踪）、技术路线（MARL通信优化）均不匹配。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，可借鉴点极少。如果强行关联，MARL中"信息价值表征"（information value realization）的思路或许对星群协同感知中的通信调度有启发，但与本课题核心（遥感图像目标检测模型轻量化）方向差异过大，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: In underwater covert cooperative missions, autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) often cannot rely on active sonar to continuously obtain complete information, since active sensing and frequent communications increase the risk of exposure. As a result, AUVs primarily rely on passive observation, an approach that yields incomplete local perception and limited task efficiency. Although underwater acoustic communications can mitigate this limitation through information sharing, they are simultaneously constrained by long delays, severe interference, low reliability, and the risk of covert exposure. Existing communications-oriented multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) studies often model communication as an ideal information flow, whereas traditional communication optimization primarily focuses on link-level performance. However, both are insufficient to characterize the actual contribution of perceptual information to cooperative tasks under realistic conditions of covert physical communications. This paper proposes a Sensed Information Value Realization Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (SVR-MARL) framework that leverages practical information to characterize the utility of information for cooperative tasks and learns distributed cooperative policies under realistic communication and covert constraints. Through a case study of covert multi-AUV cooperative localization and tracking, the potential of the proposed framework to improve collaborative task efficiency while reducing unnecessary communication and exposure risks is demonstrated.

</details>


### [268] [RF Spectrogram Anomaly Detection with Quantum Kitchen Sinks: Architecture, Representation, and Hardware Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13897)
*Abdallah Aaraba,Alexis Vieloszynski,Remon Polus,Ola Ahmad,Soumaya Cherkaoui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于量子厨房水槽(QKS)的无线射频信号异常检测方法，采用多深度数据重上传和环状纠缠结构，在真实量子处理器上验证了AUROC 0.8778的性能。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是无线射频(RF)网络的异常检测，使用量子计算(QKS)方法，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然摘要中出现了"lightweight"一词，但指的是量子特征映射的轻量性，与深度学习模型压缩/加速无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：无线射频信号异常检测；应用场景：无线频谱安全管理；传感器类型：RF接收器；目标类别：异常/恶意传输信号。不涉及遥感、卫星、光学/SAR/高光谱图像。

Model architecture: Quantum Kitchen Sinks (QKS)混合量子特征映射，包含多深度数据重上传(multi-depth data re-uploading)和环状纠缠(ring entanglement)结构，结合经典读出层。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明深度学习模型轻量化方法。文中"lightweight"指QKS量子特征映射本身的计算轻量性，与剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩方法无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。验证平台为ibm_quebec量子处理单元(QPU)，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：真实测量的sub-6 GHz蜂窝信号；评价指标：AUROC (0.8778)、F1-score (0.7995)；输入表示：DCT、PCA、原始信号。

Experiments: 五阶段消融协议：浅层架构、重上传深度、episode预算、输入表示、经典读出。DCT表示优于PCA和原始输入；中等深度纠缠QKS配置表现最佳；在ibm_quebec QPU上仿真与真实设备偏差<0.013 AUROC。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 量子计算硬件目前不具备星上部署可行性；3. 仅针对RF信号异常检测，不涉及图像目标检测；4. 未提供模型参数量、计算量等轻量化关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可参考的是其消融实验设计思路（五阶段分离消融协议），可用于遥感轻量化检测模型各模块贡献度的系统分析，但方法本身（量子计算+RF信号）与遥感目标检测轻量化方向无直接关联。

Abstract: The broadcast nature of wireless channels exposes radio-frequency (RF) networks to anomalous and malicious transmissions, making anomaly detection a fundamental requirement for secure spectrum management. Quantum Kitchen Sinks (QKS) offer a lightweight hybrid quantum feature map suitable for near-term quantum devices, yet their behavior on structured signal data remains poorly understood. In this paper, we extend the standard QKS template with multi-depth data re-uploading and ring entanglement, and evaluate the resulting pipeline on controlled RF spectrogram anomaly detection. We introduce a validation-locked five-stage ablation protocol that systematically separates the effects of shallow architecture, re-uploading depth, episode budget, input representation, and classical readout. Across the completed benchmark, Discrete Cosine Transform (DCT) representations consistently dominate raw and Principal Component Analysis (PCA) inputs, moderate-depth entangled QKS configurations form the strongest operating regime, and QKS improves over matched classical direct-readout baselines across all evaluated representation-readout pairs on the held-out test set, with the best configuration reaching a test Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.8778 and a test F1 of 0.7995. The study bridges two levels of realism: real measured sub-6\,GHz cellular signals on the data side and real-device validation on the ibm_quebec Quantum Processing Unit (QPU) on the computing side, with AUROC deviations below 0.013 relative to simulation. These results provide a practical, reproducible framework for deploying QKS-based anomaly detection in wireless networks.

</details>


### [269] [An Efficient Newton Algorithm for Nonnegative Matrix Factorization with the Kullback-Leibler Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13919)
*Damien Lesens,Jérémy E. Cohen,Bora Uçar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于牛顿法的KL散度非负矩阵分解（KL-NMF）算法，通过二阶泰勒展开替代传统可分离上界优化，并推广HALS算法实现高效求解。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于无监督学习中的非负矩阵分解（NMF）算法优化，属于纯机器学习方法改进，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化部署中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：无监督学习中的矩阵分解算法优化；应用场景：通用数据分解（如文档-词项矩阵、图像）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体网络结构。核心方法是KL-NMF的牛顿型优化算法，推广了HALS（Hierarchical Alternating Least Squares）算法。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是NMF算法的收敛速度和优化效率，并非模型压缩或加速方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：多种通用数据集（large variety of datasets），但未具体列出名称；评价指标：KL散度损失、收敛性证明，未涉及目标检测指标（mAP、F1等）。

Experiments: 与state-of-the-art KL-NMF算法在多种数据集上对比，证明所提算法收敛更快、效果更优。但未提供具体数值结果。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署完全无关；2. 仅针对KL-NMF这一特定优化问题，适用范围有限；3. 未讨论算法在实际工程部署中的计算开销和硬件适配性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。如果硬要提取可借鉴点：1）二阶优化方法（牛顿法）在收敛速度上的优势思路，可迁移到轻量化模型训练加速中；2）HALS算法的推广策略可能对某些矩阵分解类轻量化方法（如低秩分解）有启发。但整体而言，与在轨遥感目标检测轻量化的核心问题（实时推理、资源受限、精度保持）关联度极低。

Abstract: Nonnegative Matrix Factorization (NMF) is a fundamental tool in unsupervised learning, which approximates a nonnegative matrix by the product of two low-rank nonnegative factors. The Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence is best suited to measure the data to model discrepancy when the decomposed data sample follows a Poisson distribution, which is the case for count datasets such as term-document matrices or images. Most KL-NMF algorithms in the literature minimize a separable majorant of the loss to find their next iterate. We argue that this method has reached its limits and propose to use instead the second-order Taylor expansion of the loss, leading to a Newton-type method. We minimize this non-separable surrogate by proposing a generalization of the well-known HALS algorithm. This yields an efficient KL-NMF algorithm which provably converges and which competes favorably with state-of-the-art algorithms on a large variety of datasets.

</details>


### [270] [VAIOM: Continuous-Input, Discrete-Output Decoder-Only Financial Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13929)
*Yiming Ma,Xinyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VAIOM，一种用于外汇收益率建模的向量输入自回归解码器Transformer，核心贡献是将连续金融向量作为输入、用分类分布建模归一化收益率，并在外汇数据上超越LightGBM基线。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦金融时间序列（外汇收益率建模），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：概率性下一收益率建模（probabilistic next-return modeling）；场景：外汇市场（foreign-exchange bars, 1小时粒度）；传感器/数据类型：连续金融多变量事件向量（continuous multivariate financial-event vectors）；目标类别：无目标检测相关类别。

Model architecture: Decoder-only Transformer架构，称为VAIOM（Vector-Input Autoregressive Inference for Ordinal-Return Modeling）。包含：Hybrid Continuous Input（连续事件特征+分类资产元数据）、Mixture-of-Market-States return head、Gap/volatility-regime/Ordinal辅助目标、全序列监督（full-sequence supervision）。参数量0.9M。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。模型参数量0.9M（较小），但未报告FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：2024年前的外汇训练数据（Train），2024下半年验证集（Validation），两个2025测试时段（Test periods）。评价指标：bits per event（每事件比特数），对比LightGBM基线提升0.029和0.043 bits/event。

Experiments: 主要实验：三个独立训练种子下，所有模型在两个测试时段均优于固定单条LightGBM基线。消融实验验证了：连续输入优于离散token输入、全序列监督优于最后位置训练、辅助表征塑造+混合结构return head提升似然。容量研究发现最小完整架构在验证集上似然最优。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法提供任何遥感场景下的借鉴。金融时序建模与遥感图像/信号处理在数据模态、任务目标、部署约束上差异极大。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，可借鉴点极少。唯一可能的技术点：连续向量输入替代离散token化的思路，在遥感中处理连续光谱信号或SAR复数数据时可能有理论上的类比意义，但论文本身未涉及遥感场景，且无轻量化方法，借鉴价值很低。

Abstract: Financial observations are continuous, heterogeneous, and noisy, whereas decoder-only next-token models are usually built around discrete symbolic inputs. We introduce Vector-Input Autoregressive Inference for Ordinal-Return Modeling (VAIOM), a decoder-only Transformer for probabilistic next-return modeling on one-hour foreign-exchange bars. VAIOM separates input representation from output likelihood: continuous multivariate financial-event vectors preserve numerical structure at the input, while a categorical distribution over the next volatility-normalized return bucket supports cross-entropy training and likelihood evaluation. The selected 0.9M Hybrid Continuous Input model combines continuous event features with categorical asset metadata, a Mixture-of-Market-States return head, Gap, volatility-regime, and Ordinal auxiliary objectives, and full-sequence supervision. Models and preprocessing are fit using pre-2024 Train data; models are selected on 2024H2 Validation and evaluated without refitting on two 2025 Test periods. Across three independent training seeds, every model outperforms fixed single-bar LightGBM baseline in both Test halves. For the canonical checkpoint, paired gains over LightGBM are 0.029 and 0.043 bits per event. Validation experiments show that continuous input improves over discrete-token input under the same categorical return objective, full-sequence supervision improves over last-position training, and auxiliary representation shaping together with a mixture-structured return head improves return likelihood in controlled comparisons. A supporting capacity study finds that the smallest evaluated complete architecture rung achieves the strongest Validation likelihood on the present corpus.

</details>


### [271] [TRACE: Turn-level Reward Assignment via Credit Estimation for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13988)
*Leitian Tao,Baolin Peng,Wenlin Yao,Tao Ge,Hao Cheng,Mike Hang Wang,Jianfeng Gao,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向多轮智能体强化学习的密集信用分配方法TRACE，通过冻结参考模型的log-probability推导TD奖励信号，解决长轨迹下的奖励稀疏问题。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化均无任何关联。论文聚焦于大语言模型智能体的强化学习信用分配问题，属于NLP/Agent领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多轮工具调用智能体的强化学习后训练（post-training）；应用场景：复杂搜索任务（BrowseComp-Plus基准）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-30B-A3B大语言模型，使用冻结的参考模型（frozen reference model）提供gold-answer log-probabilities，通过log-ratio state values和TD误差推导每步奖励。不涉及视觉/遥感模型架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量化网络设计。其"无需额外critic或过程标签训练"的特点与轻量化无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。使用的Qwen3-30B-A3B是MoE架构，但论文未从部署角度分析。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：BrowseComp-Plus（封闭网页基准）、开放网页基准；评价指标：准确率（Qwen3-4B从7.2提升至35.6，Qwen3-30B-A3B从8.4提升至42.6）。

Experiments: 在BrowseComp-Plus上验证TRACE方法有效性，纯RL训练（无冷启动SFT、无agentic mid-training、无实时网页数据）显著提升工具使用能力，学习曲线显示更早提升和更快收敛。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。论文面向的是LLM Agent的文本推理和工具调用场景，不涉及视觉感知、遥感图像处理或边缘部署。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。虽然TRACE的密集奖励分配思想在强化学习领域有理论价值，但遥感在轨目标检测的轻量化问题涉及的是模型结构压缩和硬件部署，与Agent RL的信用分配问题属于不同研究范式。

Abstract: Multi-turn agents solve complex tasks through extended sequences of tool interactions before producing a final answer, making credit assignment a fundamental challenge during post-training. Outcome rewards provide reliable supervision for short-horizon reasoning, but become sparse and high-variance as trajectories grow to tens or hundreds of tool calls. They can also be misleading: a failed rollout may contain many useful actions that move the agent closer to the goal, yet outcome-only training assigns them the same negative advantage as the eventual mistake. We propose TRACE (Turn-level Reward Assignment via Credit Estimation), a dense credit-assignment method for agentic reinforcement learning. TRACE represents rollouts as state transitions at tool-call boundaries, obtains gold-answer log-probabilities from a frozen reference model, transforms them into log-ratio state values, and derives per-action rewards as Temporal-Difference changes in those values. This requires no additional critic or process-label training, and its one-step log-ratio TD component telescopes across redundant tool calls. On long-horizon complex search, TRACE substantially improves base-model tool-use ability using pure RL, without a cold-start supervised fine-tuning stage, an agentic mid-training stage, or training on live-web data. On the closed-web BrowseComp-Plus benchmark, it raises Qwen3-4B from $7.2$ to $35.6$ and Qwen3-30B-A3B from $8.4$ to $42.6$. The learned search behavior also transfers to open-web benchmarks, and the learning curves show earlier improvement and faster convergence during RL training.

</details>


### [272] [Lyapunov Exponent as Physics-Informed Dense Reward: RL Discovery of Stabilization Beyond the Kapitza Pendulum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14001)
